

=== src\App.tsx ===

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useSession } from "./app/session";
import MyBusiness from "./features/manager/MyBusiness";
import Pipeline from "./features/manager/Pipeline";
import AdminDashboard from "./features/admin/Dashboard";
import AdminDisputes from "./features/admin/Disputes";
import AdminAllRequestsJobs from "./features/admin/AllRequestsJobs";
import { NotificationBell } from "./components/NotificationBell";
import { FlowAIBubble } from "./components/FlowAIBubble";
import PolicyPage from "./features/manager/PolicyPage";
import { Footer } from "./components/layout/Footer";
import { ThemeToggle } from "./components/ThemeToggle";

import {
  BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Link, Navigate, useParams,
  useLocation, useNavigate,
} from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ActionButton, AppSidebar, EmptyState, Modal, roleNav, SelectField,
  Skeleton, StatusBadge, SurfaceCard, TextField,
} from "./components";
import { Notice, PageHeader } from "./components/client-shared";
import { ClientShell } from "./components/layout/ClientShell";
import { ClientGuard, SessionProvider, destination } from "./app/session";
import { DirectoryPage } from "./features/marketplace/DirectoryPage";
import { LandingPage } from "./features/marketplace/LandingPage";
import { CompanyPage } from "./features/marketplace/pages";
import { AuthPage } from "./features/auth/AuthPage";
import { ChangePasswordPage, WorkspacePage } from "./features/auth/pages";
import {
  EmailVerificationPage, PasswordResetPage, PendingVerificationPage, ProviderOnboardingPage,
} from "./features/auth/AccountFlows";
import {
  ClientDashboardPage, LegacyClientRoute, RequestEntryRoute,
} from "./features/client/ClientDashboardPage";
import { SharedExamples } from "./features/client/SharedExamples";

import RequestInbox from "./features/manager/Requests";
import QuoteBuilder from "./features/manager/QuoteBuilder";

import AdminCatalog from "./features/admin/Catalog";
import "./styles/client.css";
import ManagerDisputes from "./features/manager/Disputes";

type Role = "manager" | "employee" | "admin";

function Shell({ role, children }: { role: Role; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user } = useSession();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const items = roleNav[role];

  const activeHref = items.reduce<string>((current, item) => {
    const isExactMatch = location.pathname === item.href;
    const isNestedMatch = item.href !== "/" && location.pathname.startsWith(`${item.href}/`);
    if (isExactMatch || isNestedMatch) {
      return item.href.length > current.length ? item.href : current;
    }
    return current;
  }, items[0]?.href ?? "/");

  const subtitleMap: Record<Role, string> = {
    manager: "Manager · BrightFix",
    employee: "Employee · BrightFix",
    admin: "Admin · BrightFix",
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <AppSidebar
        items={items}
        activeHref={activeHref}
        userName={user?.name ?? "User"}
        subtitle={subtitleMap[role]}
        onNavigate={(href) => navigate(href)}
      />
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col min-w-0">
        <header className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 border-b border-line bg-paper px-6 py-2.5">
          <NotificationBell />
          <span className="text-sm text-text-secondary">{user?.name}</span>
          <ThemeToggle />
        </header>
        <main className="flex-1 p-6 bg-paper text-graphite">{children}</main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
      <FlowAIBubble />
    </div>
  );
}

function RoleGuard({ role, children }: { role: Role; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user } = useSession()
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  if (user.email_verified === false) return <Navigate to="/verify-email" replace />
  if (user.must_change_password) return <Navigate to="/change-password" replace />
  if (user.role !== role) return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />
  if (role === 'manager' && user.company_status !== 'APPROVED') return <Navigate to="/pending-verification" replace />
  return <>{children}</>
}

export default function App() {
  return <BrowserRouter>
    <SessionProvider>
      <Routes>
        <Route element={<ClientShell />}>
          <Route index element={<LandingPage />} />
          <Route path="services" element={<DirectoryPage />} />
          <Route path="companies" element={<DirectoryPage />} />
          <Route path="companies/:id" element={<CompanyRoute />} />
          <Route path="companies/:id/request" element={<ClientGuard><RequestEntryRoute /></ClientGuard>} />
          <Route path="login" element={<AuthPage />} />
          <Route path="register" element={<AuthPage register />} />
          <Route path="verify-email" element={<EmailVerificationPage />} />
          <Route path="pending-verification" element={<PendingVerificationPage />} />
          <Route path="account-status" element={<PendingVerificationPage />} />
          <Route path="password-reset" element={<PasswordResetPage />} />
          <Route path="provider-onboarding" element={<ProviderOnboardingPage />} />
          <Route path="change-password" element={<ChangePasswordPage />} />
          <Route path="workspace" element={<WorkspacePage />} />
          <Route path="style-guide" element={<StyleGuidePage />} />
          <Route path="client" element={<ClientGuard><ClientDashboardPage /></ClientGuard>} />
          {(['requests', 'quotations', 'jobs', 'invoices', 'profile'] as const).map(tab => (
            <Route key={tab} path={`client/${tab}`} element={<ClientGuard><LegacyClientRoute tab={tab} /></ClientGuard>} />
          ))}
          {(['requests', 'quotations', 'jobs', 'invoices'] as const).map(tab => (
            <Route key={tab} path={`client/${tab}/:id`} element={<ClientGuard><LegacyClientRoute tab={tab} /></ClientGuard>} />
          ))}
          <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
        </Route>

        <Route path="/manager" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><ManagerDashboard /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/requests" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><RequestInbox /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/pipeline" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><Pipeline /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/quotations/:requestId" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><QuoteBuilder /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/jobs" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><ManagerJobs /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/schedule" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><ManagerSchedule /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/invoices" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><Invoices /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/business" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><MyBusiness /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/team" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><ManagerTeam /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/ai" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><PolicyPage /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/company" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Navigate to="/manager/business" replace /></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/manager/disputes" element={<RoleGuard role="manager"><Shell role="manager"><ManagerDisputes /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />

        <Route path="/employee" element={<RoleGuard role="employee"><Navigate to="/employee/jobs" replace /></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/employee/jobs" element={<RoleGuard role="employee"><Shell role="employee"><EmployeeJobs /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/employee/jobs/:jobId" element={<RoleGuard role="employee"><Shell role="employee"><EmployeeJobDetail /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/employee/schedule" element={<RoleGuard role="employee"><Navigate to="/employee/jobs" replace /></RoleGuard>} />

        <Route path="/admin" element={<RoleGuard role="admin"><Shell role="admin"><AdminDashboard /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/admin/companies" element={<RoleGuard role="admin"><Shell role="admin"><AdminCompanies /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/admin/catalog" element={<RoleGuard role="admin"><Shell role="admin"><AdminCatalog /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/admin/requests" element={<RoleGuard role="admin"><Shell role="admin"><AdminAllRequestsJobs /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/admin/disputes" element={<RoleGuard role="admin"><Shell role="admin"><AdminDisputes /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
        <Route path="/admin/settings" element={<RoleGuard role="admin"><Shell role="admin"><AdminSettingsPlaceholder /></Shell></RoleGuard>} />
      </Routes>
    </SessionProvider>
  </BrowserRouter>
}

function AdminSettingsPlaceholder() {
  return <div>
    <PageHeader title="Settings" description="Platform settings." />
    <SurfaceCard className="max-w-2xl p-5">
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
        Platform-wide settings are not yet exposed by the backend.
        Use the API docs at <code>/docs</code> for administrative operations until this endpoint ships.
      </p>
    </SurfaceCard>
  </div>
}

function StyleGuidePage() {
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(false)
  return <><PageHeader eyebrow="SHARED UI · MEMBER 1" title="The ServiceFlow toolkit" description="One shared component library for public, client and operational screens. All examples below use sample content." /><div className="grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2"><SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6"><h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Actions</h2><div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4"><ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setModal(true)}>Open example dialog</ActionButton><ActionButton>Secondary</ActionButton><ActionButton variant="void">Destructive</ActionButton><ActionButton disabled>Unavailable</ActionButton></div><Notice>Use the yellow stamp for the primary action. Pair destructive actions with confirmation.</Notice></SurfaceCard><SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6"><h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Form fields</h2><TextField label="Service address" placeholder="Enter your address" /><TextField label="Email address" error="Enter a valid email address." defaultValue="sample" /><SelectField label="Service"><option>Basic inspection</option></SelectField><TextField label="Unavailable field" disabled value="Not editable" /></SurfaceCard><SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6"><h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Status vocabulary</h2><div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">{(['awaiting', 'approved', 'declined', 'scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'paid', 'unpaid', 'cancelled'] as const).map(status => <StatusBadge key={status} status={status} />)}</div><p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Use the backend state and a text label. A status color alone is insufficient.</p></SurfaceCard><SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6"><h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Feedback</h2><Skeleton rows={2} /><Notice error>Sample error: this appointment is no longer available.</Notice><EmptyState title="No requests yet" description="Your service requests will appear here." /></SurfaceCard></div><SharedExamples /><Modal open={modal} title="Example confirmation" description="This demonstrates the shared keyboard-accessible dialog." onClose={() => setModal(false)}><ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setModal(false)}>Close example</ActionButton></Modal></>
}
function CompanyRoute() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  return <CompanyPage key={id} />
}

function NotFoundPage() {
  return <><PageHeader title="We couldn't find that page" description="The link may have changed or the record may be unavailable." /><Link className="sf-link" to="/services">Browse services →</Link></>
}

=== src\vite-env.d.ts ===

/// <reference types="vite/client" />



=== src\app\session.tsx ===

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { clientService } from '../lib/client-service'
import { Navigate, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, Modal } from '../components'
import { setAccessToken } from '../lib/api'
import { demoAccount } from '../lib/demo-account'
import type { Session, User } from '../types/client'

type SessionContextValue = {
  user: User | null
  signIn: (session: Session) => void
  signOut: () => void
  requestLogout: () => void
}
const SessionContext = createContext<SessionContextValue>({
  user: null, signIn: () => {}, signOut: () => {}, requestLogout: () => {},
})

export function SessionProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  //bypass the login for demo purposes
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(() => {
    const bypass = import.meta.env.VITE_DEV_BYPASS_AUTH === 'true';
    if (!bypass) return null;
    return {
      id: 'dev-user',
      name: 'Dev User',
      email: 'dev@test.com',
      role: 'manager',
      email_verified: true,
      must_change_password: false,
      company_status: 'APPROVED',
    } as User;
  })

  const [logoutAccount, setLogoutAccount] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) return
    clientService.me().then(setUser).catch(() => {})
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [])

  function signOut() {
    setAccessToken(null)
    demoAccount.logout()
    setLogoutAccount(null)
    setUser(null)
  }
  function confirmLogout() {
    if (!user || logoutAccount !== user.id) return
    signOut()
    navigate('/', { replace: true })
  }

  return <SessionContext.Provider value={{
    user,
    signIn: session => {
      if (session.access_token) setAccessToken(session.access_token)
      setUser(session.user)
    },
    signOut,
    requestLogout: () => { if (user) setLogoutAccount(user.id) },
  }}>
    {children}
    <Modal
      open={!!user && logoutAccount === user.id}
      title="Log out of your account?"
      description={`Are you sure you want to log out of ${user?.name || 'your account'}? You will need to sign in again to access your workspace.`}
      onClose={() => setLogoutAccount(null)}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4">
        <ActionButton onClick={() => setLogoutAccount(null)}>Stay signed in</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="void" onClick={confirmLogout}>Yes, log out</ActionButton>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  </SessionContext.Provider>
}

export const useSession = () => useContext(SessionContext)

export function destination(user: User) {
  if (user.email_verified === false) return '/verify-email'
  if (user.must_change_password) return '/change-password'
  if (user.role === 'manager' && user.company_status !== 'APPROVED') return '/pending-verification'
  return user.role === 'client' ? '/client' :
         user.role === 'manager' ? '/manager' :
         user.role === 'employee' ? '/employee/jobs' :
         user.role === 'admin' ? '/admin' : '/workspace'
}

export function ClientGuard({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user } = useSession()
  const location = useLocation()
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" state={{ from: location.pathname + location.search }} replace />
  if (destination(user) !== '/client') return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />
  return <>{children}</>
}


=== src\components\client-shared.tsx ===

import { useEffect, useState, type DependencyList, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, EmptyState, Skeleton, StatusBadge, type WorkflowStatus } from './index'
export const money = (value: number) => `LKR ${value.toLocaleString('en-LK', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}`
export const dateTime = (value: string) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Colombo', dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' }).format(new Date(value))
export const today = () => {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Colombo', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(new Date())
  return ['year', 'month', 'day'].map(type => parts.find(part => part.type === type)!.value).join('-')
}
export function useResource<T>(loader: () => Promise<T>, deps: DependencyList = []) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T>(); const [error, setError] = useState(''); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const [revision, refresh] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => { let active = true; setLoading(true); setError(''); setData(undefined)
    loader().then(value => { if (active) setData(value) }).catch(reason => { if (active) setError(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Unable to load this page.') }).finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false) })
    return () => { active = false }
  }, [...deps, revision])
  return { data, error, loading, reload: () => refresh(n => n + 1) }
}
export function Resource({ loading, error, retry, children }: { loading: boolean; error: string; retry: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  if (loading) return <Skeleton rows={5} />
  if (error) return <div role="alert" className="sf-card p-6"><h2 className="sf-display font-semibold">We couldn’t load this</h2><p className="my-3">{error}</p><ActionButton onClick={retry}>Try again</ActionButton></div>
  return <>{children}</>
}
export function PageHeader({ eyebrow, title, description, children }: { eyebrow?: string; title: string; description?: string; children?: ReactNode }) {
  return <header className="mb-7 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-5"><div>{eyebrow && <p className="sf-mono mb-2 text-xs text-text-secondary">{eyebrow}</p>}<h1 className="sf-display text-3xl font-bold md:text-4xl">{title}</h1>{description && <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-text-secondary">{description}</p>}</div>{children}</header>
}
const statusMap: Record<string, WorkflowStatus> = {
  // Request statuses
  NEW: 'awaiting',
  UNDER_REVIEW: 'awaiting',
  NEED_INFORMATION: 'awaiting',
  QUOTATION_SENT: 'scheduled',
  // Quotation statuses
  DRAFT: 'awaiting',
  SENT: 'awaiting',
  APPROVED: 'approved',
  DECLINED: 'declined',
  EXPIRED: 'cancelled',
  // Request / Quotation legacy (client side)
  QUOTED: 'awaiting',
  CONVERTED: 'approved',
  // Job statuses
  JOB_CREATED: 'awaiting',
  SCHEDULE_PROPOSED: 'awaiting',
  SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED: 'scheduled',
  IN_PROGRESS: 'in_progress',
  COMPLETED: 'completed',
  // Schedule statuses
  NOT_PROPOSED: 'awaiting',
  PENDING: 'awaiting',
  CONFIRMED: 'approved',
  // Invoice statuses
  UNPAID: 'unpaid',
  PARTIALLY_PAID: 'awaiting',
  PAID: 'paid',
  // Shared
  REJECTED: 'declined',
  CANCELLED: 'cancelled',
}
const statusLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  // Request statuses
  NEW: 'New',
  UNDER_REVIEW: 'Under review',
  NEED_INFORMATION: 'Needs information',
  QUOTATION_SENT: 'Quotation sent',
  // Quotation statuses
  DRAFT: 'Draft',
  SENT: 'Sent',
  APPROVED: 'Approved',
  DECLINED: 'Declined',
  EXPIRED: 'Expired',
  // Request / Quotation legacy
  QUOTED: 'Quotation ready',
  CONVERTED: 'Job created',
  // Job statuses
  JOB_CREATED: 'Job created',
  SCHEDULE_PROPOSED: 'Appointment proposed',
  SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED: 'Appointment confirmed',
  IN_PROGRESS: 'Work in progress',
  COMPLETED: 'Completed',
  // Schedule statuses
  NOT_PROPOSED: 'Not scheduled',
  PENDING: 'Awaiting approval',
  CONFIRMED: 'Confirmed',
  // Invoice statuses

}
export const statusLabel = (status: string) => statusLabels[status] || status.toLowerCase().replaceAll('_', ' ')
export function RecordStatus({ status }: { status: string }) { return <StatusBadge status={statusMap[status] || 'awaiting'} label={statusLabel(status)} /> }
export function EmptyRecords({ title }: { title: string }) { return <EmptyState title={title} description="Your records will appear here as your service request progresses."><Link className="sf-link" to="/companies">Browse companies →</Link></EmptyState> }
export function Notice({ children, error = false }: { children: ReactNode; error?: boolean }) { return <p role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'} className={`rounded-docket border p-3 text-sm ${error ? 'border-red-300 bg-red-50 text-red-800' : 'border-border bg-paper'}`}>{children}</p> }


=== src\components\FlowAIBubble.tsx ===

import { useState, useRef, useEffect, type ChangeEvent } from 'react'
import { Sparkles, X, Send, Paperclip, FileText, Check, Lock } from 'lucide-react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useSession } from '../app/session'
import { api, apiUpload, ApiError } from '../lib/api'

type Message = {
  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system'
  content: string
  citations?: Array<{ source: string; page: number }>
  error?: boolean
  quickActions?: Array<{ label: string; action: 'business' | 'policy' | 'upload' }>
}

type PolicyType = 'SERVICE' | 'WARRANTY' | 'CANCELLATION' | 'PAYMENT' | 'TERMS_AND_CONDITIONS'

const GREETINGS = [
  'hi', 'hello', 'hey', 'helo', 'hiya', 'yo', 'sup',
  'good morning', 'good afternoon', 'good evening',
  'ayubowan', 'hello there', 'hi there', 'hey there',
]

const THANKS = ['thanks', 'thank you', 'thx', 'thnks', 'thank u']

const COMMON_QUESTIONS = [
  'What is the cancellation policy?',
  'What is the arrival window for appointments?',
  'What is the warranty on completed work?',
  'What payment methods are accepted?',
  'How do I request a refund?',
]

const POLICY_TYPES: { value: PolicyType; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'SERVICE', label: 'Service' },
  { value: 'WARRANTY', label: 'Warranty' },
  { value: 'CANCELLATION', label: 'Cancellation' },
  { value: 'PAYMENT', label: 'Payment' },
  { value: 'TERMS_AND_CONDITIONS', label: 'Terms & Conditions' },
]

function normalize(text: string): string {
  return text.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[!?.,]/g, '')
}

function isGreeting(text: string): boolean {
  const t = normalize(text)
  return GREETINGS.some(g => t === g || t.startsWith(g + ' '))
}

function isThanks(text: string): boolean {
  const t = normalize(text)
  return THANKS.some(g => t === g || t.startsWith(g + ' '))
}

export function FlowAIBubble() {
  const { user } = useSession()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const isProvider = user?.role === 'manager'

  if (user?.role === 'admin') return null

  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'data' | 'policy'>('data')
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([])
  const [input, setInput] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
  const [policyUploaded, setPolicyUploaded] = useState(false)
  const [showSuggestions, setShowSuggestions] = useState(false)
  const [showUploadDialog, setShowUploadDialog] = useState(false)
  const [pendingFile, setPendingFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [policyTitle, setPolicyTitle] = useState('')
  const [policyType, setPolicyType] = useState<PolicyType>('SERVICE')
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState<'PUBLIC' | 'PRIVATE'>('PRIVATE')
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ top: scrollRef.current.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth' })
  }, [messages, loading])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open && user && isProvider && messages.length === 0) {
      setMessages([{
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Hi ${user.name.split(' ')[0] || 'there'}! 👋 I'm Flow AI. I can help you with:\n\n• Business questions — requests, jobs, invoices, revenue\n• Policy questions — answers from your uploaded policy documents\n\nWhat would you like to do?`,
        quickActions: [
          { label: 'Ask about my business', action: 'business' },
          { label: 'Ask about my policy', action: 'policy' },
          { label: 'Upload a policy PDF', action: 'upload' },
        ],
      }])
    }
  }, [open, user, isProvider, messages.length])

  function handleBubbleClick() {
    if (!user) {
      navigate('/login', { state: { from: window.location.pathname } })
      return
    }
    setOpen(!open)
  }

  function handleSignIn() {
    navigate('/login', { state: { from: window.location.pathname } })
  }

  function handleQuickAction(action: 'business' | 'policy' | 'upload') {
    if (action === 'business') {
      setMode('data')
      setMessages(m => [...m, {
        role: 'assistant',
        content: 'Great! Ask me anything about your business — like "How many service requests have I received?" or "What is my unpaid invoice total?"',
      }])
    } else if (action === 'policy') {
      setMode('policy')
      if (policyUploaded) {
        setShowSuggestions(true)
        setMessages(m => [...m, {
          role: 'assistant',
          content: 'Sure! Here are some common policy questions you can try:',
        }])
      } else {
        setMessages(m => [...m, {
          role: 'assistant',
          content: 'You haven\'t uploaded a policy yet. Click the 📎 paperclip to upload a policy PDF first, then I can answer questions from it.',
          quickActions: [{ label: 'Upload policy PDF', action: 'upload' }],
        }])
      }
    } else if (action === 'upload') {
      fileRef.current?.click()
    }
  }

  async function send(text?: string) {
    if (!user) {
      handleSignIn()
      return
    }
    if (!isProvider) {
      setMessages(m => [...m, {
        role: 'assistant',
        content: 'Flow AI is only available to provider accounts. If you have questions about a company\'s policy, visit their company profile page.',
        error: true,
      }])
      return
    }
    const question = (text ?? input).trim()
    if (!question || loading) return
    setInput('')
    setShowSuggestions(false)
    setMessages(m => [...m, { role: 'user', content: question }])

    if (isGreeting(question)) {
      setMessages(m => [...m, {
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Hello! 👋 How can I help you today?\n\nI can answer questions about your business data or policy documents.`,
        quickActions: [
          { label: 'Ask about my business', action: 'business' },
          { label: 'Ask about my policy', action: 'policy' },
          { label: 'Upload a policy PDF', action: 'upload' },
        ],
      }])
      return
    }

    if (isThanks(question)) {
      setMessages(m => [...m, {
        role: 'assistant',
        content: 'You\'re welcome! 😊 Anything else I can help with?',
      }])
      return
    }

    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const endpoint = mode === 'policy' ? '/ai/ask-docs' : '/ai/ask-data'
      const response = await api<{
        status?: string
        answer?: string
        citations?: Array<{ source: string; page: number }>
      }>(endpoint, 'POST', { question }, { timeout: 120000 })

      const answer = response.status === 'insufficient_evidence'
        ? (response.answer || 'Not enough evidence to answer.')
        : (response.answer || 'No answer available.')

      setMessages(m => [...m, {
        role: 'assistant',
        content: answer,
        citations: response.citations,
      }])
    } catch (e) {
      let msg = 'Something went wrong.'
      if (e instanceof ApiError) {
        if (e.status === 502 || e.status === 503) msg = 'Flow AI is unavailable right now. Please try again.'
        else if (e.status === 403) msg = 'Flow AI is only available to provider accounts.'
        else if (e.status === 401) { msg = 'Your session expired. Please sign in again.'; handleSignIn() }
        else if (e.status === 0) msg = 'Cannot reach the server. Check backend is running.'
        else msg = e.message
      }
      setMessages(m => [...m, { role: 'assistant', content: msg, error: true }])
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  function pickFile(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    e.target.value = ''
    if (!file) return
    if (!user) { handleSignIn(); return }
    if (!isProvider) {
      setMessages(m => [...m, { role: 'assistant', content: 'Only provider accounts can upload policies.', error: true }])
      return
    }
    if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
      setMessages(m => [...m, { role: 'assistant', content: 'Only PDF files can be uploaded.', error: true }])
      return
    }
    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setMessages(m => [...m, { role: 'assistant', content: 'PDF must be 5 MB or smaller.', error: true }])
      return
    }
    setPendingFile(file)
    setPolicyTitle(file.name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, ''))
    setPolicyType('SERVICE')
    setVisibility('PRIVATE')
    setShowUploadDialog(true)
  }

  async function confirmUpload() {
    if (!pendingFile || !policyTitle.trim()) return
    setUploading(true)
    setShowUploadDialog(false)
    setMessages(m => [...m, { role: 'system', content: `Uploading ${pendingFile.name}…` }])
    try {
      const form = new FormData()
      form.append('file', pendingFile)
      form.append('title', policyTitle.trim())
      form.append('policy_type', policyType)
      form.append('visibility', visibility)
      await apiUpload('/policies/upload', form)
      setPolicyUploaded(true)
      setMode('policy')
      setShowSuggestions(true)
      setMessages(m => [
        ...m.filter(x => !(x.role === 'system' && x.content.startsWith('Uploading'))),
        { role: 'assistant', content: `✓ Policy "${policyTitle}" uploaded. I can now answer questions from it — try one of the suggestions below or ask your own.` },
      ])
    } catch (e) {
      let msg = 'Upload failed.'
      if (e instanceof ApiError) {
        if (e.status === 404) msg = 'Policy upload endpoint not found.'
        else if (e.status === 409) msg = 'An active policy of this type already exists. Deactivate it first or choose a different type.'
        else if (e.status === 403) msg = 'Only providers can upload policies.'
        else if (e.status === 422) msg = 'Please check the file and try again.'
        else msg = e.message
      }
      setMessages(m => [
        ...m.filter(x => !(x.role === 'system' && x.content.startsWith('Uploading'))),
        { role: 'assistant', content: msg, error: true },
      ])
    } finally {
      setUploading(false)
      setPendingFile(null)
      setPolicyTitle('')
    }
  }

  function cancelUpload() {
    setShowUploadDialog(false)
    setPendingFile(null)
    setPolicyTitle('')
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        onClick={handleBubbleClick}
        className="fixed bottom-20 md:bottom-6 right-4 md:right-6 z-40 h-14 w-14 rounded-full shadow-xl flex items-center justify-center hover:scale-105 transition-transform bg-signal text-ink"
        aria-label={open ? 'Close Flow AI' : 'Open Flow AI'}
        title={user ? (isProvider ? 'Flow AI' : 'Flow AI (provider only)') : 'Sign in to use Flow AI'}
      >
        {open ? <X size={22} /> : user && isProvider ? <Sparkles size={22} /> : <Lock size={22} />}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="fixed z-40 bottom-36 md:bottom-24 right-4 left-4 md:left-auto md:right-6 md:w-96 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex flex-col h-[32rem] max-h-[calc(100vh-10rem)] bg-paper border border-line text-graphite">
          <div className="p-3 border-b border-line">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <Sparkles className="text-signal" size={18} />
              <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-graphite">Flow AI</h3>
              {policyUploaded && (
                <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#DCFCE7] text-[#166534]">
                  <Check size={11} /> Policy loaded
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            {user && (
              <p className="text-[11px] mb-2 text-text-secondary">
                Signed in as <strong className="text-graphite">{user.name}</strong> ({user.role})
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              <button
                onClick={() => setMode('data')}
                className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded font-medium ${
                  mode === 'data'
                    ? 'bg-signal text-ink'
                    : 'bg-paper text-text-secondary border border-line'
                }`}
              >
                Business
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setMode('policy')}
                className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded font-medium ${
                  mode === 'policy'
                    ? 'bg-signal text-ink'
                    : 'bg-paper text-text-secondary border border-line'
                }`}
              >
                Policy
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div ref={scrollRef} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-3">
            {!user && (
              <div className="text-center py-12 px-4">
                <Lock className="mx-auto mb-3 text-signal" size={32} />
                <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2 text-graphite">Sign in required</h4>
                <p className="text-xs mb-4 text-text-secondary">
                  Flow AI is available only to signed-in users. Please sign in to continue.
                </p>
                <button
                  onClick={handleSignIn}
                  className="inline-block px-5 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-signal text-ink"
                >
                  Sign in
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {user && !isProvider && messages.length === 0 && (
              <div className="text-center py-12 px-4">
                <Lock className="mx-auto mb-3 text-signal" size={32} />
                <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2 text-graphite">Provider-only feature</h4>
                <p className="text-xs mb-2 text-text-secondary">
                  Flow AI is available to provider accounts only.
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
                  For policy questions, visit the company profile page.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {messages.map((m, i) => {
              if (m.role === 'system') {
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="text-xs text-center py-1 italic text-text-secondary">
                    {m.content}
                  </div>
                )
              }
              const isUser = m.role === 'user'
              return (
                <div key={i} className={`flex ${isUser ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
                  <div
                    className={`max-w-[85%] p-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm ${
                      isUser
                        ? 'bg-graphite text-white'
                        : m.error
                          ? 'border border-red-500 bg-red-500/10 text-red-600'
                          : 'bg-paper border border-line text-graphite'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{m.content}</p>
                    {m.citations && m.citations.length > 0 && (
                      <div className={`mt-2 space-y-1 pt-2 ${isUser ? 'border-t border-white/20' : 'border-t border-line'}`}>
                        {m.citations.map((c, j) => (
                          <p key={j} className="text-[11px] opacity-85">
                            📄 {c.source} · p.{c.page}
                          </p>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {m.quickActions && m.quickActions.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                        {m.quickActions.map((qa, j) => (
                          <button
                            key={j}
                            onClick={() => handleQuickAction(qa.action)}
                            className="text-left text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium border border-signal text-signal bg-paper hover:bg-signal hover:text-ink transition"
                          >
                            → {qa.label}
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}

            {loading && (
              <div className="flex justify-start">
                <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm bg-paper border border-line text-text-secondary">
                  Thinking…
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {showSuggestions && isProvider && (
              <div className="pt-2">
                <p className="text-[11px] mb-2 font-semibold text-text-secondary">Try asking:</p>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  {COMMON_QUESTIONS.map(q => (
                    <button
                      key={q}
                      onClick={() => send(q)}
                      className="text-left text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-paper border border-line text-graphite hover:bg-signal hover:text-ink transition"
                      disabled={loading}
                    >
                      {q}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {showUploadDialog && pendingFile && (
            <div className="p-3 space-y-2 border-t border-line bg-paper">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-graphite">Upload policy: {pendingFile.name}</p>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] mb-1 text-text-secondary">Title</label>
                <input
                  value={policyTitle}
                  onChange={e => setPolicyTitle(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none sf-input"
                  maxLength={255}
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] mb-1 text-text-secondary">Policy type</label>
                <select
                  value={policyType}
                  onChange={e => setPolicyType(e.target.value as PolicyType)}
                  className="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none sf-input"
                >
                  {POLICY_TYPES.map(t => (
                    <option key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] mb-1 text-text-secondary">Visibility</label>
                <select
                  value={visibility}
                  onChange={e => setVisibility(e.target.value as 'PUBLIC' | 'PRIVATE')}
                  className="w-full px-2 py-1.5 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none sf-input"
                >
                  <option value="PRIVATE">Private (only you)</option>
                  <option value="PUBLIC">Public (visible to clients)</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                <button
                  onClick={cancelUpload}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm border border-line bg-paper text-graphite"
                  disabled={uploading}
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={confirmUpload}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-semibold bg-signal text-ink disabled:opacity-40"
                  disabled={uploading || !policyTitle.trim()}
                >
                  {uploading ? 'Uploading…' : 'Upload'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!user ? (
            <div className="p-3 border-t border-line text-center">
              <button
                onClick={handleSignIn}
                className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-signal text-ink"
              >
                Sign in to chat
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : !isProvider ? (
            <div className="p-3 border-t border-line text-center text-xs text-text-secondary">
              Flow AI is provider-only
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="p-2 border-t border-line flex gap-2 items-center">
              <input
                ref={fileRef}
                type="file"
                accept="application/pdf"
                className="hidden"
                onChange={pickFile}
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={uploading || loading}
                className="p-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-40 text-signal hover:bg-line/20"
                aria-label="Upload PDF"
                title="Upload policy PDF"
              >
                <Paperclip size={16} />
              </button>
              <input
                value={input}
                onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={e => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
                    e.preventDefault()
                    send()
                  }
                }}
                placeholder={mode === 'policy' ? 'Ask about policy…' : 'Ask about business…'}
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none sf-input"
                disabled={loading || uploading}
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => send()}
                disabled={loading || uploading || !input.trim()}
                className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center disabled:opacity-40 bg-signal text-ink"
                aria-label="Send"
              >
                <Send size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState, type ButtonHTMLAttributes, type HTMLAttributes, type InputHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode, type SelectHTMLAttributes, type TextareaHTMLAttributes } from 'react'
import {
  BadgeCheck, Ban, Banknote, BriefcaseBusiness, Building2, CalendarCheck2, CalendarDays,
  CheckCheck, ChevronDown, CircleCheck, CircleX, ClipboardList, Clock3, Download, Ellipsis,
  Eye, FileSearch, FileText, Grid2X2, LayoutDashboard, ListFilter, MapPin, Menu, MessageCircleQuestion,
  Pencil, Play, Plus, Receipt, Search, Settings, ShieldCheck, Sparkles, TriangleAlert, Upload, UserRound,
  UserRoundCheck, Users, X, type LucideIcon
} from 'lucide-react'

const cx = (...classes: Array<string | false | undefined>) => classes.filter(Boolean).join(' ')
const iconMap = { dashboard: LayoutDashboard, browse: Search, categories: Grid2X2, requests: ClipboardList,
  quotations: FileText, jobs: BriefcaseBusiness, schedule: CalendarDays, invoices: Receipt,
  ai: Sparkles, company: Building2, team: Users, settings: Settings } satisfies Record<string, LucideIcon>
export type NavIcon = keyof typeof iconMap
export { Tabs } from './Tabs'

export type ActionVariant = 'stamp' | 'stub' | 'void' | 'ghost' | 'icon'
export function ActionButton({ variant = 'stub', size = 'default', icon: Icon, children, className, title, 'aria-label': ariaLabel, ...props }:
  ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: ActionVariant; size?: 'default' | 'small'; icon?: LucideIcon }) {
  if (variant === 'icon' && !ariaLabel) throw new Error('Icon-only ActionButton requires aria-label')
  return <button type="button" {...props} aria-label={ariaLabel} title={title ?? (variant === 'icon' ? ariaLabel : undefined)}
    className={cx('sf-action inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-docket font-semibold',
      variant === 'icon' ? 'h-10 w-10 sf-stub' : size === 'small' ? 'min-h-9 px-3 text-sm' : 'min-h-11 px-5 text-sm',
      variant !== 'icon' && ('sf-' + variant), className)}>
    {Icon && <Icon size={variant === 'icon' ? 18 : 17} strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden="true"/>}{children}
  </button>
}

export type WorkflowStatus = 'awaiting' | 'approved' | 'declined' | 'scheduled' | 'in_progress' | 'completed' | 'paid' | 'unpaid' | 'cancelled'
const statuses: Record<WorkflowStatus, { label: string; icon: LucideIcon; color: string; bg: string; solid?: boolean }> = {
  awaiting: { label:'Awaiting response',icon:Clock3,color:'var(--sf-warning)',bg:'var(--sf-warning-bg)' },
  approved: { label:'Approved',icon:CircleCheck,color:'var(--sf-success)',bg:'var(--sf-success-bg)' },
  declined: { label:'Declined',icon:CircleX,color:'var(--sf-error)',bg:'var(--sf-error-bg)' },
  scheduled: { label:'Scheduled',icon:CalendarCheck2,color:'var(--sf-info)',bg:'var(--sf-info-bg)' },
  in_progress: { label:'In progress',icon:Play,color:'var(--sf-info)',bg:'var(--sf-info-bg)' },
  completed: { label:'Completed',icon:CheckCheck,color:'var(--sf-success)',bg:'var(--sf-success-bg)' },
  paid: { label:'Paid',icon:BadgeCheck,color:'#fff',bg:'var(--sf-success)',solid:true },
  unpaid: { label:'Unpaid',icon:TriangleAlert,color:'var(--sf-warning)',bg:'var(--sf-warning-bg)' },
  cancelled: { label:'Cancelled',icon:Ban,color:'#fff',bg:'var(--sf-cancelled)',solid:true }
}
export function StatusBadge({ status, label }: { status: WorkflowStatus; label?: string }) {
  const config = statuses[status]; const Icon = config.icon
  return <span className="sf-tag" style={{ color:config.color,background:config.bg,...(config.solid ? { borderColor:config.bg } : {}) }}>
    <Icon size={13} strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden="true"/>{label ?? config.label}
  </span>
}

type FieldBase = { label:string; hint?:string; error?:string; required?:boolean; id?:string }
export function TextField({ label, hint, error, id, required, className, ...props }: FieldBase & InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>) {
  const generated = useId(), fieldId = id ?? generated, messageId = fieldId + '-message'
  return <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <label htmlFor={fieldId} className="text-[13.5px] font-medium">{label}{required && <span className="ml-1 text-red-700" aria-hidden="true">*</span>}</label>
    <input {...props} id={fieldId} required={required} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error || hint ? messageId : undefined} className={cx('sf-input',className)}/>
    {(error || hint) && <p id={messageId} className={cx('text-[13px]',error ? 'text-[#B93832]' : 'text-text-secondary')}>{error && <TriangleAlert className="mr-1 inline" size={13} aria-hidden="true"/>}{error ?? hint}</p>}
  </div>
}
export function SelectField({ label, hint, error, id, required, children, className, ...props }: FieldBase & SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement>) {
  const generated = useId(), fieldId = id ?? generated, messageId = fieldId + '-message'
  return <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <label htmlFor={fieldId} className="text-[13.5px] font-medium">{label}{required && <span className="ml-1 text-red-700" aria-hidden="true">*</span>}</label>
    <select {...props} id={fieldId} required={required} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error || hint ? messageId : undefined} className={cx('sf-input',className)}>{children}</select>
    {(error || hint) && <p id={messageId} className={cx('text-[13px]',error ? 'text-[#B93832]' : 'text-text-secondary')}>{error ?? hint}</p>}
  </div>
}
export function TextAreaField({ label, hint, error, id, required, className, ...props }: FieldBase & TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement>) {
  const generated = useId(), fieldId = id ?? generated, messageId = fieldId + '-message'
  return <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5"><label htmlFor={fieldId} className="text-[13.5px] font-medium">{label}{required && <span className="ml-1 text-red-700" aria-hidden="true">*</span>}</label>
    <textarea {...props} id={fieldId} required={required} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error || hint ? messageId : undefined} className={cx('sf-input min-h-24',className)}/>
    {(error || hint) && <p id={messageId} className={cx('text-[13px]',error ? 'text-[#B93832]' : 'text-text-secondary')}>{error ?? hint}</p>}
  </div>
}
export const DateField = (props: Omit<React.ComponentProps<typeof TextField>,'type'>) => <TextField {...props} type="date"/>
export const TimeField = (props: Omit<React.ComponentProps<typeof TextField>,'type'>) => <TextField {...props} type="time"/>

export function SurfaceCard({ children, className, ...props }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
  return <div {...props} className={cx('sf-card',className)}>{children}</div>
}
export function DetailRow({ icon: Icon, label, children }: { icon:LucideIcon;label:string;children:ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 border-b border-dashed border-line py-2.5 last:border-0">
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[13.5px] text-text-secondary"><Icon size={16} aria-hidden="true"/>{label}</span>
    <span className="sf-tabular text-right text-sm font-semibold">{children}</span>
  </div>
}

export function AppSidebar({ items, activeHref, userName, subtitle, onNavigate }: {
  items:NavItem[];activeHref:string;userName:string;subtitle:string;onNavigate?:(href:string)=>void
}) {
  const [open,setOpen] = useState(false)
  return <aside className="sf-dark bg-graphite text-white md:w-[248px] md:shrink-0">
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-5"><div className="sf-display flex items-center gap-2.5 font-bold"><span className="sf-mono grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-docket bg-signal text-xs text-ink">SF</span>ServiceFlow</div>
      <button type="button" className="rounded-docket p-2 md:hidden" aria-label={open?'Close navigation':'Open navigation'} aria-expanded={open} aria-controls="sf-sidebar-nav" onClick={()=>setOpen(!open)}>{open?<X size={20}/>:<Menu size={20}/>}</button></div>
    <nav id="sf-sidebar-nav" aria-label="App navigation" className={cx('px-3 pb-5 md:block',!open && 'hidden')}>
      {items.map(item=>{ const Icon=iconMap[item.icon],active=item.href===activeHref
        return <a key={item.href} href={item.href} aria-current={active?'page':undefined} onClick={e=>{if(onNavigate){e.preventDefault();onNavigate(item.href)}setOpen(false)}}
          className={cx('mb-1 flex min-h-10 items-center gap-3 rounded-docket px-3 text-sm font-medium no-underline hover:bg-graphite-2 hover:text-white',active?'bg-signal font-semibold text-ink hover:bg-signal hover:text-ink':'text-[#C9CDD2]')}>
          <Icon size={19} strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden="true"/>{item.label}{item.count != null && <span className={cx('sf-mono ml-auto rounded-full px-2 text-[11px]',active?'bg-ink text-signal':'bg-[#B93832] text-white')}>{item.count}</span>}
        </a>})}
      <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3 border-t border-dashed border-white/20 px-2 pt-4"><div className="sf-mono grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-full bg-graphite-2 text-xs text-signal">{userName.split(' ').map(x=>x[0]).join('').slice(0,2)}</div><div><strong className="block text-sm">{userName}</strong><span className="text-xs text-[#A8ADB3]">{subtitle}</span></div></div>
    </nav>
  </aside>
}

export type Column<T> = {key:string;heading:string;render:(item:T)=>ReactNode}
export function ResponsiveTable<T extends {id:string}>({ columns, rows, caption, empty }: {columns:Column<T>[];rows:T[];caption:string;empty?:ReactNode}) {
  if (!rows.length) return <EmptyState title="No records yet" description="New records will appear here.">{empty}</EmptyState>
  return <><div className="hidden overflow-x-auto sf-card md:block"><table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm"><caption className="sr-only">{caption}</caption><thead className="bg-paper">{<tr>{columns.map(col=><th scope="col" key={col.key} className="sf-mono px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{col.heading}</th>)}</tr>}</thead>
    <tbody>{rows.map(row=><tr key={row.id} className="border-t border-line">{columns.map(col=><td key={col.key} className="px-4 py-3 align-top">{col.render(row)}</td>)}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
    <div className="space-y-3 md:hidden" role="list" aria-label={caption}>{rows.map(row=><SurfaceCard key={row.id} role="listitem" className="space-y-3 p-4">{columns.map(col=><div key={col.key} className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="sf-mono text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{col.heading}</span><span className="text-right text-sm">{col.render(row)}</span></div>)}</SurfaceCard>)}</div></>
}
export function Modal({ open, title, description, children, onClose }: {open:boolean;title:string;description?:string;children:ReactNode;onClose:()=>void}) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const closeRef = useRef(onClose)
  closeRef.current = onClose
  useEffect(()=>{ if (!open) return
    const previous=document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null
    const focusable=()=>Array.from(dialogRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button:not([disabled]),[href],input:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),select:not([disabled]),[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])') ?? [])
    focusable()[0]?.focus()
    const handler=(event:KeyboardEvent)=>{if(event.key==='Escape')closeRef.current()
      if(event.key==='Tab'){const controls=focusable();if(!controls.length)return
        const first=controls[0],last=controls[controls.length-1]
        if(event.shiftKey && document.activeElement===first){event.preventDefault();last.focus()}
        else if(!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first.focus()}
      }}
    document.addEventListener('keydown',handler);return()=>{document.removeEventListener('keydown',handler);previous?.focus()}
  },[open])
  const headingId = useId()
  if(!open)return null
  return <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 grid place-items-center bg-[#22262B]/45 p-4" onMouseDown={e=>{if(e.target===e.currentTarget)onClose()}}>
    <div ref={dialogRef} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby={headingId} className="sf-card max-h-[90dvh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4"><h2 id={headingId} className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">{title}</h2><ActionButton variant="icon" icon={X} aria-label="Close dialog" onClick={onClose}/></div>
      {description&&<p className="mt-2 text-sm text-text-secondary">{description}</p>}<div className="mt-5">{children}</div>
    </div>
  </div>
}
export function EmptyState({ title, description, children }: {title:string;description:string;children?:ReactNode}) {
  return <div className="rounded-docket border-2 border-dashed border-border bg-white px-6 py-10 text-center"><ClipboardList className="mx-auto mb-3 text-graphite" size={32} aria-hidden="true"/><h3 className="sf-mono text-xs font-semibold">{title}</h3><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-text-secondary">{description}</p>{children&&<div className="mt-5">{children}</div>}</div>
}
export function Skeleton({ rows=3 }: {rows?:number}) {
  return <div role="status" aria-label="Loading" className="sf-card space-y-3 p-5"><span className="sr-only">Loading</span>{Array.from({length:rows},(_,i)=><div key={i} aria-hidden="true" className="sf-skeleton h-4 rounded-docket" style={{width:i%2?'70%':'95%'}}/>)}</div>
}
export function FlowAIPanel({ question, answer, source, page, citations, loading=false }: {
  question:string;
  answer?:string;
  source?:string;
  page?:number;
  citations?: Array<{ source: string; page: number }>;
  loading?:boolean;
}) {
  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-xl p-5">
    <h3 className="sf-display flex items-center gap-2 font-bold">
      <Sparkles className="text-[#6E5600]" size={18} aria-hidden="true"/>Flow AI
    </h3>
    <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2 rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
      <MessageCircleQuestion className="shrink-0" size={17} aria-hidden="true"/>{question}
    </div>
    {loading ? (
      <div className="mt-4"><Skeleton rows={2}/></div>
    ) : (
      <>
        <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2 text-sm">
          <Sparkles className="shrink-0 text-[#6E5600]" size={17} aria-hidden="true"/>
          <p>{answer ?? 'The uploaded policy document does not provide enough evidence to answer this question.'}</p>
        </div>
        {citations && citations.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {citations.map((c, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="sf-mono inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-docket border border-border px-2 py-1 text-[11px] text-text-secondary">
                <FileSearch size={15} aria-hidden="true"/>Source: {c.source} · Page {c.page}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        {(!citations || citations.length === 0) && answer && source && page != null && (
          <div className="sf-mono mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-docket border border-border px-2 py-1 text-[11px] text-text-secondary">
            <FileSearch size={15} aria-hidden="true"/>Source: {source} · Page {page}
          </div>
        )}
      </>
    )}
  </SurfaceCard>
}

=== src\components\NotificationBell.tsx ===

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { Bell } from 'lucide-react'
import { ActionButton } from './index'
import { Notice, dateTime } from './client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../lib/provider-service'
import type { NotificationRow } from '../types/provider'

export function NotificationBell() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(0)
  const [items, setItems] = useState<NotificationRow[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [unreadOnly, setUnreadOnly] = useState(false)
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    async function tick() {
      try {
        const r = await providerService.unreadNotificationCount()
        if (alive) setUnread(r.unread_count)
      } catch { /* silent */ }
    }
    tick()
    const id = window.setInterval(tick, 30000)
    return () => { alive = false; window.clearInterval(id) }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    void load()
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [open, unreadOnly])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    function onClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (!wrapperRef.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false)
    }
    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false)
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onClick)
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onClick)
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    }
  }, [open])

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true); setError('')
    try {
      const r = await providerService.listNotifications({ unread_only: unreadOnly, limit: 20 })
      setItems(r.items)
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  async function markRead(id: number) {
    try {
      await providerService.markNotificationRead(id)
      setItems(prev => prev.map(n => n.id === id ? { ...n, is_read: true } : n))
      setUnread(u => Math.max(0, u - 1))
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) }
  }

  async function markAll() {
    try {
      await providerService.markAllNotificationsRead()
      setItems(prev => prev.map(n => ({ ...n, is_read: true })))
      setUnread(0)
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) }
  }

  return <div ref={wrapperRef} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setOpen(v => !v)}
      aria-label={`Notifications${unread > 0 ? `, ${unread} unread` : ''}`}
      aria-expanded={open}
      style={{
        position: 'relative', width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 8,
        border: '1px solid #d8d8d8', background: 'white', cursor: 'pointer',
        display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center',
      }}
    >
      <Bell size={18} />
      {unread > 0 && <span style={{
        position: 'absolute', top: -6, right: -6, minWidth: 18, height: 18,
        padding: '0 5px', borderRadius: 999, background: '#B93832', color: 'white',
        fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center',
      }}>{unread > 99 ? '99+' : unread}</span>}
    </button>

    {open && <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-label="Notifications"
      style={{
        position: 'absolute', right: 0, top: 48, width: 360, maxWidth: '90vw',
        background: 'white', border: '1px solid #d8d8d8', borderRadius: 10,
        boxShadow: '0 12px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.12)', zIndex: 60, overflow: 'hidden',
      }}
    >
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: '10px 14px', borderBottom: '1px solid #eaeaea',
      }}>
        <strong style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>Notifications</strong>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setUnreadOnly(v => !v)}
            style={{
              fontSize: 12, padding: '4px 8px', borderRadius: 6,
              border: '1px solid #d8d8d8', background: unreadOnly ? '#22262B' : 'white',
              color: unreadOnly ? 'white' : '#22262B', cursor: 'pointer',
            }}
          >{unreadOnly ? 'Unread only' : 'All'}</button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={markAll}
            disabled={unread === 0}
            style={{
              fontSize: 12, padding: '4px 8px', borderRadius: 6,
              border: '1px solid #d8d8d8', background: 'white',
              cursor: unread === 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
              opacity: unread === 0 ? 0.5 : 1,
            }}
          >Mark all read</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ maxHeight: 400, overflowY: 'auto' }}>
        {error && <div style={{ padding: 12 }}><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
        {loading && <p style={{ padding: 14, fontSize: 13, color: '#666' }}>Loading…</p>}
        {!loading && !error && items.length === 0 && (
          <p style={{ padding: 14, fontSize: 13, color: '#666' }}>
            {unreadOnly ? 'No unread notifications.' : 'No notifications yet.'}
          </p>
        )}
        {!loading && items.map(n => (
          <button
            key={n.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { if (!n.is_read) void markRead(n.id) }}
            style={{
              display: 'block', width: '100%', textAlign: 'left',
              padding: '10px 14px', borderBottom: '1px solid #f0f0f0',
              background: n.is_read ? 'white' : '#f7f9fb',
              cursor: n.is_read ? 'default' : 'pointer', border: 0,
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8 }}>
              <strong style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{n.title}</strong>
              {!n.is_read && <span style={{
                width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: '50%',
                background: '#3b82f6', marginTop: 5, flexShrink: 0,
              }} />}
            </div>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#444', margin: '4px 0 2px' }}>{n.message}</p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#888', margin: 0 }}>
              {n.type} · {dateTime(n.created_at)}
            </p>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>}
  </div>
}


=== src\components\Tabs.tsx ===

import { useEffect, useId, useRef, type KeyboardEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react'

type Props<T extends string> = {
  label: string
  items: readonly { key: T; label: string }[]
  value: T
  onChange: (value: T) => void
  children: ReactNode
}

export function Tabs<T extends string>({ label, items, value, onChange, children }: Props<T>) {
  const id = useId()
  const list = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    const selected = list.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-selected="true"]')
    if (!selected || !list.current) return
    const bounds = list.current.getBoundingClientRect()
    const tab = selected.getBoundingClientRect()
    if (tab.left < bounds.left) list.current.scrollLeft -= bounds.left - tab.left
    else if (tab.right > bounds.right) list.current.scrollLeft += tab.right - bounds.right
  }, [value])
  function navigate(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, index: number) {
    const offsets: Record<string, number> = { ArrowRight: 1, ArrowLeft: -1, Home: -index, End: items.length - 1 - index }
    if (!(event.key in offsets)) return
    event.preventDefault()
    const next = (index + offsets[event.key] + items.length) % items.length
    event.currentTarget.parentElement?.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="tab"]')[next]?.focus()
    onChange(items[next].key)
  }
  return <>
    <div ref={list} role="tablist" aria-label={label} className="sf-dashboard-tabs">
      {items.map((item, index) => <button
        key={item.key}
        type="button"
        role="tab"
        id={`${id}-${item.key}`}
        aria-selected={value === item.key}
        aria-controls={`${id}-panel`}
        tabIndex={value === item.key ? 0 : -1}
        onKeyDown={event => navigate(event, index)}
        onClick={() => onChange(item.key)}
      >{item.label}</button>)}
    </div>
    <div role="tabpanel" id={`${id}-panel`} aria-labelledby={`${id}-${value}`} tabIndex={0}>
      {children}
    </div>
  </>
}



=== src\components\ThemeToggle.tsx ===

import { Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-react'
import { useTheme } from '../lib/theme'

export function ThemeToggle() {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme()
  const dark = theme === 'dark'
  return <button type="button" className="sf-theme-toggle" onClick={toggleTheme} aria-label={dark ? 'Switch to light theme' : 'Switch to dark theme'} aria-pressed={dark}>
    {dark ? <Sun size={18} aria-hidden="true" /> : <Moon size={18} aria-hidden="true" />}
  </button>
}
