=== src\features\admin\AllRequestsJobs.tsx ===

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  EmptyState, SelectField, SurfaceCard, Tabs,
} from '../../components'
import { PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, dateTime, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { JobRow, RequestRow } from '../../types/provider'

const tabs = [
  { key: 'requests', label: 'Requests' },
  { key: 'jobs', label: 'Jobs' },
] as const
type Tab = typeof tabs[number]['key']

const REQUEST_STATUS = [
  { value: '', label: 'All statuses' },
  { value: 'NEW', label: 'New' },
  { value: 'UNDER_REVIEW', label: 'Under review' },
  { value: 'NEED_INFORMATION', label: 'Needs information' },
  { value: 'QUOTATION_SENT', label: 'Quotation sent' },
  { value: 'REJECTED', label: 'Rejected' },
  { value: 'CANCELLED', label: 'Cancelled' },
]

const JOB_STATUS = [
  { value: '', label: 'All statuses' },
  { value: 'JOB_CREATED', label: 'Job created' },
  { value: 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED', label: 'Schedule proposed' },
  { value: 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED', label: 'Schedule confirmed' },
  { value: 'IN_PROGRESS', label: 'In progress' },
  { value: 'COMPLETED', label: 'Completed' },
  { value: 'CANCELLED', label: 'Cancelled' },
]

export default function AllRequestsJobs() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('requests')
  return <>
    <PageHeader title="All requests & jobs" description="Platform-wide view of every request and job.">
      <Link to="/admin/disputes" className="sf-link">Review disputes →</Link>
    </PageHeader>
    <Tabs label="Platform data" items={tabs} value={tab} onChange={setTab}>
      {tab === 'requests' && <RequestsView />}
      {tab === 'jobs' && <JobsView />}
    </Tabs>
  </>
}

function RequestsView() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('')
  const data = useResource(
    () => providerService.listIncomingRequests(status || undefined),
    [status],
  )
  return <>
    <div className="mb-4 max-w-xs">
      <SelectField label="Status" value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
        {REQUEST_STATUS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
    </div>
    <Resource {...data} retry={data.reload}>
      {data.data && data.data.items.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No requests" description="No requests match these filters." />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{data.data?.items.map(r => (
            <RequestCard key={r.id} r={r} />
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function JobsView() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('')
  const data = useResource(
    () => providerService.listJobs({ status: status || undefined }),
    [status],
  )
  return <>
    <div className="mb-4 max-w-xs">
      <SelectField label="Status" value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
        {JOB_STATUS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
    </div>
    <Resource {...data} retry={data.reload}>
      {data.data && data.data.items.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No jobs" description="No jobs match these filters." />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{data.data?.items.map(j => (
            <JobCard key={j.id} j={j} />
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function RequestCard({ r }: { r: RequestRow }) {
  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-2 p-4">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
      <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Request #{r.id}</span>
      <RecordStatus status={r.status} />
    </div>
    <p className="text-sm">{r.description}</p>
    <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
      Provider #{r.provider_id} · Client #{r.client_id} · {r.address}
    </p>
    <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
      Preferred {r.preferred_date}
      {r.preferred_time ? ` ${r.preferred_time.slice(0, 5)}` : ''}
      {' · Created '}{dateTime(r.created_at)}
    </p>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

function JobCard({ j }: { j: JobRow }) {
  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-2 p-4">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
      <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Job #{j.id} · Request #{j.request_id}</span>
      <RecordStatus status={j.status} />
    </div>
    <p className="text-sm">
      {j.schedule_date
        ? `Scheduled ${j.schedule_date}${j.schedule_time ? ` ${j.schedule_time.slice(0, 5)}` : ''}`
        : 'Not scheduled'}
      {' · '}<span className="text-text-secondary">{j.schedule_status}</span>
    </p>
    <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
      Provider #{j.provider_id}
      {j.assigned_user_id ? ` · Employee #${j.assigned_user_id}` : ' · Unassigned'}
      {' · Created '}{dateTime(j.created_at)}
    </p>
    {j.completed_at && <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Completed {dateTime(j.completed_at)}</p>}
    {j.cancellation_reason && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--sf-error)]">Cancelled: {j.cancellation_reason}</p>}
  </SurfaceCard>
}


=== src\features\admin\Catalog.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { CatalogService } from '../../types/provider'

export default function AdminCatalog() {
  const services = useResource(() => providerService.adminListServices())
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<CatalogService | null>(null)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')

  const list = services.data ?? []
  const activeCount = list.filter(s => s.is_active).length
  const inactiveCount = list.filter(s => !s.is_active).length

  async function deactivate(id: number) {
    setBusy(id); setError(''); setSuccess('')
    try {
      await providerService.adminDeactivateService(id)
      setSuccess(`Service #${id} deactivated.`)
      services.reload()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setBusy(null) }
  }

  async function reactivate(id: number) {
    setBusy(id); setError(''); setSuccess('')
    try {
      await providerService.adminUpdateService(id, { is_active: true })
      setSuccess(`Service #${id} reactivated.`)
      services.reload()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setBusy(null) }
  }

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Service catalog" description="Master list every provider picks from when offering services.">
      <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>+ Add service</ActionButton>
    </PageHeader>

    <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
      <SurfaceCard className="p-4">
        <p className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">ACTIVE</p>
        <p className="sf-tabular mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold">{activeCount}</p>
      </SurfaceCard>
      <SurfaceCard className="p-4">
        <p className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">INACTIVE</p>
        <p className="sf-tabular mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold">{inactiveCount}</p>
      </SurfaceCard>
    </div>

    {error && <div className="mb-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
    {success && <div className="mb-3"><Notice>{success}</Notice></div>}

    <Resource {...services} retry={services.reload}>
      {list.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No services yet" description="Add the first service to the catalog." />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{list.map(s => (
            <SurfaceCard key={s.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="sf-display font-semibold">{s.name}</h3>
                  <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${s.is_active
                    ? 'bg-[var(--sf-success-bg)] text-[var(--sf-success)]'
                    : 'bg-paper text-text-secondary'}`}>
                    {s.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                  </span>
                  {s.category && (
                    <span className="rounded bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">{s.category}</span>
                  )}
                </div>
                {s.description && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">{s.description}</p>}
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setEditing(s)}>Edit</ActionButton>
                {s.is_active
                  ? <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => deactivate(s.id)} disabled={busy === s.id}>
                      {busy === s.id ? '…' : 'Deactivate'}
                    </ActionButton>
                  : <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => reactivate(s.id)} disabled={busy === s.id}>
                      {busy === s.id ? '…' : 'Reactivate'}
                    </ActionButton>}
              </div>
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>

    <Modal open={adding} title="Add service" onClose={() => setAdding(false)}>
      {adding && <ServiceForm
        onDone={() => { setAdding(false); services.reload() }}
        onCancel={() => setAdding(false)}
      />}
    </Modal>

    <Modal open={!!editing} title={`Edit ${editing?.name ?? ''}`} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
      {editing && <ServiceForm
        existing={editing}
        onDone={() => { setEditing(null); services.reload() }}
        onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
      />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function ServiceForm({ existing, onDone, onCancel }: {
  existing?: CatalogService
  onDone: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(existing?.name ?? '')
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(existing?.category ?? '')
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(existing?.description ?? '')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      if (existing) {
        await providerService.adminUpdateService(existing.id, {
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description.trim() || undefined,
          category: category.trim(),
        })
      } else {
        await providerService.adminCreateService({
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description.trim() || undefined,
          category: category.trim(),
        })
      }
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <TextField label="Service name" value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} required maxLength={150} />
    <TextField label="Category" value={category} onChange={e => setCategory(e.target.value)} required maxLength={100} placeholder="e.g. Electrical, Plumbing" />
    <TextAreaField label="Description (optional)" value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !name.trim() || !category.trim()}>
        {pending ? 'Saving…' : existing ? 'Save changes' : 'Add service'}
      </ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}


=== src\features\admin\Companies.tsx ===

import { useState } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, SelectField, SurfaceCard,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, dateTime, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { AdminProviderRow } from '../../types/provider'

const VERIFICATION_FILTERS = [
  { value: '', label: 'All providers' },
  { value: 'PENDING', label: 'Pending approval' },
  { value: 'APPROVED', label: 'Approved' },
  { value: 'REJECTED', label: 'Rejected' },
  { value: 'SUSPENDED', label: 'Suspended' },
]

const TYPE_FILTERS = [
  { value: '', label: 'All types' },
  { value: 'INDIVIDUAL', label: 'Individual' },
  { value: 'COMPANY', label: 'Company' },
]

export default function AdminCompanies() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('PENDING')
  const [type, setType] = useState('')
  const providers = useResource(
    () => providerService.adminListProviders({
      verification_status: status || undefined,
      provider_type: type || undefined,
    }),
    [status, type],
  )
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')

  async function act(id: number, action: 'approve' | 'reject' | 'suspend') {
    setBusy(id); setError(''); setSuccess('')
    try {
      const fn =
        action === 'approve' ? providerService.adminApproveProvider :
        action === 'reject' ? providerService.adminRejectProvider :
        providerService.adminSuspendProvider
      const res = await fn(id)
      setSuccess(res.message || `Provider #${id} ${action}d.`)
      providers.reload()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setBusy(null) }
  }

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Verification queue" description="Approve, reject or suspend service providers." />

    <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
      <SelectField label="Status" value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
        {VERIFICATION_FILTERS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
      <SelectField label="Provider type" value={type} onChange={e => setType(e.target.value)}>
        {TYPE_FILTERS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
    </div>

    {error && <div className="mb-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
    {success && <div className="mb-3"><Notice>{success}</Notice></div>}

    <Resource {...providers} retry={providers.reload}>
      {providers.data && providers.data.items.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState
            title="No providers"
            description={status === 'PENDING' ? 'No providers awaiting verification.' : 'No providers match these filters.'}
          />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{providers.data?.items.map(p => (
            <ProviderCard key={p.id} provider={p} busy={busy === p.id} onAction={act} />
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function ProviderCard({ provider, busy, onAction }: {
  provider: AdminProviderRow
  busy: boolean
  onAction: (id: number, action: 'approve' | 'reject' | 'suspend') => void
}) {
  const s = provider.verification_status
  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-3 p-4">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <h3 className="sf-display font-semibold">{provider.name}</h3>
          <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${statusClass(s)}`}>{s}</span>
          <span className="rounded bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">{provider.type}</span>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">{provider.description ?? 'No description'}</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 text-sm sm:grid-cols-3">
      <div><p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Email</p><p>{provider.email}</p></div>
      <div><p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Phone</p><p>{provider.phone}</p></div>
      <div><p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Address</p><p>{provider.address ?? '—'}</p></div>
    </div>

    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-line pt-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Registered {dateTime(provider.created_at)}</p>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {s !== 'APPROVED' && (
          <ActionButton variant="stamp" size="small" onClick={() => onAction(provider.id, 'approve')} disabled={busy}>
            Approve
          </ActionButton>
        )}
        {s !== 'REJECTED' && (
          <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => onAction(provider.id, 'reject')} disabled={busy}>
            Reject
          </ActionButton>
        )}
        {s === 'APPROVED' && (
          <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => onAction(provider.id, 'suspend')} disabled={busy}>
            Suspend
          </ActionButton>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

function statusClass(s: string): string {
  if (s === 'APPROVED') return 'bg-[var(--sf-success-bg)] text-[var(--sf-success)]'
  if (s === 'PENDING') return 'bg-[var(--sf-warning-bg)] text-[var(--sf-warning)]'
  if (s === 'REJECTED') return 'bg-[var(--sf-error-bg)] text-[var(--sf-error)]'
  return 'bg-paper text-text-secondary'
}


=== src\features\admin\Dashboard.tsx ===

import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, SurfaceCard } from '../../components'
import { Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const data = useResource(async () => {
    const [providers, services, disputes] = await Promise.all([
      providerService.adminListProviders().catch(() => ({ items: [], total: 0, limit: 0, offset: 0 })),
      providerService.adminListServices().catch(() => []),
      providerService.adminListDisputes().catch(() => []),
    ])
    return { providers, services, disputes }
  })

  return <>
    <div className="mb-6">
      <h1 className="sf-display text-2xl font-bold">Admin dashboard</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">Platform overview.</p>
    </div>

    <Resource {...data} retry={data.reload}>
      {data.data && <AdminContent
        providerTotal={data.data.providers.total}
        pendingProviders={data.data.providers.items.filter(p => p.verification_status === 'PENDING').length}
        services={data.data.services}
        disputes={data.data.disputes}
        onNavigate={navigate}
      />}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function AdminContent({ providerTotal, pendingProviders, services, disputes, onNavigate }: {
  providerTotal: number
  pendingProviders: number
  services: { is_active: boolean }[]
  disputes: { status: string }[]
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void
}) {
  const activeServices = services.filter(s => s.is_active).length
  const openDisputes = disputes.filter(d => d.status === 'OPEN' || d.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW').length
  const resolvedDisputes = disputes.filter(d => d.status === 'RESOLVED' || d.status === 'CLOSED').length

  return <>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <Tile label="PROVIDERS" value={String(providerTotal)} detail={`${pendingProviders} pending`} onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/companies')} warning={pendingProviders > 0} />
      <Tile label="ACTIVE SERVICES" value={String(activeServices)} detail={`${services.length} total`} onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/catalog')} />
      <Tile label="OPEN DISPUTES" value={String(openDisputes)} detail={`${resolvedDisputes} resolved`} onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/disputes')} warning={openDisputes > 0} />
      <Tile label="TOTAL DISPUTES" value={String(disputes.length)} detail="All time" onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/disputes')} />
    </div>

    <SurfaceCard className="mt-6 p-5">
      <h2 className="mb-3 font-semibold">Quick actions</h2>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/companies')}>Review providers</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/disputes')}>Open disputes</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={() => onNavigate('/admin/catalog')}>Service catalog</ActionButton>
      </div>
    </SurfaceCard>

    <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 p-5">
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
        Platform-wide user management, reports and settings are not yet exposed by the backend.
        Use the API docs at <code>/docs</code> for those operations until they ship.
      </p>
    </SurfaceCard>
  </>
}

function Tile({ label, value, detail, onClick, warning = false }: {
  label: string
  value: string
  detail?: string
  onClick: () => void
  warning?: boolean
}) {
  return <button type="button" onClick={onClick}
    className="sf-card w-full p-5 text-left transition hover:border-graphite">
    <p className="sf-mono text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{label}</p>
    <p className={`sf-tabular mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold ${warning ? 'text-[var(--sf-warning)]' : ''}`}>{value}</p>
    {detail && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-secondary">{detail}</p>}
  </button>
}


=== src\features\admin\Disputes.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SelectField, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, dateTime, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { DisputeRow } from '../../types/provider'

const STATUS_FILTERS = [
  { value: '', label: 'All disputes' },
  { value: 'OPEN', label: 'Open' },
  { value: 'UNDER_REVIEW', label: 'Under review' },
  { value: 'RESOLVED', label: 'Resolved' },
  { value: 'CLOSED', label: 'Closed' },
]

export default function AdminDisputes() {
  const disputes = useResource(() => providerService.adminListDisputes())
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<DisputeRow | null>(null)

  const list = (disputes.data ?? []).filter(d => !filter || d.status === filter)

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Disputes" description="Review and resolve disputes between clients and providers." />

    <div className="mb-4 max-w-xs">
      <SelectField label="Filter by status" value={filter} onChange={e => setFilter(e.target.value)}>
        {STATUS_FILTERS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
    </div>

    <Resource {...disputes} retry={disputes.reload}>
      {list.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No disputes" description={filter ? 'No disputes with this status.' : 'No disputes have been raised.'} />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{list.map(d => (
            <SurfaceCard key={d.id} className="space-y-3 p-4">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Dispute #{d.id} · Job #{d.job_id}</span>
                  <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${statusClass(d.status)}`}>{d.status.replaceAll('_', ' ')}</span>
                </div>
                {!['RESOLVED', 'CLOSED'].includes(d.status) && (
                  <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setEditing(d)}>Manage</ActionButton>
                )}
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{d.reason}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap">{d.description}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 text-xs text-text-secondary sm:grid-cols-3">
                <div>Opened by user #{d.opened_by}</div>
                <div>Created {dateTime(d.created_at)}</div>
                {d.closed_at && <div>Closed {dateTime(d.closed_at)}</div>}
              </div>
              {d.admin_note && (
                <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
                  <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Admin note</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">{d.admin_note}</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>

    <Modal open={!!editing} title={`Dispute #${editing?.id ?? ''}`} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
      {editing && <UpdateDispute
        dispute={editing}
        onDone={() => { setEditing(null); disputes.reload() }}
        onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
      />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function UpdateDispute({ dispute, onDone, onCancel }: {
  dispute: DisputeRow
  onDone: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<DisputeRow['status']>(dispute.status)
  const [adminNote, setAdminNote] = useState(dispute.admin_note ?? '')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.adminUpdateDispute(dispute.id, {
        status,
        admin_note: adminNote.trim() || null,
      })
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
      <p className="font-semibold">{dispute.reason}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">{dispute.description}</p>
    </div>
    <SelectField label="New status" value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value as DisputeRow['status'])} required>
      <option value="OPEN">Open</option>
      <option value="UNDER_REVIEW">Under review</option>
      <option value="RESOLVED">Resolved</option>
      <option value="CLOSED">Closed</option>
    </SelectField>
    <TextAreaField label="Admin note (optional)" value={adminNote} onChange={e => setAdminNote(e.target.value)} />
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Update dispute'}</ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}

function statusClass(s: string): string {
  if (s === 'OPEN') return 'bg-[var(--sf-warning-bg)] text-[var(--sf-warning)]'
  if (s === 'UNDER_REVIEW') return 'bg-[var(--sf-info-bg)] text-[var(--sf-info)]'
  if (s === 'RESOLVED') return 'bg-[var(--sf-success-bg)] text-[var(--sf-success)]'
  return 'bg-paper text-text-secondary'
}


=== src\features\auth\AccountFlows.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link, Navigate, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, SurfaceCard, TextField } from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { destination, useSession } from '../../app/session'
import { demoMode, liveApiMode } from '../../lib/api'
import { demoAccount, requireDemo } from '../../lib/demo-account'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'

export function EmailVerificationPage() {
  const { user, signIn } = useSession()
  const [search, setSearch] = useSearchParams()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [token, setToken] = useState('')
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const useLive = liveApiMode || !demoMode
  const linkToken = search.get('token') || new URLSearchParams(window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, '')).get('token') || ''
  const hasToken = !!linkToken
  if (user && user.email_verified) return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />
  if (!user && !hasToken) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />

  async function perform(verify: boolean) {
    if (pending) return
    setPending(true)
    setError('')
    setMessage('')
    try {
      if (useLive) {
        if (verify) {
          await clientService.verifyEmail(linkToken)
          setSearch({})
          window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/verify-email')
          setMessage('Your email has been verified. You can now sign in.')
        } else {
          const address = user ? user.email : email.trim()
          if (!address) throw new Error('Enter the email address you registered with.')
          await clientService.resendVerification(address)
          setMessage('If the account is eligible, a verification email will be sent. Check your inbox.')
        }
      } else {
        requireDemo('Email verification')
        if (verify) {
          const next = demoAccount.redeem(linkToken, 'verify')
          signIn({ access_token: '', user: next })
          navigate(destination(next), { replace: true })
        } else {
          setToken(demoAccount.issueLink(demoAccount.profile().email, 'verify') || '')
        }
      }
    } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl">
    <PageHeader title="Verify your email" description="Confirm your email address before opening your workspace." />
    <SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6">
      <Notice>{useLive ? 'Verification links expire in 30 minutes and can be used once. Verifying activates a new client account.' : 'Demo verification uses a single-use sample link valid for 15 minutes. No email is sent.'}</Notice>
      {!hasToken && useLive && !user && <TextField label="Email address" type="email" required autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={event => setEmail(event.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com" />}
      <ActionButton variant="stamp" disabled={pending} onClick={() => void perform(hasToken)}>
        {pending ? 'Please wait…' : hasToken ? 'Confirm email verification' : useLive ? 'Resend verification email' : 'Create sample verification link'}
      </ActionButton>
      {token && <Link className="sf-link block" to={`/verify-email?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`}>Open sample verification link →</Link>}
      {message && <Notice>{message} <Link className="sf-link" to="/login">Sign in</Link></Notice>}
      {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    </SurfaceCard>
  </div>
}

export function PasswordResetPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useSearchParams()
  const { signOut } = useSession()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('')
  const [sampleToken, setSampleToken] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const useLive = liveApiMode || !demoMode
  const token = search.get('token') || new URLSearchParams(window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, '')).get('token') || ''
  const reset = token !== ''

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending) return
    setError('')
    if (reset && password !== confirm) { setError('Passwords must match.'); return }
    setPending(true)
    try {
      if (useLive) {
        if (reset) {
          await clientService.resetPassword(token, password)
          signOut()
          setSearch({})
          window.history.replaceState(null, '', '/password-reset')
          setSampleToken('')
          setPassword('')
          setConfirm('')
          setMessage('Your password has been changed. You can now sign in.')
        } else {
          await clientService.forgotPassword(email.trim())
          setMessage('If an account exists for this email, a password reset email has been sent. Check your inbox.')
        }
      } else {
        requireDemo('Password recovery')
        if (reset) {
          demoAccount.redeem(token, 'reset', password)
          signOut()
          setSearch({})
          setSampleToken('')
          setPassword('')
          setConfirm('')
          setMessage('Your sample password has been changed. You can now sign in.')
        } else {
          setSampleToken(demoAccount.issueLink(email.trim(), 'reset') || '')
          setMessage('If a sample account exists, a demo reset link is available below. No email is sent.')
        }
      }
    } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl">
    <PageHeader title={reset ? 'Choose a new password' : 'Reset your password'} description="Recover access to your ServiceFlow account." />
    <SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6">
      <Notice>{useLive ? 'A reset link will be emailed to you. Links expire in 30 minutes and can be used once.' : 'Sample recovery links expire in 15 minutes and can be used once. All sample accounts reset on refresh.'}</Notice>
      <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
        {reset ? <>
          <TextField label="New password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" minLength={8} required value={password} onChange={event => setPassword(event.target.value)} />
          <TextField label="Confirm password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={confirm} onChange={event => setConfirm(event.target.value)} />
        </> : <TextField label="Email address" type="email" autoComplete="email" required value={email} onChange={event => setEmail(event.target.value)} />}
        {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
        {message && <Notice>{message}</Notice>}
        <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Please wait…' : reset ? 'Save new password' : 'Request reset link'}</ActionButton>
      </form>
      {sampleToken && <Link className="sf-link block" to={`/password-reset?token=${sampleToken}`}>Open sample password reset link →</Link>}
      <Link className="sf-link block" to="/login">Return to sign in →</Link>
    </SurfaceCard>
  </div>
}

export function PendingVerificationPage() {
  const { user, signIn } = useSession()
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  if (user.role !== 'manager') return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />
  const rejected = user.company_status === 'REJECTED'
  async function refresh() {
    setPending(true)
    setError('')
    try { signIn({ access_token: '', user: await clientService.me() }) }
    catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
    <PageHeader title={rejected ? 'Your application needs attention' : 'Your company is awaiting approval'} description="An administrator reviews company applications before they become public." />
    <SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6">
      <p>Application status <strong>{user.company_status || 'PENDING'}</strong></p>
      {demoMode && <Notice>This is a sample application. Approval can only be performed by an administrator in the connected system.</Notice>}
      {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
      <ActionButton disabled={pending} onClick={() => void refresh()}>{pending ? 'Checking…' : 'Check status'}</ActionButton>
      <Link className="sf-link block" to="/provider-onboarding">View onboarding checklist →</Link>
      {user.company_status === 'APPROVED' && <Link className="sf-link block" to="/workspace">Open workspace →</Link>}
    </SurfaceCard>
  </div>
}

export function ProviderOnboardingPage() {
  const { user } = useSession()
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  if (user.role !== 'manager') return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />
  const steps = [
    { title: 'Register company and manager', complete: true },
    { title: 'Verify your email address', complete: user.email_verified === true },
    { title: 'Receive administrator approval', complete: user.company_status === 'APPROVED' },
  ]
  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
    <PageHeader title="Get your company ready" description="Track your account setup, then complete operational setup in the manager workspace." />
    <SurfaceCard className="space-y-5 p-6">
      <p className="sf-mono text-sm">{steps.filter(step => step.complete).length} / {steps.length} account steps complete</p>
      <ol className="space-y-4">{steps.map(step => <li key={step.title} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 border-b border-line pb-4">
        <span>{step.title}</span><strong>{step.complete ? 'Complete' : 'Pending'}</strong>
      </li>)}</ol>
      <Notice>After approval, complete your company details, active services, working hours, employees and public policy in the manager workspace.</Notice>
      <Link className="sf-link block" to={user.email_verified === false ? '/verify-email' : '/pending-verification'}>Continue account setup →</Link>
      {user.company_status === 'APPROVED' && <Link className="sf-link block" to="/workspace">Continue to manager workspace →</Link>}
    </SurfaceCard>
  </div>
}



=== src\features\auth\AuthPage.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link, Navigate, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ArrowRight, Check, Eye, EyeOff, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { ActionButton, SelectField, TextField } from '../../components'
import { Notice } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { destination, useSession } from '../../app/session'
import { ApiError, demoMode } from '../../lib/api'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'

export function AuthPage({ register = false }: { register?: boolean }) {
  const [kind, setKind] = useState('client')
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [company, setCompany] = useState('')
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')
  const [fields, setFields] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const { signIn, user } = useSession()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const location = useLocation()
  if (user) return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError(''); setFields({})
    try {
      const session = register
        ? await clientService.register(kind, { name: name.trim(), email: email.trim(), phone: phone.trim(), password, ...(kind === 'company' ? { company_name: company.trim() } : {}) })
        : await clientService.login(email.trim(), password)
      if (session) {
        signIn(session)
        const from = location.state?.from
        const safeReturn = session.user.role === 'client' && session.user.email_verified !== false && !session.user.must_change_password && typeof from === 'string' && /^\/(?!\/)/.test(from) && !from.includes('\\')
        navigate(safeReturn ? from : destination(session.user), { replace: true })
      } else setSuccess('Registration submitted. Your account may need activation before you can sign in.')
    } catch (cause) {
      setError((cause as Error).message)
      if (cause instanceof ApiError) setFields(cause.fieldErrors)
    } finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <div className="sf-auth-layout">
    <aside className="sf-auth-story"><div><p className="sf-eyebrow">A CLEARER WAY TO GET THINGS DONE</p><h2>A little help.<br />A lot less hassle.</h2><p>Your home services, organised from the first request to the final receipt.</p><ul>{['Find a company that fits your needs', 'Approve the price before work begins', 'Keep every appointment and invoice together'].map(text => <li key={text}><Check size={17} />{text}</li>)}</ul></div><div className="sf-auth-story-footnote"><ShieldCheck size={18} /><span>Your decisions. Your details. Your workspace.</span></div></aside>
    <section className="sf-auth-form"><p className="sf-eyebrow">YOUR SERVICEFLOW ACCOUNT</p><h1>{register ? 'Let’s get started' : 'Welcome back'}</h1><p className="sf-auth-description">{register ? 'Create a client account or register your service company.' : 'Sign in to manage your requests and see what’s next.'}</p>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>{register && <><SelectField label="Account type" value={kind} onChange={event => setKind(event.target.value)}><option value="client">Client — request services</option><option value="company">Company — provide services</option></SelectField><TextField label={kind === 'company' ? 'Manager name' : 'Full name'} required autoComplete="name" value={name} onChange={event => setName(event.target.value)} error={fields.name} />{kind === 'company' && <TextField label="Company name" required value={company} onChange={event => setCompany(event.target.value)} error={fields.company_name} />}<TextField label="Phone number" type="tel" required autoComplete="tel" minLength={7} maxLength={30} value={phone} onChange={event => setPhone(event.target.value)} error={fields.phone} placeholder="e.g. 077 123 4567" /></>}
        <TextField label="Email address" type="email" required autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={event => setEmail(event.target.value)} error={fields.email} placeholder="you@example.com" />
        <div className="sf-password-field"><TextField label="Password" type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'} required minLength={register ? 8 : 1} maxLength={128} autoComplete={register ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'} value={password} onChange={event => setPassword(event.target.value)} error={fields.password} hint={register ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : undefined} /><button type="button" aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'} aria-pressed={showPassword} onClick={() => setShowPassword(value => !value)}>{showPassword ? <EyeOff size={17} /> : <Eye size={17} />}</button></div>
        {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}{success && <Notice>{success} <Link className="sf-link" to="/login">Sign in</Link></Notice>}
        <ActionButton className="w-full" type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !!success}>{pending ? 'Please wait…' : register ? 'Create account' : 'Sign in'}<ArrowRight size={16} /></ActionButton>
      </form>
      {!register && <Link className="sf-link mt-5 inline-block" to="/password-reset">Forgot your password?</Link>}
      <p className="sf-auth-toggle">{register ? 'Already have an account?' : 'New to ServiceFlow?'} <Link to={register ? '/login' : '/register'} state={location.state}>{register ? 'Sign in' : 'Create an account'}</Link></p>
      {demoMode && <div className="sf-demo-login"><strong>Explore before you get started.</strong><p>{register ? 'Demo registration creates a sample session only.' : 'Sample client: nimal@example.com · DemoClient123!'}</p><p>Sample data resets on refresh. No real accounts or bookings.</p>{!register && <button type="button" disabled={pending} onClick={() => { setEmail('nimal@example.com'); setPassword('DemoClient123!') }}>Fill sample account<ArrowRight size={14} /></button>}</div>}
    </section>
  </div>
}

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link, Navigate, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, SurfaceCard, TextField } from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { destination, useSession } from '../../app/session'
import { demoMode } from '../../lib/api'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'
export function AccountStatusPage() {
  const { user, signIn } = useSession(); const [error, setError] = useState(''); const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  if (user.role !== 'manager') return <Navigate to={destination(user)} replace />
  async function refresh() { setPending(true); setError(''); try { if (!demoMode) { const current = await clientService.me(); signIn({ access_token: '', user: current }) } } catch (e) { setError((e as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) } }
  return <><PageHeader title={user.company_status === 'REJECTED' ? 'Application needs attention' : 'Your company is awaiting approval'} description={user.company_status === 'REJECTED' ? 'Review the application with your team. Profile editing and resubmission belong to the manager workspace.' : 'An administrator will review your company before it can appear in the directory.'} /><SurfaceCard className="max-w-2xl space-y-4 p-6"><p>Current status: <strong>{user.company_status || 'PENDING'}</strong></p><ActionButton disabled={pending} onClick={refresh}>{pending ? 'Checking…' : 'Check status'}</ActionButton>{error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}{user.company_status === 'APPROVED' && <Link className="sf-link block" to="/workspace">Open workspace →</Link>}{demoMode && <Notice>This is a sample application. No real company has been submitted.</Notice>}</SurfaceCard></>
}
export function ChangePasswordPage() {
  const { user, signIn } = useSession(); const navigate = useNavigate(); const [password, setPassword] = useState(''); const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(''); const [pending, setPending] = useState(false); const [error, setError] = useState('')
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  async function submit(event: FormEvent) { event.preventDefault(); if (pending) return; if (password !== confirm) { setError('Passwords must match.'); return } setPending(true); setError(''); try { await clientService.changePassword(password); const next = demoMode ? { ...user!, must_change_password: false } : await clientService.me(); signIn({ access_token: '', user: next }); navigate(destination(next)) } catch (e) { setError((e as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) } }
  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg"><PageHeader title="Set your own password" description="Change your temporary password before accessing your workspace." /><SurfaceCard className="p-6"><form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}><TextField label="New password" type="password" required minLength={8} autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} /><TextField label="Confirm new password" type="password" required autoComplete="new-password" value={confirm} onChange={e => setConfirm(e.target.value)} />{error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}<ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save password'}</ActionButton></form></SurfaceCard></div>
}

=== src\features\client\ClientDashboardPage.tsx ===

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Navigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, Modal, SelectField, Tabs } from '../../components'
import { Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'
import { DashboardPage } from './DashboardPage'
import { InvoiceDetailPage, JobDetailPage, QuoteDetailPage, RecordsPage, RequestDetailPage } from './pages'
import { ProfileSettings } from './ProfileSettings'
import { NewRequestPage } from '../marketplace/RequestForm'
import { ClientFlowAI } from '../marketplace/ClientFlowAI'

const tabs = [
  { key: 'overview', label: 'Overview' },
  { key: 'requests', label: 'Requests' },
  { key: 'quotations', label: 'Quotations' },
  { key: 'jobs', label: 'Jobs' },
  { key: 'invoices', label: 'Invoices' },
  { key: 'profile', label: 'Profile / Settings' },
] as const
type Tab = typeof tabs[number]['key']

export function LegacyClientRoute({ tab }: { tab: Tab }) {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const query = new URLSearchParams({ tab })
  if (id) query.set('record', id)
  return <Navigate to={`/client?${query}`} replace />
}

export function RequestEntryRoute() {
  const { id = '' } = useParams()
  const [search] = useSearchParams()
  const query = new URLSearchParams({ tab: 'requests', action: 'new', company: id })
  if (search.get('service')) query.set('service', search.get('service')!)
  return <Navigate to={`/client?${query}`} replace />
}

export function ClientDashboardPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useSearchParams()
  const tab: Tab = tabs.find(value => value.key === search.get('tab'))?.key || 'overview'
  const record = search.get('record') || ''
  const creating = tab === 'requests' && search.get('action') === 'new'
  const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0)

  function selectTab(next: Tab) { setSearch(next === 'overview' ? {} : { tab: next }) }
  function closeRequest() { setSearch({ tab: 'requests' }) }
  function created(id: string) {
    setRevision(current => current + 1)
    setSearch({ tab: 'requests', record: id })
  }
  return <>
    <Tabs label="Client dashboard sections" items={tabs} value={tab} onChange={selectTab}>
    <section key={`${tab}-${revision}`} aria-label={`${tabs.find(value => value.key === tab)?.label} content`}>
      {tab === 'overview' && <DashboardPage />}
      {tab === 'profile' && <ProfileSettings />}
      {tab === 'requests' && <div className="mb-5"><ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setSearch({ tab: 'requests', action: 'new' })}>Create new request</ActionButton></div>}
      {tab === 'requests' && (record ? <RequestDetailPage key={record} recordId={record} /> : <RecordsPage kind="requests" />)}
      {tab === 'quotations' && (record ? <QuoteDetailPage key={record} recordId={record} /> : <RecordsPage kind="quotations" />)}
      {tab === 'jobs' && (record ? <JobDetailPage key={record} recordId={record} /> : <RecordsPage kind="jobs" />)}
      {tab === 'invoices' && (record ? <InvoiceDetailPage key={record} recordId={record} /> : <RecordsPage kind="invoices" />)}
    </section>
    </Tabs>
    <details className="sf-card mt-8 p-5">
      <summary className="cursor-pointer font-semibold">Client Flow AI · ask about a company policy</summary>
      <div className="mt-5"><CompanyAI /></div>
    </details>
    <Modal open={creating} title="Create a service request" onClose={closeRequest}>
      {creating && <CreateRequest companyId={search.get('company') || ''} onCreated={created} onCancel={closeRequest} />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function CreateRequest({ companyId, onCreated, onCancel }: { companyId: string; onCreated: (id: string) => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  const resource = useResource(() => clientService.companies())
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(companyId)
  return <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <SelectField label="Provider company" value={selected} onChange={event => setSelected(event.target.value)}>
        <option value="">Choose a company</option>
        {resource.data?.map(company => <option key={company.id} value={company.id}>{company.name} · {company.location}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
      {selected && <NewRequestPage key={selected} companyId={selected} onCreated={onCreated} onCancel={onCancel} />}
    </div>
  </Resource>
}

function CompanyAI() {
  const resource = useResource(() => clientService.companies())
  const [company, setCompany] = useState('')
  return <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
    <div className="max-w-xl space-y-5">
      <SelectField label="Policy company" value={company} onChange={event => setCompany(event.target.value)}>
        <option value="">Choose a company</option>
        {resource.data?.map(value => <option key={value.id} value={value.id}>{value.name}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
      {company && <ClientFlowAI key={company} companyId={company} />}
    </div>
  </Resource>
}



=== src\features\client\DashboardPage.tsx ===

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight, CalendarDays, Check, ClipboardList, FileText, Plus, Receipt } from 'lucide-react'
import { RecordStatus, Resource, dateTime, money, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { useSession } from '../../app/session'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'

function toArray<T>(value: unknown): T[] {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value as T[]
  if (value && typeof value === 'object' && Array.isArray((value as any).items)) {
    return (value as any).items as T[]
  }
  return []
}

export function DashboardPage() {
  const { user } = useSession()
  const resource = useResource(async () => {
    const [requests, quotes, jobs, invoices] = await Promise.all([
      clientService.requests(),
      clientService.quotes(),
      clientService.jobs(),
      clientService.invoices(),
    ])
    return {
      requests: toArray<any>(requests),
      quotes: toArray<any>(quotes),
      jobs: toArray<any>(jobs),
      invoices: toArray<any>(invoices),
    }
  })
  const data = resource.data
  const pendingQuotes = data ? data.quotes.filter((quote: any) => quote.status === 'SENT') : []
  const pendingJobs = data ? data.jobs.filter((job: any) => job.status === 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED') : []
  const date = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Colombo', weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' }).format(new Date())

  return <>
    <div className="sf-dashboard-heading">
      <div>
        <p className="sf-eyebrow">YOUR DAY, A LITTLE MORE ORGANISED</p>
        <h1>Hello, {user?.name.split(' ')[0] || 'there'}.</h1>
        <p>Here's where things stand with your services.</p>
      </div>
      <div className="sf-dashboard-heading-actions">
        <span>{date}</span>
        <Link to="/companies" className="sf-primary-link"><Plus size={17} />New service request</Link>
      </div>
    </div>
    <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
      {data && <>
        <div className="sf-metrics-grid">
          {[
            { label: 'Service requests', count: data.requests.length, path: 'requests', icon: ClipboardList, note: 'All your requests' },
            { label: 'Quotes to review', count: pendingQuotes.length, path: 'quotations', icon: FileText, note: pendingQuotes.length ? 'Your decision is needed' : "You're all caught up" },
            { label: 'Active jobs', count: data.jobs.filter((job: any) => !['COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED'].includes(job.status)).length, path: 'jobs', icon: CalendarDays, note: 'Work moving forward' },
            { label: 'Invoices', count: data.invoices.length, path: 'invoices', icon: Receipt, note: 'Everything on record' },
          ].map(({ label, count, path, icon: Icon, note }) => (
            <Link key={path} to={`/client/${path}`} className="sf-metric-card">
              <div>
                <span className="sf-metric-icon"><Icon size={19} /></span>
                <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
              </div>
              <p>{label}</p>
              <strong>{count.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</strong>
              <small>{note}</small>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="sf-dashboard-columns">
          <section className="sf-dashboard-panel">
            <div className="sf-panel-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="sf-eyebrow">LET'S KEEP THINGS MOVING</span>
                <h2>Your next steps</h2>
              </div>
              <span className="sf-count-badge">{pendingQuotes.length + pendingJobs.length}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="sf-task-list">
              {pendingQuotes.map((quote: any) => (
                <div key={quote.id} className="sf-task-row">
                  <span className="sf-task-icon"><FileText size={21} /></span>
                  <div>
                    <p className="sf-task-category">QUOTATION READY</p>
                    <h3>Review your {String(quote.service || '').toLowerCase()} quotation</h3>
                    <p>{quote.company_name} · {money(Number(quote.total || 0))}</p>
                    <Link to={`/client/quotations/${quote.id}`}>Review quotation →</Link>
                  </div>
                  <span className="sf-task-dot" />
                </div>
              ))}
              {pendingJobs.map((job: any) => (
                <div key={job.id} className="sf-task-row">
                  <span className="sf-task-icon sf-task-icon-blue"><CalendarDays size={21} /></span>
                  <div>
                    <p className="sf-task-category">APPOINTMENT PROPOSED</p>
                    <h3>Confirm a proposed appointment</h3>
                    <p>{job.company_name} · {job.appointment ? dateTime(job.appointment) : 'Awaiting slot'}</p>
                    <Link to={`/client/jobs/${job.id}`}>Review appointment →</Link>
                  </div>
                  <span className="sf-task-dot" />
                </div>
              ))}
              {!pendingQuotes.length && !pendingJobs.length && (
                <div className="sf-caught-up">
                  <Check size={28} />
                  <h3>You're all caught up.</h3>
                  <p>We'll show your next decision here when a quotation or appointment is ready.</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>

          <aside className="sf-dashboard-aside">
            <div className="sf-service-prompt">
              <span className="sf-eyebrow">MAKE ROOM FOR WHAT MATTERS</span>
              <h2>One less thing<br />on your list.</h2>
              <p>Find a company to help with your next home service.</p>
              <Link to="/companies">Explore services<ArrowUpRight size={18} /></Link>
              <div className="sf-prompt-lines" aria-hidden="true"><span /><span /><span /></div>
            </div>
            <div className="sf-help-note">
              <Receipt size={20} />
              <div>
                <strong>A quick note on payments</strong>
                <p>Pay your company offline. Your manager records the payment, and you'll see the updated invoice here.</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>

        <section className="sf-dashboard-panel sf-recent-panel">
          <div className="sf-panel-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="sf-eyebrow">EVERYTHING IN VIEW</span>
              <h2>Recent requests</h2>
            </div>
            <Link to="/client/requests" className="sf-text-action">View all<ArrowRight size={16} /></Link>
          </div>
          {data.requests.length ? (
            <div className="sf-recent-list">
              {data.requests.slice(0, 4).map((request: any) => (
                <Link key={request.id} to={`/client/requests/${request.id}`} className="sf-recent-row">
                  <span className="sf-recent-icon"><ClipboardList size={19} /></span>
                  <div>
                    <strong>{request.service}</strong>
                    <p>{request.company_name} · {request.id}</p>
                  </div>
                  <RecordStatus status={request.status} />
                  <ArrowRight size={17} />
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="sf-caught-up">
              <ClipboardList size={28} />
              <h3>Your first request starts here.</h3>
              <p>Browse companies to find the service you need.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}


=== src\features\client\FeedbackForms.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { ActionButton, SelectField, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField } from '../../components'
import { Notice, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { clientFeedback } from '../../lib/client-feedback'
import { demoMode } from '../../lib/api'

export function ReviewForm({ jobId }: { jobId: string }) {
  const resource = useResource(() => clientFeedback.reviewForJob(jobId), [jobId])
  const [rating, setRating] = useState('5')
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending) return
    setPending(true)
    setError('')
    try { await clientFeedback.review(jobId, Number(rating), comment); resource.reload() }
    catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-3xl space-y-5 p-6">
    <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Review your completed service</h2>
    {!demoMode ? <Notice>Reviews are awaiting a backend API.</Notice> : <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
      {resource.data ? <Notice>Your sample review was saved. {resource.data.rating} / 5 — {resource.data.comment}</Notice> : <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
        <Notice>Sample reviews appear on the company profile during this demo session.</Notice>
        <SelectField label="Rating" value={rating} onChange={event => setRating(event.target.value)}>{[5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map(value => <option key={value} value={value}>{value} out of 5</option>)}</SelectField>
        <TextAreaField label="Your review" required minLength={10} maxLength={1000} value={comment} onChange={event => setComment(event.target.value)} />
        {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" type="submit" disabled={pending || comment.trim().length < 10}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Submit review'}</ActionButton>
      </form>}
    </Resource>}
  </SurfaceCard>
}

export function DisputeForm({ kind, recordId }: { kind: 'request' | 'job'; recordId: string }) {
  const resource = useResource(() => clientFeedback.disputes(kind, recordId), [kind, recordId])
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending) return
    setPending(true)
    setError('')
    try { await clientFeedback.dispute(kind, recordId, reason); resource.reload() }
    catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-3xl p-6">
    <details>
      <summary className="cursor-pointer font-semibold">Report an issue with this {kind}</summary>
      <div className="mt-5 space-y-5">
        {!demoMode ? <Notice>Disputes are awaiting a backend API.</Notice> : <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
          {resource.data?.length ? resource.data.map(dispute => <Notice key={dispute.id}>Sample dispute open · {dispute.reason}</Notice>) : <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
            <Notice>This sample submission does not notify the company or an administrator.</Notice>
            <TextAreaField label="Describe the issue" required minLength={10} maxLength={2000} value={reason} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} />
            {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
            <ActionButton variant="stamp" type="submit" disabled={pending || reason.trim().length < 10}>{pending ? 'Submitting…' : 'Submit dispute'}</ActionButton>
          </form>}
        </Resource>}
      </div>
    </details>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, Modal, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField } from '../../components'
import { dateTime, EmptyRecords, money, Notice, PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, statusLabel, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'
import { DisputeForm, ReviewForm } from './FeedbackForms'
import { RequestTimeline } from './RequestTimeline'
import type { Invoice, Job, Quote, RequestRecord } from '../../types/client'

type Kind = 'requests' | 'quotations' | 'jobs' | 'invoices'
type Row = RequestRecord | Quote | Job | Invoice
export function RecordsPage({ kind }: { kind: Kind }) {
  const loader = async (): Promise<Row[]> => kind === 'requests' ? clientService.requests() : kind === 'quotations' ? clientService.quotes() : kind === 'jobs' ? clientService.jobs() : clientService.invoices()
  const resource = useResource(loader, [kind]); const [filter, setFilter] = useState(''); const rows = resource.data ?? []; const filtered = rows.filter(r => !filter || r.status === filter)
  return <><PageHeader eyebrow="CLIENT WORKSPACE" title={`Your ${kind}`} description="Open a record to see the details and available actions." /><div className="mb-5 max-w-xs"><label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium" htmlFor="record-filter">Status</label><select id="record-filter" className="sf-input" value={filter} onChange={e => setFilter(e.target.value)}><option value="">All statuses</option>{[...new Set(rows.map(r => r.status))].map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{statusLabel(s)}</option>)}</select></div><Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>{filtered.length ? <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">{filtered.map(row => <SurfaceCard className="p-5" key={row.id}><div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">{row.id}</span><RecordStatus status={row.status} /></div><h2 className="sf-display text-lg font-semibold">{row.service}</h2><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">{row.company_name}</p>{'total' in row && <p className="sf-tabular mt-4 font-semibold">{money(row.total)}</p>}{'appointment' in row && row.appointment && <p className="mt-3 text-sm">{dateTime(row.appointment)} · Asia/Colombo</p>}<div className="sf-separator mt-5 pt-4"><Link className="sf-link" to={`/client/${kind}/${row.id}`}>View details →</Link></div></SurfaceCard>)}</div> : <EmptyRecords title={filter ? 'No records with this status' : `No ${kind} yet`} />}</Resource></>
}
type Confirmation = { title: string; description: string; reason?: boolean; run: (reason: string) => Promise<unknown> }
function useDecision(reload: () => void) {
  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState<Confirmation>(); const [reason, setReason] = useState(''); const [pending, setPending] = useState(false); const [error, setError] = useState(''); const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')
  const open = (value: Confirmation) => { setReason(''); setError(''); setSuccess(''); setConfirmation(value) }
  async function perform() { if (!confirmation || pending) return; setPending(true); setError(''); try { await confirmation.run(reason.trim()); setConfirmation(undefined); setSuccess('Your decision was saved.'); reload() } catch (e) { setError((e as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) } }
  return { open, feedback: success ? <Notice>{success}</Notice> : null, dialog: <Modal open={!!confirmation} title={confirmation?.title || ''} description={confirmation?.description} onClose={() => { if (!pending) setConfirmation(undefined) }}><form onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); void perform() }} className="space-y-5">{confirmation?.reason && <TextAreaField label="Reason" required maxLength={1000} value={reason} onChange={e => setReason(e.target.value)} />}{error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}<div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3"><ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || (!!confirmation?.reason && !reason.trim())}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Confirm decision'}</ActionButton><ActionButton disabled={pending} onClick={() => setConfirmation(undefined)}>Go back</ActionButton></div></form></Modal> }
}
function DetailShell({ kind, id, title, company, status, children }: { kind: Kind; id: string; title: string; company: string; status: string; children: ReactNode }) { return <><Link className="sf-link mb-6 inline-block" to={`/client/${kind}`}>← Your {kind}</Link><PageHeader eyebrow={id} title={title} description={company}><RecordStatus status={status} /></PageHeader><div className="space-y-5">{children}</div></> }
export function RequestDetailPage({ recordId }: { recordId?: string } = {}) {
  const params = useParams(); const id = recordId ?? params.id ?? '';  const resource = useResource(() => clientService.request(id), [id]); const row = resource.data; const decision = useDecision(resource.reload)
  return <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>{row && <DetailShell kind="requests" id={id} title={row.service} company={row.company_name} status={row.status}>{decision.feedback}<SurfaceCard className="max-w-3xl space-y-5 p-6"><div><h2 className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">DESCRIPTION</h2><p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap">{row.description}</p></div><div><h2 className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">SERVICE ADDRESS</h2><p className="mt-2">{row.address}</p></div><div><h2 className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">PREFERRED DATE</h2><p className="mt-2">{row.preferred_date} · Not a confirmed appointment</p></div><div className="sf-separator flex flex-wrap items-center gap-5 pt-5">{row.quotation_id && <Link className="sf-link" to={`/client/quotations/${row.quotation_id}`}>View quotation →</Link>}{['NEW', 'QUOTED'].includes(row.status) && <ActionButton variant="void" onClick={() => decision.open({ title: 'Cancel this request?', description: 'Any outstanding quotation will also be cancelled.', reason: true, run: reason => clientService.cancelRequest(id, reason) })}>Cancel request</ActionButton>}{row.status === 'CONVERTED' && <Link className="sf-link" to="/client/jobs">Track your jobs →</Link>}</div></SurfaceCard><RequestTimeline request={row} /><RequestAttachments request={row} /><DisputeForm kind="request" recordId={id} />{decision.dialog}</DetailShell>}</Resource>
}
function LineItems({ items, total }: { items: Quote['items']; total: number }) { return <div><div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left text-sm"><caption className="sr-only">Price breakdown in LKR</caption><thead><tr className="sf-mono border-b border-border text-xs"><th className="py-3">Description</th><th className="px-3 py-3 text-right">Qty</th><th className="py-3 text-right">Unit price</th></tr></thead><tbody>{items.map((item, i) => <tr key={i} className="border-b border-line"><td className="py-4">{item.description}</td><td className="px-3 text-right">{item.quantity}</td><td className="sf-tabular text-right">{money(item.unit_price)}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-3"><span className="sf-mono text-sm">TOTAL</span><strong className="sf-display sf-tabular text-2xl">{money(total)}</strong></div></div> }
export function QuoteDetailPage({ recordId }: { recordId?: string } = {}) {

An appointment will be arranged separately.`, run: () => clientService.decideQuote(id, 'approve') })}>Approve quotation</ActionButton><ActionButton variant="void" onClick={() => decision.open({ title: 'Decline this quotation?', description: 'This closes the linked request.', run: () => clientService.decideQuote(id, 'decline') })}>Decline</ActionButton></div></div>}{row.status === 'APPROVED' && <Link className="sf-link mt-6 inline-block" to="/client/jobs">View your jobs →</Link>}</SurfaceCard><Link className="sf-link inline-block" to={`/client/requests/${row.request_id}`}>View original request →</Link>{decision.dialog}</DetailShell>}</Resource>

const jobStages = ['JOB_CREATED', 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED', 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED']
export function JobDetailPage({ recordId }: { recordId?: string } = {}) {
  const params = useParams(); const id = recordId ?? params.id ?? '';  const resource = useResource(() => clientService.job(id), [id]); const row = resource.data; const decision = useDecision(resource.reload)

'step' : undefined} className={`flex items-center gap-3 text-sm ${index <= jobStages.indexOf(row.status) ? 'font-semibold' : 'text-text-secondary'}`}><span className={`sf-mono grid h-7 w-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-xs ${index <= jobStages.indexOf(row.status) ? 'bg-graphite text-white' : 'bg-paper'}`}>{index + 1}</span>{stage.toLowerCase().replaceAll('_', ' ')}</li>)}</ol>}</SurfaceCard></div>{row.status === 'COMPLETED' && <ReviewForm key={id} jobId={id} />}<DisputeForm key={id} kind="job" recordId={id} />{decision.dialog}</DetailShell>}</Resource>
}
