function RequestAttachments({ request }: { request: RequestRecord }) {
  if (!request.photos?.length && request.latitude === undefined && !request.preferred_time) return null
  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-3xl space-y-4 p-6">
    <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Request location and attachments</h2>
    {request.preferred_time && <p>Preferred time {request.preferred_time} · Asia/Colombo</p>}
    {request.latitude !== undefined && <p>Coordinates {request.latitude}, {request.longitude}</p>}
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">{request.photos?.map((photo, index) => (
      <figure key={index}><img className="w-full rounded-docket" src={photo.data_url} alt={`Request attachment ${index + 1}`} /><figcaption className="break-all text-xs">{photo.name}</figcaption></figure>
    ))}</div>
  </SurfaceCard>
}



=== src\features\client\ProfileSettings.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { ActionButton, SurfaceCard, TextField } from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { useSession } from '../../app/session'
import { demoMode, liveApiMode } from '../../lib/api'
import { demoAccount, requireDemo } from '../../lib/demo-account'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'

export function ProfileSettings() {
  const { user, requestLogout } = useSession()
  const resource = useResource(async () => {
    if (liveApiMode) return { name: user?.name || '', email: user?.email || '', phone: user?.phone || '' }
    requireDemo('Profile settings')
    return demoAccount.profile()
  }, [user?.id])
  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Profile / Settings" description="Keep your contact details and account password up to date." />
    <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
      {resource.data && <ProfileForm initial={resource.data} />}
    </Resource>
    <SurfaceCard className="mt-6 max-w-3xl space-y-4 p-6">
      <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Account session</h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Signed in as {user?.name}. End your current session when you have finished.</p>
      <ActionButton variant="void" onClick={requestLogout}>Log out</ActionButton>
    </SurfaceCard>
  </>
}

function ProfileForm({ initial }: { initial: { name: string; email: string; phone: string } }) {
  const { signIn } = useSession()
  const [name, setName] = useState(initial.name)
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(initial.phone)
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')

  async function submit(event: FormEvent, changePassword: boolean) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending) return
    setError('')
    setMessage('')
    if (changePassword && password !== confirm) { setError('Passwords must match.'); return }
    setPending(true)
    try {
      if (!liveApiMode) requireDemo('Profile settings')
      if (changePassword) {
        if (liveApiMode) await clientService.changePassword(password, currentPassword)
        else {
          await clientService.login(initial.email, currentPassword)
          await clientService.changePassword(password)
        }
        setCurrentPassword('')
        setPassword('')
        setConfirm('')
        setMessage('Your sample password was updated.')
      } else if (!liveApiMode) {
        const user = demoAccount.updateProfile(name.trim(), phone.trim())
        signIn({ access_token: '', user })
        setMessage('Your sample profile was updated.')
      }
    } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-5">
    {liveApiMode ? <Notice>Your account is connected to the shared API. Client profile editing needs a backend profile-update endpoint.</Notice> : demoMode && <Notice>Profile changes apply to this sample session and reset on refresh.</Notice>}
    <SurfaceCard className="p-6">
      <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={event => void submit(event, false)}>
        <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Contact details</h2>
        <TextField label="Full name" required maxLength={100} disabled={liveApiMode} value={name} onChange={event => setName(event.target.value)} />
        <TextField label="Email address" disabled value={initial.email} hint="Email changes require a new verification flow." />
        <TextField label="Phone number" type="tel" required minLength={7} maxLength={30} disabled={liveApiMode} value={phone} onChange={event => setPhone(event.target.value)} />
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" type="submit" disabled={liveApiMode || pending || !name.trim() || !phone.trim()}>Save profile</ActionButton>
      </form>
    </SurfaceCard>
    <SurfaceCard className="p-6">
      <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={event => void submit(event, true)}>
        <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Change password</h2>
        <TextField label="Current password" type="password" autoComplete="current-password" required value={currentPassword} onChange={event => setCurrentPassword(event.target.value)} />
        <TextField label="New password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required minLength={8} value={password} onChange={event => setPassword(event.target.value)} />
        <TextField label="Confirm password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={confirm} onChange={event => setConfirm(event.target.value)} />
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" type="submit" disabled={pending}>Update password</ActionButton>
      </form>
    </SurfaceCard>
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    {message && <Notice>{message}</Notice>}
  </div>
}



=== src\features\client\RequestTimeline.tsx ===

import { SurfaceCard } from '../../components'
import { Notice, Resource, statusLabel, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'
import type { RequestRecord } from '../../types/client'

export function RequestTimeline({ request }: { request: RequestRecord }) {
  const resource = useResource(async () => {
    const jobs = await clientService.jobs()
    const job = jobs.find(value => value.request_id === request.id)
    const invoices = job?.status === 'COMPLETED' ? await clientService.invoices() : []
    return { job, invoice: invoices.find(value => value.job_id === job?.id) }
  }, [request.id, request.status])
  const job = resource.data?.job
  const invoice = resource.data?.invoice
  const stages = [
    { title: 'Request submitted', complete: true },
    { title: 'Quotation received', complete: !!request.quotation_id },
    { title: 'Quotation approved / job created', complete: request.status === 'CONVERTED' },
    { title: 'Appointment proposed', complete: !!job?.appointment },
    { title: 'Appointment approved', complete: !!job && ['SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED'].includes(job.status) },
    { title: 'Work completed', complete: job?.status === 'COMPLETED' },
    { title: 'Invoice issued', complete: !!invoice },
    { title: 'Offline payment recorded', complete: invoice?.status === 'PAID' },
  ]
  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-3xl p-6">
    <details>
      <summary className="cursor-pointer font-semibold">Request tracking timeline</summary>
      <div className="mt-5">
        <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
          <Notice>Current request status · {statusLabel(request.status)}{job ? ` · ${statusLabel(job.status)}` : ''}</Notice>
          <p className="my-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">Progress is derived from current records. Historical event times will come from the backend.</p>
          <ol className="space-y-4">{stages.map(stage => <li key={stage.title} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line pb-3">
            <span>{stage.title}</span><span className="sf-mono text-xs">{stage.complete ? 'Reached' : ['CANCELLED', 'DECLINED', 'REJECTED'].includes(request.status) || job?.status === 'CANCELLED' ? 'Closed' : 'Pending'}</span>
          </li>)}</ol>
        </Resource>
      </div>
    </details>
  </SurfaceCard>
}



=== src\features\client\SharedExamples.tsx ===

import { useState } from 'react'

      </dl>}

=== src\features\employee\JobDetail.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  ActionButton, Modal, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, RecordStatus, Resource, dateTime, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { employeeService } from '../../lib/employee-service'

export default function EmployeeJobDetail() {
  const { jobId } = useParams<{ jobId: string }>()
  const id = Number(jobId)
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  if (!id || Number.isNaN(id)) return <p className="p-6">Invalid job.</p>

  const job = useResource(() => employeeService.getJob(id), [id])
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState<'NOTE' | 'MATERIAL' | 'PHOTO' | null>(null)
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<'start' | 'complete' | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState<number | null>(null)

  async function start() {
    setPending('start'); setError('')
    try { await employeeService.startJob(id); job.reload() }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }
  async function complete() {
    setPending('complete'); setError('')
    try {
      await employeeService.completeJob(id)
      setConfirming(false); job.reload()
      setTimeout(() => navigate('/employee/jobs'), 800)
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }

  async function downloadPhoto(recordId: number, fileName: string) {
    setDownloading(recordId); setError('')
    try { await employeeService.downloadJobPhoto(id, recordId, fileName) }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setDownloading(null) }
  }

  return <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
    <Link to="/employee/jobs" className="sf-link">← Back to My Day</Link>
    <Resource {...job} retry={job.reload}>
      {job.data && <>
        <SurfaceCard className="space-y-3 p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <h1 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Job #{job.data.id}</h1>
            <RecordStatus status={job.data.status} />
          </div>
          {job.data.schedule_date
            ? <p className="text-sm">
                Scheduled <strong>{job.data.schedule_date} {job.data.schedule_time?.slice(0, 5)}</strong>
                {' · '}<span className="text-text-secondary">{job.data.schedule_status}</span>
              </p>
            : <Notice>Not scheduled yet.</Notice>}
          {job.data.completed_at && (
            <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Completed {dateTime(job.data.completed_at)}</p>
          )}
        </SurfaceCard>

        {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {job.data.status === 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED' && (
            <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={start} disabled={pending === 'start'}>
              {pending === 'start' ? 'Starting…' : 'Start job'}
            </ActionButton>
          )}
          {job.data.status === 'IN_PROGRESS' && (
            <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setConfirming(true)}>Complete job</ActionButton>
          )}
          {job.data.status === 'JOB_CREATED' && (
            <Notice>Waiting for the schedule to be confirmed.</Notice>
          )}
          {['COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED'].includes(job.data.status) && (
            <Notice>This job is {job.data.status.toLowerCase()}.</Notice>
          )}
        </div>

        <SurfaceCard className="space-y-3 p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">Work log</h2>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setAdding('NOTE')}>+ Note</ActionButton>
              <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setAdding('MATERIAL')}>+ Material</ActionButton>
              <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setAdding('PHOTO')}>+ Photo</ActionButton>
            </div>
          </div>
          {job.data.records.length === 0
            ? <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">No records yet.</p>
            : <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                {job.data.records.map(r => (
                  <li key={r.id} className="py-2">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-text-secondary">
                      <span>{r.record_type}</span>
                      <span>{dateTime(r.created_at)}</span>
                    </div>
                    {r.note && <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm">{r.note}</p>}
                    {r.material_name && (
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm">
                        <strong>{r.material_name}</strong>
                        {r.quantity && ` · ${r.quantity} ${r.unit ?? ''}`}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {r.file_name && (
                      <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-2 text-sm">
                        <span className="truncate">{r.file_name}</span>
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="stub"
                          size="small"
                          onClick={() => downloadPhoto(r.id, r.file_name!)}
                          disabled={downloading === r.id}
                        >
                          {downloading === r.id ? '…' : 'Download'}
                        </ActionButton>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>}
        </SurfaceCard>
      </>}
    </Resource>

    <Modal
      open={adding !== null}
      title={adding === 'NOTE' ? 'Add note' : adding === 'MATERIAL' ? 'Add material' : 'Upload photo'}
      onClose={() => setAdding(null)}
    >
      {adding && adding !== 'PHOTO' && <AddRecord
        jobId={id}
        type={adding}
        onDone={() => { setAdding(null); job.reload() }}
        onCancel={() => setAdding(null)}
      />}
      {adding === 'PHOTO' && <UploadPhoto
        jobId={id}
        onDone={() => { setAdding(null); job.reload() }}
        onCancel={() => setAdding(null)}
      />}
    </Modal>

    <Modal open={confirming} title="Complete this job?" onClose={() => setConfirming(false)}>
      <p className="text-sm">Completing the job creates an invoice. This cannot be undone.</p>
      {error && <div className="mt-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
        <ActionButton onClick={() => setConfirming(false)}>Cancel</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={complete} disabled={pending === 'complete'}>
          {pending === 'complete' ? 'Completing…' : 'Complete job'}
        </ActionButton>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  </div>
}

function AddRecord({ jobId, type, onDone, onCancel }: {
  jobId: number
  type: 'NOTE' | 'MATERIAL'
  onDone: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const [materialName, setMaterialName] = useState('')
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState('')
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      if (type === 'NOTE') {
        await employeeService.createRecord(jobId, { record_type: 'NOTE', note: note.trim() })
      } else {
        await employeeService.createRecord(jobId, {
          record_type: 'MATERIAL',
          material_name: materialName.trim(),
          quantity: quantity ? Number(quantity) : undefined,
          unit: unit.trim() || undefined,
        })
      }
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    {type === 'NOTE'
      ? <TextAreaField label="Note" value={note} onChange={e => setNote(e.target.value)} required />
      : <>
          <TextField label="Material name" value={materialName} onChange={e => setMaterialName(e.target.value)} required maxLength={150} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <TextField label="Quantity" type="number" min={0} step="0.01" value={quantity} onChange={e => setQuantity(e.target.value)} />
            <TextField label="Unit" value={unit} onChange={e => setUnit(e.target.value)} maxLength={30} placeholder="e.g. pcs, m" />
          </div>
        </>}
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save'}</ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}

function UploadPhoto({ jobId, onDone, onCancel }: {
  jobId: number
  onDone: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending || !file) return
    if (!['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'image/heic'].includes(file.type)) {
      setError('Only JPG, PNG, WebP or HEIC images are allowed.')
      return
    }
    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError('Image must be 5 MB or smaller.')
      return
    }
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      await employeeService.uploadJobPhoto(jobId, file)
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <div>
      <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium">Photo (JPG, PNG, WebP, HEIC · max 5 MB)</label>
      <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="sf-input" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} required />
    </div>
    {file && <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">{file.name} · {(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB</p>}
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !file}>{pending ? 'Uploading…' : 'Upload'}</ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}


=== src\features\employee\Jobs.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, SurfaceCard, Tabs, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, RecordStatus, Resource, useResource, today } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { employeeService } from '../../lib/employee-service'
import type { EmployeeRow, SkillRow } from '../../types/provider'

const tabs = [
  { key: 'today', label: 'Today' },
  { key: 'upcoming', label: 'Upcoming' },
  { key: 'history', label: 'History' },
  { key: 'profile', label: 'Profile' },
  { key: 'skills', label: 'Skills' },
] as const
type Tab = typeof tabs[number]['key']

export default function EmployeeJobs() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('today')
  return <div className="space-y-6">
    <div>
      <h1 className="sf-display text-2xl font-bold">My Day</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">Your jobs and personal info.</p>
    </div>
    <Tabs label="My Day" items={tabs} value={tab} onChange={setTab}>
      {tab === 'today' && <JobsTab tab="today" />}
      {tab === 'upcoming' && <JobsTab tab="upcoming" />}
      {tab === 'history' && <JobsTab tab="history" />}
      {tab === 'profile' && <ProfileTab />}
      {tab === 'skills' && <SkillsTab />}
    </Tabs>
  </div>
}

// ---------- Jobs ----------
function JobsTab({ tab }: { tab: 'today' | 'upcoming' | 'history' }) {
  const jobs = useResource(() => employeeService.listMyJobs())
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const all = jobs.data?.items ?? []
  const todayStr = today()

  const filtered =
    tab === 'today'
      ? all.filter(j =>
          j.schedule_date === todayStr &&
          ['SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED'].includes(j.status),
        )
      : tab === 'upcoming'
      ? all
          .filter(j =>
            j.status === 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED' &&
            j.schedule_date &&
            j.schedule_date > todayStr,
          )
          .sort((a, b) => (a.schedule_date ?? '').localeCompare(b.schedule_date ?? ''))
      : all
          .filter(j => j.status === 'COMPLETED')
          .sort((a, b) => (b.schedule_date ?? '').localeCompare(a.schedule_date ?? ''))

  return <Resource {...jobs} retry={jobs.reload}>
    {filtered.length === 0
      ? <EmptyState
          title={`No ${tab} jobs`}
          description={
            tab === 'today' ? 'No jobs scheduled for today.' :
            tab === 'upcoming' ? 'No upcoming jobs assigned yet.' :
            'Completed jobs will appear here.'
          }
        />
      : <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">{filtered.map(j => (
          <button
            key={j.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate(`/employee/jobs/${j.id}`)}
            className="text-left"
          >
            <SurfaceCard className="space-y-2 p-4 transition hover:border-graphite">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Job #{j.id}</span>
                <RecordStatus status={j.status} />
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm">
                {j.schedule_date
                  ? `${j.schedule_date}${j.schedule_time ? ` ${j.schedule_time.slice(0, 5)}` : ''}`
                  : 'Not scheduled'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
                Schedule: {j.schedule_status.replaceAll('_', ' ').toLowerCase()}
              </p>
            </SurfaceCard>
          </button>
        ))}</div>}
  </Resource>
}

// ---------- Profile ----------
function ProfileTab() {
  const profile = useResource(() => employeeService.me())
  return <Resource {...profile} retry={profile.reload}>
    {profile.data && <ProfileForm employee={profile.data} onSaved={profile.reload} />}
  </Resource>
}

function ProfileForm({ employee, onSaved }: { employee: EmployeeRow; onSaved: () => void }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(employee.name)
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(employee.phone)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)

  const dirty = name !== employee.name || phone !== employee.phone

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (pending || !dirty) return
    setPending(true); setError(''); setSaved(false)
    try {
      await employeeService.updateMe({ name: name.trim(), phone: phone.trim() })
      setSaved(true)
      onSaved()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-xl p-5">
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
      <div>
        <p className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">EMAIL</p>
        <p className="mt-1">{employee.email}</p>
      </div>
      <TextField
        label="Full name"
        value={name}
        onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
        required
        minLength={2}
        maxLength={150}
      />
      <TextField
        label="Phone"
        type="tel"
        value={phone}
        onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)}
        required
        minLength={7}
        maxLength={30}
      />
      {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
      {saved && <Notice>Profile saved.</Notice>}
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !dirty}>
        {pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save profile'}
      </ActionButton>
    </form>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

// ---------- Skills ----------
function SkillsTab() {
  const skills = useResource(() => employeeService.listSkills())
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function add(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (pending || !name.trim()) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      await employeeService.addSkill(name.trim())
      setName('')
      skills.reload()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  async function remove(id: number) {
    setError('')
    try {
      await employeeService.removeSkill(id)
      skills.reload()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) }
  }

  return <>
    <SurfaceCard className="mb-4 max-w-xl p-4">
      <form onSubmit={add} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-[200px] flex-1">
          <TextField
            label="Add a skill"
            value={name}
            onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
            required
            maxLength={100}
            placeholder="e.g. Electrical maintenance"
          />
        </div>
        <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !name.trim()}>
          {pending ? 'Adding…' : 'Add'}
        </ActionButton>
      </form>
      {error && <div className="mt-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
    </SurfaceCard>

    <Resource {...skills} retry={skills.reload}>
      {skills.data && skills.data.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No skills yet" description="Add skills so your manager can schedule you for matching jobs." />
        : <div className="max-w-xl space-y-2">{skills.data?.map((s: SkillRow) => (
            <SurfaceCard key={s.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3">
              <span>{s.skill_name}</span>
              <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => remove(s.id)}>Remove</ActionButton>
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}


=== src\features\manager\CompanyProfile.tsx ===

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ActionButton,
  SurfaceCard,
  TextField,
  TextAreaField,
  SelectField,
  ResponsiveTable,
  EmptyState,
} from "../../components";
import {
  mockCompanyProfile,
  mockOfferedServices,
  mockServices,
  type CompanyProfile,
  type OfferedService,
} from "../../mocks/data";

export default function CompanyProfilePage() {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<CompanyProfile>(mockCompanyProfile);
  const [offered, setOffered] = useState<OfferedService[]>(mockOfferedServices);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [addingService, setAddingService] = useState(false);
  const [selectedServiceId, setSelectedServiceId] = useState("");
  const [newPrice, setNewPrice] = useState("0");

  const saveProfile = () => {
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000);
  };

  const addService = () => {
    const svc = mockServices.find((s) => s.id === selectedServiceId);
    if (!svc) return;
    if (offered.some((o) => o.serviceId === selectedServiceId)) return;

    const price = Number(newPrice) || 0;
    const newOffered: OfferedService = {
      id: `os-${Date.now()}`,
      serviceId: svc.id,
      serviceName: svc.name,
      startingPrice: price,
      isActive: true,
    };

    setOffered([...offered, newOffered]);
    setSelectedServiceId("");
    setNewPrice("0");
    setAddingService(false);
  };

  const updatePrice = (id: string, price: number) => {
    setOffered(offered.map((o) => (o.id === id ? { ...o, startingPrice: price } : o)));
  };

  const removeService = (id: string) => {
    setOffered(offered.filter((o) => o.id !== id));
  };

  const availableServices = mockServices.filter(
    (s) => s.isActive && !offered.some((o) => o.serviceId === s.id)
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-3xl">
      <h1 className="sf-display text-2xl font-bold">Company Profile</h1>

      <SurfaceCard className="p-5 space-y-4">
        <h2 className="font-semibold">Company details</h2>
        <TextField
          label="Company name"
          value={profile.name}
          onChange={(e) => setProfile({ ...profile, name: e.target.value })}
          required
        />
        <TextAreaField
          label="Description"
          value={profile.description}
          onChange={(e) => setProfile({ ...profile, description: e.target.value })}
        />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <TextField
            label="Email"
            type="email"
            value={profile.email}
            onChange={(e) => setProfile({ ...profile, email: e.target.value })}
            required
          />
          <TextField
            label="Phone"
            value={profile.phone}
            onChange={(e) => setProfile({ ...profile, phone: e.target.value })}
            required
          />
        </div>
        <TextField
          label="Address"
          value={profile.address}
          onChange={(e) => setProfile({ ...profile, address: e.target.value })}
        />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={saveProfile}>
            Save details
          </ActionButton>
          {saved && <span className="text-sm text-[var(--sf-success)]">Saved ✓</span>}
        </div>
      </SurfaceCard>

      <SurfaceCard className="p-5 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-semibold">Offered services</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
              Services your company offers, with starting prices in LKR.
            </p>
          </div>
          <ActionButton
            variant="stub"
            size="small"
            onClick={() => setAddingService(true)}
            disabled={availableServices.length === 0}
          >
            + Offer service
          </ActionButton>
        </div>

        {addingService && (
          <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 space-y-3">
            <SelectField
              label="Service"
              value={selectedServiceId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedServiceId(e.target.value)}
              required
            >
              <option value="">— Select service —</option>
              {availableServices.map((s) => (
                <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                  {s.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </SelectField>
            <TextField
              label="Starting price (LKR)"
              type="number"
              min={0}
              step="0.01"
              value={newPrice}
              onChange={(e) => setNewPrice(e.target.value)}
              required
            />
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <ActionButton
                variant="stub"
                onClick={() => {
                  setAddingService(false);
                  setSelectedServiceId("");
                  setNewPrice("0");
                }}
              >
                Cancel
              </ActionButton>
              <ActionButton variant="stamp" disabled={!selectedServiceId} onClick={addService}>
                Add service
              </ActionButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <ResponsiveTable
          caption="Offered services"
          rows={offered}
          columns={[
            {
              key: "name",
              heading: "Service",
              render: (o) => <span className="font-semibold">{o.serviceName}</span>,
            },
            {
              key: "price",
              heading: "Starting price (LKR)",
              render: (o) => (
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  step="0.01"
                  value={o.startingPrice}
                  onChange={(e) => updatePrice(o.id, Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="sf-input w-32 text-right"
                />
              ),
            },
            {
              key: "actions",
              heading: "",
              render: (o) => (
                <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => removeService(o.id)}>
                  Remove
                </ActionButton>
              ),
            },
          ]}
          empty={
            <EmptyState
              title="No services offered yet"
              description="Add services from the catalog to appear in the marketplace."
            />
          }
        />
      </SurfaceCard>
    </div>
  );
}



=== src\features\manager\Dashboard.tsx ===

import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, SurfaceCard } from '../../components'
import { Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { InvoiceRow, JobRow, RequestRow } from '../../types/provider'

export default function ManagerDashboard() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const data = useResource(async () => {
    const [requests, jobs, invoices] = await Promise.all([
      providerService.listIncomingRequests(),
      providerService.listJobs(),
      providerService.listInvoices(),
    ])
    return {
      requests: requests.items,
      jobs: jobs.items,
      invoices: invoices.items,
    }
  })

  return <>
    <div className="mb-6">
      <h1 className="sf-display text-2xl font-bold">Dashboard</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">Your work at a glance.</p>
    </div>

    <Resource {...data} retry={data.reload}>
      {data.data && <DashboardContent
        requests={data.data.requests}
        jobs={data.data.jobs}
        invoices={data.data.invoices}
        onNavigate={navigate}
      />}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function DashboardContent({ requests, jobs, invoices, onNavigate }: {
  requests: RequestRow[]
  jobs: JobRow[]
  invoices: InvoiceRow[]
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void
}) {
  const newRequests = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'NEW').length
  const underReview = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW').length
  const needInfo = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'NEED_INFORMATION').length
  const quotationSent = requests.filter(r => r.status === 'QUOTATION_SENT').length

  const jobsAwaitingSchedule = jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'JOB_CREATED').length
  const jobsInProgress = jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'IN_PROGRESS').length
  const jobsScheduled = jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED' || j.status === 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED').length
  const jobsCompleted = jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'COMPLETED').length

  const unpaid = invoices.filter(i => i.payment_status !== 'PAID')
  const outstandingAmount = unpaid.reduce((sum, i) =>
    sum + Number(i.total_amount) - Number(i.paid_amount), 0)
  const collectedAmount = invoices.reduce((sum, i) => sum + Number(i.paid_amount), 0)

  return <>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <MetricCard
        label="NEW REQUESTS"
        value={String(newRequests)}
        detail={`${underReview} under review · ${needInfo} need info`}
        onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/requests')}
      />
      <MetricCard
        label="AWAITING CLIENT"
        value={String(quotationSent)}
        detail="Quotations sent"
        onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/requests')}
      />
      <MetricCard
        label="JOBS IN PROGRESS"
        value={String(jobsInProgress)}
        detail={`${jobsScheduled} scheduled · ${jobsAwaitingSchedule} unscheduled`}
        onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/jobs')}
      />
      <MetricCard
        label="COMPLETED JOBS"
        value={String(jobsCompleted)}
        detail="All time"
        onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/jobs')}
      />
    </div>

    <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
      <MetricCard
        label="OUTSTANDING"
        value={`LKR ${outstandingAmount.toFixed(2)}`}
        detail={`${unpaid.length} invoice${unpaid.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} unpaid`}
        onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/invoices')}
        warning
      />
      <MetricCard
        label="COLLECTED"
        value={`LKR ${collectedAmount.toFixed(2)}`}
        detail="All recorded payments"
        onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/invoices')}
      />
    </div>

    <SurfaceCard className="mt-6 p-5">
      <h2 className="mb-3 font-semibold">Quick actions</h2>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/requests')}>Review requests</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/schedule')}>Schedule a job</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/invoices')}>Record payment</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/team')}>Manage team</ActionButton>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/business')}>My business</ActionButton>
      </div>
    </SurfaceCard>

    <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 p-5">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="font-semibold">Recent requests</h2>
        <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => onNavigate('/manager/requests')}>View all</ActionButton>
      </div>
      {requests.length === 0
        ? <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">No requests yet.</p>
        : <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
            {requests.slice(0, 5).map(r => (
              <li key={r.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 py-2">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium line-clamp-1">{r.description}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
                    #{r.id} · {r.status.replaceAll('_', ' ').toLowerCase()} · {r.address}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-text-secondary">{r.preferred_date}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>}
    </SurfaceCard>
  </>
}

function MetricCard({ label, value, detail, onClick, warning = false }: {
  label: string
  value: string
  detail?: string
  onClick?: () => void
  warning?: boolean
}) {
  return <button
    type="button"
    onClick={onClick}
    className="sf-card w-full p-5 text-left transition hover:border-graphite"
  >
    <p className="sf-mono text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{label}</p>
    <p className={`sf-tabular mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold ${warning && Number(value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) > 0 ? 'text-[var(--sf-warning)]' : ''}`}>
      {value}
    </p>
    {detail && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-secondary">{detail}</p>}
  </button>
}


=== src\features\manager\Disputes.tsx ===

import { EmptyState, SurfaceCard } from '../../components'
import { PageHeader, Resource, dateTime, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { DisputeRow } from '../../types/provider'

export default function ManagerDisputes() {
  const disputes = useResource(() => providerService.listMyDisputes())

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Disputes" description="Issues raised on your jobs. Resolved by the platform admin." />
    <Resource {...disputes} retry={disputes.reload}>
      {disputes.data && disputes.data.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No disputes" description="Open a dispute from a completed job if something went wrong." />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{disputes.data?.map(d => (
            <DisputeCard key={d.id} dispute={d} />
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function DisputeCard({ dispute }: { dispute: DisputeRow }) {
  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-3 p-4">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Dispute #{dispute.id} · Job #{dispute.job_id}</span>
        <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${statusClass(dispute.status)}`}>
          {dispute.status.replaceAll('_', ' ')}
        </span>
      </div>
      <span className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
        Opened by {dispute.opened_by === 0 ? 'you' : `user #${dispute.opened_by}`}
      </span>
    </div>
    <div>
      <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{dispute.reason}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm">{dispute.description}</p>
    </div>
    <div className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
      Opened {dateTime(dispute.created_at)}
      {dispute.closed_at && ` · Closed ${dateTime(dispute.closed_at)}`}
    </div>
    {dispute.admin_note && (
      <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
        <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Admin note</p>
        <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">{dispute.admin_note}</p>
      </div>
    )}
  </SurfaceCard>
}

function statusClass(s: string): string {
  if (s === 'OPEN') return 'bg-[var(--sf-warning-bg)] text-[var(--sf-warning)]'
  if (s === 'UNDER_REVIEW') return 'bg-[var(--sf-info-bg)] text-[var(--sf-info)]'
  if (s === 'RESOLVED') return 'bg-[var(--sf-success-bg)] text-[var(--sf-success)]'
  return 'bg-paper text-text-secondary'
}


=== src\features\manager\Invoices.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, ResponsiveTable, SelectField,
  StatusBadge, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { InvoiceRow, PaymentRow } from '../../types/provider'

// InvoiceRow has id: number — table needs id: string
type TableInvoice = Omit<InvoiceRow, 'id'> & { id: string }

type PaymentMethod = 'CASH' | 'BANK_TRANSFER' | 'CARD_EXTERNAL' | 'OTHER'

const methodLabels: Record<PaymentMethod, string> = {
  CASH: 'Cash',
  BANK_TRANSFER: 'Bank Transfer',
  CARD_EXTERNAL: 'Card (external)',
  OTHER: 'Other',
}

const money = (v: number) =>
  `LKR ${Number(v || 0).toLocaleString('en-LK', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`

const formatDateTime = (iso?: string) =>
  iso
    ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
        day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
        hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
      })
    : ''

// Compute status from amounts — InvoiceRow has no `status` field
function invoiceStatusOf(i: InvoiceRow): 'PAID' | 'PARTIAL' | 'UNPAID' {
  const row = i as any
  if (typeof row.status === 'string') return row.status
  if (typeof row.payment_status === 'string') return row.payment_status
  const total = Number(row.total_amount || 0)
  const paid = Number(row.paid_amount || 0)
  if (total > 0 && paid >= total) return 'PAID'
  if (paid > 0) return 'PARTIAL'
  return 'UNPAID'
}

const toBadge = (s: 'PAID' | 'PARTIAL' | 'UNPAID'): 'paid' | 'awaiting' | 'unpaid' =>
  s === 'PAID' ? 'paid' : s === 'PARTIAL' ? 'awaiting' : 'unpaid'

const getTotal = (i: InvoiceRow): number => Number((i as any).total_amount || 0)
const getPaid = (i: InvoiceRow): number => Number((i as any).paid_amount || 0)

export default function Invoices() {
  const invoices = useResource(() => providerService.listInvoices(), [])
  const [paying, setPaying] = useState<InvoiceRow | null>(null)

  return <>
    <PageHeader
      title="Invoices & Payments"
      description="View invoices and record offline payments."
    />
    <Resource {...invoices} retry={invoices.reload}>
      {invoices.data && (() => {
        const rows: InvoiceRow[] = Array.isArray(invoices.data)
          ? (invoices.data as InvoiceRow[])
          : (((invoices.data as any).items) as InvoiceRow[]) ?? []

        const unpaid = rows.filter(i => invoiceStatusOf(i) !== 'PAID')
        const paid = rows.filter(i => invoiceStatusOf(i) === 'PAID')
        const totalBilled = rows.reduce((s, i) => s + getTotal(i), 0)
        const totalPaid = rows.reduce((s, i) => s + getPaid(i), 0)
        const totalOutstanding = totalBilled - totalPaid

        // Table requires id: string — map number ids to strings
        const tableRows: TableInvoice[] = rows.map(i => ({
          ...(i as any),
          id: String((i as any).id),
        }))

        return <>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-5">
            <div className="sf-card p-4">
              <p className="sf-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-text-secondary">Outstanding</p>
              <p className="sf-tabular font-semibold text-lg mt-1">{money(totalOutstanding)}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">{unpaid.length} invoice{unpaid.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="sf-card p-4">
              <p className="sf-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-text-secondary">Collected</p>
              <p className="sf-tabular font-semibold text-lg mt-1">{money(totalPaid)}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">{paid.length} paid</p>
            </div>
            <div className="sf-card p-4">
              <p className="sf-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-text-secondary">Total billed</p>
              <p className="sf-tabular font-semibold text-lg mt-1">{money(totalBilled)}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">{rows.length} invoice{rows.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="sf-card p-4">
              <p className="sf-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-text-secondary">Awaiting payment</p>
              <p className="sf-tabular font-semibold text-lg mt-1">{unpaid.length}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1">Action needed</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {rows.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No invoices yet"
              description="Completing a job creates an invoice automatically."
            />
          ) : (
            <ResponsiveTable
              caption="Invoices"
              rows={tableRows}
              columns={[
                {
                  key: 'number',
                  heading: 'Invoice #',
                  render: (i) => <span className="sf-mono text-xs">{(i as any).invoice_number}</span>,
                },
                {
                  key: 'job',
                  heading: 'Job',
                  render: (i) => <span className="sf-mono text-xs">#{(i as any).job_id}</span>,
                },
                {
                  key: 'total',
                  heading: 'Total',
                  render: (i) => (
                    <span className="sf-tabular font-semibold">
                      {money(Number((i as any).total_amount || 0))}
                    </span>
                  ),
                },
                {
                  key: 'paid',
                  heading: 'Paid',
                  render: (i) => (
                    <span className="sf-tabular">
                      {money(Number((i as any).paid_amount || 0))}
                    </span>
                  ),
                },
                {
                  key: 'balance',
                  heading: 'Balance',
                  render: (i) => {
                    const bal = Number((i as any).total_amount || 0) - Number((i as any).paid_amount || 0)
                    return (
                      <span className={`sf-tabular font-semibold ${bal > 0 ? 'text-[#B93832]' : ''}`}>
                        {money(bal)}
                      </span>
                    )
                  },
                },
                {
                  key: 'status',
                  heading: 'Status',
                  render: (i) => <StatusBadge status={toBadge(invoiceStatusOf(i as any))} />,
                },
                {
                  key: 'actions',
                  heading: '',
                  render: (i) => (
                    <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      {invoiceStatusOf(i as any) !== 'PAID' && (
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="stamp"
                          size="small"
                          onClick={() => setPaying(i as any)}
                        >
                          Record payment
                        </ActionButton>
                      )}
                      <ActionButton
                        variant="stub"
                        size="small"
                        onClick={() => providerService.downloadInvoicePdf((i as any).id)}
                      >
                        PDF
                      </ActionButton>
                    </div>
                  ),
                },
              ]}
            />
          )}
        </>
      })()}
    </Resource>

    <Modal open={!!paying} title="Record payment" onClose={() => setPaying(null)}>
      {paying && (
        <PaymentForm
          invoice={paying}
          onDone={() => { setPaying(null); invoices.reload() }}
          onCancel={() => setPaying(null)}
        />
      )}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function PaymentForm({ invoice, onDone, onCancel }: {
  invoice: InvoiceRow
  onDone: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const invoiceId = (invoice as any).id
  const payments = useResource(
    () => providerService.listPayments(invoiceId),
    [invoiceId],
  )
  const total = getTotal(invoice)
  const paid = getPaid(invoice)
  const outstanding = Math.max(0, total - paid)
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(String(outstanding.toFixed(2)))
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<PaymentMethod>('CASH')
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setPending(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.recordPayment(invoiceId, {
        amount: Number(amount),
        method,
        note: note.trim() || undefined,
      })
      onDone()
    } catch (c) {
      setError((c as Error).message)
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm space-y-1">
        <p className="text-text-secondary text-xs">Invoice</p>
        <p className="font-semibold">{(invoice as any).invoice_number}</p>
        <p className="text-text-secondary mt-2 text-xs">Outstanding balance</p>
        <p className="sf-tabular font-semibold text-lg">{money(outstanding)}</p>
      </div>

      <Resource {...payments} retry={payments.reload}>
        {payments.data && (() => {
          const payRows: PaymentRow[] = Array.isArray(payments.data)
            ? (payments.data as PaymentRow[])
            : (((payments.data as any).items) as PaymentRow[]) ?? []
          if (payRows.length === 0) return null
          return (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2 text-sm">Payment history</h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {payRows.map((p) => {
                  const row = p as any
                  const label = methodLabels[row.method as PaymentMethod] ?? row.method ?? '—'
                  const when = row.paid_at ?? row.created_at ?? row.payment_date ?? ''
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={row.id}
                      className="text-sm border-b border-line pb-2 flex justify-between gap-2"
                    >
                      <span>
                        {label}
                        {row.note && ` · ${row.note}`}
                      </span>
                      <span className="sf-tabular text-right">
                        {money(Number(row.amount || 0))} · {formatDateTime(when)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })()}
      </Resource>

      <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3">
        <TextField
          label="Amount (LKR)"
          type="number"
          step="0.01"
          min="0.01"
          value={amount}
          onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}
          required
          hint={`Maximum ${money(outstanding)} — partial payments leave a balance`}
        />
        <SelectField
          label="Payment method"
          value={method}
          onChange={e => setMethod(e.target.value as PaymentMethod)}
          required
        >
          <option value="CASH">Cash</option>
          <option value="BANK_TRANSFER">Bank Transfer</option>
          <option value="CARD_EXTERNAL">Card (external)</option>
          <option value="OTHER">Other</option>
        </SelectField>
        <TextField
          label="Note (optional)"
          value={note}
          onChange={e => setNote(e.target.value)}
        />
        {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <ActionButton variant="stub" onClick={onCancel} disabled={pending}>
            Cancel
          </ActionButton>
          <ActionButton
            type="submit"
            variant="stamp"
            disabled={pending || !amount || Number(amount) <= 0}
          >
            {pending ? 'Recording…' : 'Record payment'}
          </ActionButton>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  )
}


=== src\features\manager\Jobs.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SelectField, SurfaceCard,
  Tabs, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { EmployeeRow, JobRow } from '../../types/provider'

const tabs = [
  { key: 'all', label: 'All jobs' },
  { key: 'active', label: 'In progress' },
  { key: 'completed', label: 'Completed' },
  { key: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled' },
] as const
type Tab = typeof tabs[number]['key']

const statusFilter: Record<Tab, string | undefined> = {
  all: undefined,
  active: 'IN_PROGRESS',
  completed: 'COMPLETED',
  cancelled: 'CANCELLED',
}

export default function ManagerJobs() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('all')
  const jobs = useResource(
    () => providerService.listJobs(statusFilter[tab] ? { status: statusFilter[tab] } : undefined),
    [tab],
  )
  const employees = useResource((): Promise<EmployeeRow[]> =>
    providerService.listEmployees().catch(() => [] as EmployeeRow[]),
  )
  const [openJobId, setOpenJobId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Jobs" description="Every job created from your accepted quotations." />
    <Tabs label="Job views" items={tabs} value={tab} onChange={setTab}>
      <Resource {...jobs} retry={jobs.reload}>
        {jobs.data && (jobs.data.items.length === 0
          ? <EmptyState
              title="No jobs here"
              description={
                tab === 'all' ? 'Completed requests become jobs.' :
                tab === 'active' ? 'No jobs currently in progress.' :
                tab === 'completed' ? 'No completed jobs yet.' :
                'No cancelled jobs.'
              }
            />
          : <div className="space-y-3">{jobs.data.items.map((j: JobRow) => (
              <SurfaceCard key={j.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Job #{j.id}</span>
                    <RecordStatus status={j.status} />
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                    Request #{j.request_id}
                    {j.schedule_date && ` · ${j.schedule_date} ${j.schedule_time?.slice(0, 5) ?? ''}`}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
                    {j.assigned_user_id ? `Assigned to employee #${j.assigned_user_id}` : 'Not assigned'}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setOpenJobId(j.id)}>Manage</ActionButton>
              </SurfaceCard>
            ))}</div>
        )}
      </Resource>
    </Tabs>
    <Modal open={openJobId !== null} title={`Job #${openJobId ?? ''}`} onClose={() => setOpenJobId(null)}>
      {openJobId !== null && <JobManager
        jobId={openJobId}
        employees={employees.data ?? []}
        onChanged={() => { jobs.reload(); employees.reload() }}
        onClose={() => setOpenJobId(null)}
      />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function JobManager({ jobId, employees, onChanged, onClose }: {
  jobId: number
  employees: EmployeeRow[]
  onChanged: () => void
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const job = useResource(() => providerService.getJob(jobId), [jobId])
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<'assign' | 'schedule' | 'cancel' | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [employeeId, setEmployeeId] = useState('')
  const [date, setDate] = useState('')
  const [time, setTime] = useState('09:00')
  const [cancelReason, setCancelReason] = useState('')

  async function assign() {
    if (!employeeId) return
    setPending('assign'); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.assignEmployee(jobId, Number(employeeId))
      setEmployeeId(''); job.reload(); onChanged()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }

  async function propose(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!date || !time) return
    setPending('schedule'); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.proposeSchedule(jobId, date, `${time}:00`)
      job.reload(); onChanged()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }

  async function cancel() {
    if (!cancelReason.trim()) return
    setPending('cancel'); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.cancelJob(jobId, cancelReason.trim())
      job.reload(); onChanged()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }

  return <Resource {...job} retry={job.reload}>
    {job.data && (() => {
      const isReassign = !!job.data.assigned_user_id
      const canAssign = employees.length > 0 &&
        ['JOB_CREATED', 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED'].includes(job.data.status)
      const canSchedule = job.data.schedule_status === 'NOT_PROPOSED' && job.data.status === 'JOB_CREATED'
      const canCancel = ['JOB_CREATED', 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED', 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED'].includes(job.data.status)
      return <>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <RecordStatus status={job.data.status} />
            <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Schedule: {job.data.schedule_status}</span>
          </div>
          {job.data.schedule_date && (
            <p className="text-sm">Proposed: <strong>{job.data.schedule_date} at {job.data.schedule_time?.slice(0, 5)}</strong></p>
          )}
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
            {job.data.assigned_user_id ? `Assigned to employee #${job.data.assigned_user_id}` : 'No employee assigned'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {error && <div className="mt-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}

        {canAssign && (
          <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 space-y-3 p-4">
            <h3 className="font-semibold">{isReassign ? 'Reassign employee' : 'Assign employee'}</h3>
            {isReassign && <Notice>Changing the employee may reset the proposed schedule. The backend will validate.</Notice>}
            <SelectField label="Employee" value={employeeId} onChange={e => setEmployeeId(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">— {isReassign ? 'Pick a new employee' : 'Select'} —</option>
              {employees.filter(e => e.status === 'ACTIVE').map(e => (
                <option key={e.id} value={e.id}>{e.name}</option>
              ))}
            </SelectField>
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={assign} disabled={!employeeId || pending === 'assign'}>
                {pending === 'assign' ? 'Assigning…' : (isReassign ? 'Reassign' : 'Assign')}
              </ActionButton>
            </div>
          </SurfaceCard>
        )}

        {canSchedule && (
          <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 space-y-3 p-4">
            <h3 className="font-semibold">Propose schedule</h3>
            <form onSubmit={propose} className="space-y-3">
              <TextField label="Date" type="date" value={date} onChange={e => setDate(e.target.value)} required />
              <TextField label="Time" type="time" value={time} onChange={e => setTime(e.target.value)} required />
              <div className="flex justify-end">
                <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={!date || !time || pending === 'schedule'}>
                  {pending === 'schedule' ? 'Proposing…' : 'Propose schedule'}
                </ActionButton>
              </div>
            </form>
          </SurfaceCard>
        )}

        {canCancel && (
          <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 space-y-3 p-4">
            <h3 className="font-semibold">Cancel job</h3>
            <TextField label="Reason" value={cancelReason} onChange={e => setCancelReason(e.target.value)} required />
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <ActionButton variant="void" onClick={cancel} disabled={!cancelReason.trim() || pending === 'cancel'}>
                {pending === 'cancel' ? 'Cancelling…' : 'Cancel job'}
              </ActionButton>
            </div>
          </SurfaceCard>
        )}

        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end">
          <ActionButton onClick={onClose}>Close</ActionButton>
        </div>
      </>
    })()}
  </Resource>
}


=== src\features\manager\MyBusiness.tsx ===

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SelectField, SurfaceCard,
  Tabs, TextAreaField, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type {
  CatalogService, PolicyRow, Provider, ProviderOffering,
  ServiceArea, WorkingHour,
} from '../../types/provider'

const tabs = [
  { key: 'services', label: 'Services' },
  { key: 'areas', label: 'Service Areas' },
  { key: 'hours', label: 'Working Hours' },
  { key: 'policies', label: 'Policies' },
  { key: 'settings', label: 'Settings' },
] as const
type Tab = typeof tabs[number]['key']

export default function MyBusiness() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('services')
  return <>
    <PageHeader title="My Business" description="Manage your offered services, availability and public profile." />
    <Tabs label="Business sections" items={tabs} value={tab} onChange={setTab}>
      {tab === 'services' && <ServicesTab />}
      {tab === 'areas' && <AreasTab />}
      {tab === 'hours' && <HoursTab />}
      {tab === 'policies' && <PoliciesTab />}
      {tab === 'settings' && <SettingsTab />}
    </Tabs>
  </>
}

// ---------- Services ----------
function ServicesTab() {
  const offerings = useResource(() => providerService.listMyOfferings())
  const catalog = useResource(() => providerService.listCatalogServices())
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<ProviderOffering | null>(null)

  return <>
    <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Pick services from the catalog and set your own price.</p>
      <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>Add service</ActionButton>
    </div>
    <Resource {...offerings} retry={offerings.reload}>
      {offerings.data && offerings.data.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No services yet" description="Add a service from the catalog to start receiving requests." />
        : <div className="space-y-3">{offerings.data?.map(o => (
            <SurfaceCard key={o.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="sf-display font-semibold">
                  {o.service_name}
                  {!o.is_active && <span className="ml-2 rounded bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">Inactive</span>}
                  {!o.catalog_is_active && <span className="ml-2 rounded bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">Catalog retired</span>}
                </h3>
                {o.description && <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">{o.description}</p>}
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="sf-tabular font-semibold">LKR {Number(o.starting_price).toFixed(2)}</span>
                <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setEditing(o)}>Edit</ActionButton>
              </div>
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
    <Modal open={adding} title="Add a service" onClose={() => setAdding(false)}>
      {adding && catalog.data && <AddOffering catalog={catalog.data} existing={offerings.data ?? []} onDone={() => { setAdding(false); offerings.reload() }} onCancel={() => setAdding(false)} />}
    </Modal>
    <Modal open={!!editing} title="Edit offering" onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
      {editing && <EditOffering offering={editing} onDone={() => { setEditing(null); offerings.reload() }} onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function AddOffering({ catalog, existing, onDone, onCancel }: {
  catalog: CatalogService[]; existing: ProviderOffering[]; onDone: () => void; onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const used = new Set(existing.map(o => o.service_id))
  const available = catalog.filter(c => c.is_active && !used.has(c.id))
  const [serviceId, setServiceId] = useState('')
  const [price, setPrice] = useState('0')
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending || !serviceId) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.createOffering({
        service_id: Number(serviceId),
        starting_price: Number(price),
        description: description.trim() || undefined,
      })
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    {available.length === 0 ? <Notice error>All catalog services are already offered.</Notice> : <>
      <SelectField label="Service" value={serviceId} onChange={e => setServiceId(e.target.value)} required>
        <option value="">— Select —</option>
        {available.map(s => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
      <TextField label="Starting price (LKR)" type="number" min={0} step="0.01" value={price} onChange={e => setPrice(e.target.value)} required />
      <TextAreaField label="Description (optional)" value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
    </>}
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !serviceId}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Add'}</ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}

function EditOffering({ offering, onDone, onCancel }: {
  offering: ProviderOffering; onDone: () => void; onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [price, setPrice] = useState(offering.starting_price)
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(offering.description ?? '')
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(offering.is_active)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.updateOffering(offering.id, {
        starting_price: Number(price),
        description: description.trim(),
        is_active: isActive,
      })
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Service: <strong>{offering.service_name}</strong></p>
    <TextField label="Starting price (LKR)" type="number" min={0} step="0.01" value={price} onChange={e => setPrice(e.target.value)} required />
    <TextAreaField label="Description" value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
      <input type="checkbox" checked={isActive} onChange={e => setIsActive(e.target.checked)} />
      Active (shown on your public profile)
    </label>
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save'}</ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}

// ---------- Service Areas ----------
function AreasTab() {
  const areas = useResource(() => providerService.listServiceAreas())
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  async function add(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending || !name.trim()) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try { await providerService.createServiceArea(name.trim()); setName(''); areas.reload() }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  async function remove(id: number) {
    setError('')
    try { await providerService.deleteServiceArea(id); areas.reload() }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) }
  }
  return <>
    <SurfaceCard className="mb-4 p-4">
      <form onSubmit={add} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-[200px] flex-1">
          <TextField label="Add a service area" value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} required maxLength={150} placeholder="e.g. Colombo 07" />
        </div>
        <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !name.trim()}>{pending ? 'Adding…' : 'Add'}</ActionButton>
      </form>
      {error && <div className="mt-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
    </SurfaceCard>
    <Resource {...areas} retry={areas.reload}>
      {areas.data && areas.data.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No service areas" description="Add the areas where you offer services." />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{areas.data?.map((a: ServiceArea) => (
            <SurfaceCard key={a.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3">
              <span>{a.area_name}</span>
              <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => remove(a.id)}>Remove</ActionButton>
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

// ---------- Working Hours ----------
const DAYS = ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday']

function HoursTab() {
  const hours = useResource(() => providerService.getWorkingHours())
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<WorkingHour[]>([])
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hours.data) return
    setDraft(Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => hours.data!.find(h => h.day_of_week === i) ?? {
      day_of_week: i, start_time: null, end_time: null, is_closed: true,
    }))
  }, [hours.data])

  const update = (i: number, patch: Partial<WorkingHour>) =>
    setDraft(d => d.map((h, idx) => idx === i ? { ...h, ...patch } : h))

  async function save() {
    setSaving(true); setError(''); setSaved(false)
    try {
      await providerService.saveWorkingHours(draft)
      setSaved(true); hours.reload()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setSaving(false) }
  }

  return <Resource {...hours} retry={hours.reload}>
    <SurfaceCard className="space-y-3 p-4">
      {draft.map((h, i) => {
        const open = !h.is_closed
        return <div key={h.day_of_week} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-b border-line pb-3 last:border-0">
          <span className="w-24 font-medium">{DAYS[h.day_of_week]}</span>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={open}
              onChange={e => update(i, e.target.checked
                ? { is_closed: false, start_time: h.start_time ?? '09:00:00', end_time: h.end_time ?? '17:00:00' }
                : { is_closed: true, start_time: null, end_time: null })}
            />
            {open ? 'Open' : 'Closed'}
          </label>
          {open && <>
            <input
              type="time"
              className="sf-input w-32"
              value={(h.start_time ?? '').slice(0, 5)}
              onChange={e => update(i, { start_time: e.target.value + ':00' })}
            />
            <span>to</span>
            <input
              type="time"
              className="sf-input w-32"
              value={(h.end_time ?? '').slice(0, 5)}
              onChange={e => update(i, { end_time: e.target.value + ':00' })}
            />
          </>}
        </div>
      })}
      {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
      {saved && <Notice>Working hours saved.</Notice>}
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={save} disabled={saving}>{saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save all 7 days'}</ActionButton>
      </div>
    </SurfaceCard>
  </Resource>
}

// ---------- Policies ----------
const POLICY_TYPES = [
  { value: 'SERVICE', label: 'Service policy' },
  { value: 'WARRANTY', label: 'Warranty' },
  { value: 'CANCELLATION', label: 'Cancellation' },
  { value: 'PAYMENT', label: 'Payment' },
  { value: 'TERMS_AND_CONDITIONS', label: 'Terms & Conditions' },
] as const

function PoliciesTab() {
  const policies = useResource(() => providerService.listMyPolicies())
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState<number | null>(null)

  async function remove(id: number) {
    setError('')
    try { await providerService.deletePolicy(id); policies.reload() }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) }
  }
  async function activate(id: number) {
    setError('')
    try { await providerService.activatePolicy(id); policies.reload() }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) }
  }
  async function download(id: number, title: string) {
    setDownloading(id); setError('')
    try { await providerService.downloadPolicy(id, title) }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setDownloading(null) }
  }

  return <>
    <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">One active policy per type. Only public policies are used by Flow AI.</p>
      <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>Upload policy</ActionButton>
    </div>
    {error && <div className="mb-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}
    <Resource {...policies} retry={policies.reload}>
      {policies.data && policies.data.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No policies yet" description="Upload a PDF policy to publish your terms." />
        : <div className="space-y-3">{policies.data?.map((p: PolicyRow) => (
            <SurfaceCard key={p.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="sf-display font-semibold">
                  {p.title}
                  <span className="ml-2 rounded bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">{p.visibility}</span>
                  <span className={`ml-2 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${p.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'bg-[var(--sf-success-bg)] text-[var(--sf-success)]' : 'bg-paper text-text-secondary'}`}>{p.status}</span>
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">{p.policy_type.replaceAll('_', ' ')}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <ActionButton
                  variant="stub"
                  size="small"
                  onClick={() => download(p.id, p.title)}
                  disabled={downloading === p.id}
                >
                  {downloading === p.id ? '…' : 'Download'}
                </ActionButton>
                {p.status !== 'ACTIVE' && <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => activate(p.id)}>Activate</ActionButton>}
                <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => remove(p.id)}>Delete</ActionButton>
              </div>
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>
    <Modal open={adding} title="Upload policy" onClose={() => setAdding(false)}>
      {adding && <UploadPolicy onDone={() => { setAdding(false); policies.reload() }} onCancel={() => setAdding(false)} />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function UploadPolicy({ onDone, onCancel }: { onDone: () => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('')
  const [policyType, setPolicyType] = useState<string>('SERVICE')
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState<'PUBLIC' | 'PRIVATE'>('PUBLIC')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending || !file || !title.trim()) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      const form = new FormData()
      form.append('title', title.trim())
      form.append('policy_type', policyType)
      form.append('visibility', visibility)
      form.append('file', file)
      await providerService.uploadPolicy(form)
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }
