  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <div>
      <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium">PDF file</label>
      <input type="file" accept="application/pdf" className="sf-input" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} />
    </div>
    <TextField label="Policy title" value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} required maxLength={200} />
    <SelectField label="Type" value={policyType} onChange={e => setPolicyType(e.target.value)}>
      {POLICY_TYPES.map(t => <option key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</option>)}
    </SelectField>
    <SelectField label="Visibility" value={visibility} onChange={e => setVisibility(e.target.value as 'PUBLIC' | 'PRIVATE')}>
      <option value="PUBLIC">Public — visible to clients and Flow AI</option>
      <option value="PRIVATE">Private — internal only</option>
    </SelectField>
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !file || !title.trim()}>
        {pending ? 'Uploading…' : 'Upload'}
      </ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}

// ---------- Settings ----------
function SettingsTab() {
  const profile = useResource(() => providerService.getProfile())
  return <Resource {...profile} retry={profile.reload}>
    {profile.data && <SettingsForm provider={profile.data} onSaved={profile.reload} />}
  </Resource>
}

function SettingsForm({ provider, onSaved }: { provider: Provider; onSaved: () => void }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(provider.name)
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(provider.description ?? '')
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(provider.phone ?? '')
  const [address, setAddress] = useState(provider.address ?? '')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError(''); setSaved(false)
    try {
      await providerService.updateProfile({ name, description, phone, address })
      setSaved(true); onSaved()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <SurfaceCard className="max-w-xl p-5">
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
      <TextField label="Business name" value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} required />
      <TextAreaField label="Description" value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
      <TextField label="Phone" value={phone} onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)} />
      <TextField label="Address" value={address} onChange={e => setAddress(e.target.value)} />
      {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
      {saved && <Notice>Profile saved.</Notice>}
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save profile'}</ActionButton>
    </form>
  </SurfaceCard>
}


=== src\features\manager\Pipeline.tsx ===

import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, EmptyState, SurfaceCard } from '../../components'
import { PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { JobRow, RequestRow } from '../../types/provider'

type Column = {
  key: string
  label: string
  cards: Card[]
}

type Card =
  | { kind: 'request'; id: number; label: string; status: string; sub: string }
  | { kind: 'job'; id: number; label: string; status: string; sub: string }

export default function Pipeline() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const data = useResource(async () => {
    const [requests, jobs] = await Promise.all([
      providerService.listIncomingRequests(),
      providerService.listJobs(),
    ])
    return { requests: requests.items, jobs: jobs.items }
  })

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Pipeline" description="Every request and job in one board." />
    <Resource {...data} retry={data.reload}>
      {data.data && <Board
        requests={data.data.requests}
        jobs={data.data.jobs}
        onOpen={(path) => navigate(path)}
      />}
    </Resource>
  </>
}

function Board({ requests, jobs, onOpen }: {
  requests: RequestRow[]
  jobs: JobRow[]
  onOpen: (path: string) => void
}) {
  const reqCard = (r: RequestRow): Card => ({
    kind: 'request', id: r.id, status: r.status,
    label: `Request #${r.id}`,
    sub: r.description.slice(0, 60) + (r.description.length > 60 ? '…' : ''),
  })
  const jobCard = (j: JobRow): Card => ({
    kind: 'job', id: j.id, status: j.status,
    label: `Job #${j.id}`,
    sub: j.schedule_date
      ? `${j.schedule_date}${j.schedule_time ? ` ${j.schedule_time.slice(0, 5)}` : ''}`
      : 'Not scheduled',
  })

  const columns: Column[] = [
    { key: 'NEW', label: 'New', cards: requests.filter(r => r.status === 'NEW').map(reqCard) },
    { key: 'UNDER_REVIEW', label: 'Under review', cards: requests.filter(r => r.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW').map(reqCard) },
    { key: 'NEED_INFO', label: 'Needs information', cards: requests.filter(r => r.status === 'NEED_INFORMATION').map(reqCard) },
    { key: 'QUOTED', label: 'Quotation sent', cards: requests.filter(r => r.status === 'QUOTATION_SENT').map(reqCard) },
    { key: 'JOB_CREATED', label: 'Accepted', cards: jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'JOB_CREATED').map(jobCard) },
    { key: 'SCHEDULED', label: 'Scheduled', cards: jobs.filter(j => ['SCHEDULE_PROPOSED', 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRMED'].includes(j.status)).map(jobCard) },
    { key: 'IN_PROGRESS', label: 'In progress', cards: jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'IN_PROGRESS').map(jobCard) },
    { key: 'COMPLETED', label: 'Completed', cards: jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'COMPLETED').map(jobCard) },
  ]

  const totalCards = columns.reduce((s, c) => s + c.cards.length, 0)
  if (totalCards === 0) {
    return <EmptyState
      title="Pipeline is empty"
      description="Requests and jobs will appear here as they move through the workflow."
    />
  }

  return <div className="overflow-x-auto pb-4">
    <div className="flex min-w-max gap-3">
      {columns.map(col => (
        <SurfaceCard key={col.key} className="w-72 shrink-0 space-y-2 p-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line pb-2">
            <h3 className="sf-mono text-xs font-semibold">{col.label}</h3>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-text-secondary">
              {col.cards.length}
            </span>
          </div>
          {col.cards.length === 0
            ? <p className="py-4 text-center text-xs text-text-secondary">Empty</p>
            : <div className="space-y-2">{col.cards.map(c => (
                <button
                  key={`${c.kind}-${c.id}`}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onOpen(c.kind === 'request'
                    ? `/manager/requests`
                    : `/manager/jobs`)}
                  className="w-full rounded-docket border border-line bg-white p-3 text-left transition hover:border-graphite"
                >
                  <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="sf-mono text-[11px] text-text-secondary">{c.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm line-clamp-2">{c.sub}</p>
                  <div className="mt-2"><RecordStatus status={c.status} /></div>
                </button>
              ))}</div>}
        </SurfaceCard>
      ))}
    </div>
  </div>
}


=== src\features\manager\PolicyPage.tsx ===

import { useState } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, StatusBadge, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField,
  FlowAIPanel, Skeleton, Tabs,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { api, ApiError } from '../../lib/api'
import { mockPolicyStatus, type PolicyStatus, type PolicyState } from '../../mocks/data'
import { Upload } from 'lucide-react'

const policyStatus = (s: PolicyState): 'awaiting' | 'scheduled' | 'approved' | 'declined' => {
  if (s === 'READY') return 'approved'
  if (s === 'PROCESSING') return 'scheduled'
  if (s === 'FAILED') return 'declined'
  return 'awaiting'
}

type AskResponse = {
  status?: string
  answer?: string
  citations?: Array<{ source: string; page: number }>
}

const askTabs = [
  { key: 'policy', label: 'Ask about policy' },
  { key: 'business', label: 'Ask about business' },
] as const
type AskTab = typeof askTabs[number]['key']

export default function PolicyPage() {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Flow AI"
        description="Ask questions about your policy or your business data. Scope is derived from your account."
      />
      <div className="max-w-2xl space-y-6">
        <PolicyUploadSection />
        <AskSection />
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function PolicyUploadSection() {
  const [policy, setPolicy] = useState<PolicyStatus>(mockPolicyStatus)
  const [uploadError, setUploadError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const handleUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
    if (!file) return
    setUploadError(null)
    if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
      setUploadError('Only PDF files are allowed.')
      return
    }
    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setUploadError('PDF must be 5 MB or smaller.')
      return
    }
    setPolicy({
      state: 'PROCESSING',
      filename: file.name,
      message: 'Uploading and extracting text...',
    })
    setTimeout(() => {
      setPolicy({
        state: 'READY',
        activeVersion: (policy.activeVersion ?? 0) + 1,
        filename: file.name,
        uploadedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      })
    }, 1500)
  }

  return (
    <SurfaceCard className="p-5 space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-semibold">Public policy PDF</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary mt-1">
            One active policy at a time. Uploading a new version keeps the current one active until indexing completes.
          </p>
        </div>
        <StatusBadge status={policyStatus(policy.state)} />
      </div>
      {policy.state === 'READY' && (
        <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm space-y-1">
          <p><span className="text-text-secondary">File:</span> <strong>{policy.filename}</strong></p>
          <p><span className="text-text-secondary">Version:</span> v{policy.activeVersion}</p>
          <p><span className="text-text-secondary">Uploaded:</span> {policy.uploadedAt}</p>
        </div>
      )}
      {policy.state === 'PROCESSING' && (
        <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
          <p className="text-text-secondary">{policy.message}</p>
          <div className="mt-2"><Skeleton rows={2} /></div>
        </div>
      )}
      {uploadError && (
        <div className="rounded-docket border border-[#B93832] bg-[#FDF2F2] p-3 text-sm text-[#B93832]">{uploadError}</div>
      )}
      <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-docket border border-line px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-paper">
        <Upload size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
        {policy.state === 'READY' ? 'Upload new version' : 'Upload PDF'}
        <input type="file" accept="application/pdf" className="hidden" onChange={handleUpload} disabled={policy.state === 'PROCESSING'} />
      </label>
      <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">Max 5 MB · Max 20 pages · Text-based PDF only</p>
    </SurfaceCard>
  )
}

function AskSection() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<AskTab>('policy')

  return (
    <SurfaceCard className="p-5 space-y-4">
      <h2 className="font-semibold">Ask Flow AI</h2>
      <Tabs label="Flow AI question types" items={askTabs} value={tab} onChange={setTab}>
        {tab === 'policy' && (
          <AskPanel
            endpoint="/ai/ask-docs"
            description="Answers come from your uploaded public policy document."
            placeholder="e.g. What is the arrival window for a standard appointment?"
          />
        )}
        {tab === 'business' && (
          <AskPanel
            endpoint="/ai/ask-data"
            description="Answers come from your own requests, quotations, jobs, and invoices."
            placeholder="e.g. How many service requests have I received this month?"
          />
        )}
      </Tabs>
    </SurfaceCard>
  )
}

function AskPanel({ endpoint, description, placeholder }: {
  endpoint: string
  description: string
  placeholder: string
}) {
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [response, setResponse] = useState<AskResponse | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function ask() {
    if (!question.trim() || loading) return
    setLoading(true); setError(''); setResponse(null)
    try {
      const result = await api<AskResponse>(
        endpoint,
        'POST',
        { question: question.trim() },
        { timeout: 120000 },
      )
      setResponse(result)
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof ApiError) {
        if (e.status === 502 || e.status === 503) setError('Flow AI is unavailable right now. Please try again.')
        else if (e.status === 403) setError('Flow AI is only available to provider accounts.')
        else if (e.status === 0) setError('Flow AI timed out. Check your connection or try a shorter question.')
        else setError(e.message)
      } else {
        setError('Something went wrong. Please try again.')
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const displayAnswer = loading
    ? undefined
    : response?.status === 'insufficient_evidence'
      ? (response.answer || 'The available data does not provide enough evidence to answer this question.')
      : response?.answer

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 pt-3">
      <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">{description}</p>
      <TextAreaField
        label="Your question"
        value={question}
        onChange={e => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        onKeyDown={(e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
            e.preventDefault(); ask()
          }
        }}
      />
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <span className="text-xs text-text-secondary">{loading ? 'Usually takes 4–10 seconds' : ''}</span>
        <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={ask} disabled={loading || !question.trim()}>
          {loading ? 'Thinking…' : 'Ask'}
        </ActionButton>
      </div>
      {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
      {(loading || response) && (
        <FlowAIPanel
          question={question}
          answer={displayAnswer}
          citations={response?.citations}
          loading={loading}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


=== src\features\manager\QuoteBuilder.tsx ===

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  ActionButton, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import {
  Notice, PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, useResource,
} from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { QuotationItem, QuotationRow, RequestRow } from '../../types/provider'

export default function QuoteBuilder() {
  const { requestId } = useParams<{ requestId: string }>()
  const id = Number(requestId)
  if (!id || Number.isNaN(id)) return <p className="p-6">Invalid request.</p>

  const request = useResource(() => providerService.getRequest(id), [id])
  const quotations = useResource(() => providerService.listQuotations(id), [id])

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Build quotation" description={`For request #${id}`}>
      <Link to="/manager/requests" className="sf-link">← Back to requests</Link>
    </PageHeader>

    <Resource {...request} retry={request.reload}>
      {request.data && <RequestSummary request={request.data} />}
    </Resource>

    <div className="mt-6">
      <Resource {...quotations} retry={quotations.reload}>
        <QuoteSection
          requestId={id}
          quotations={quotations.data ?? []}
          onChanged={quotations.reload}
        />
      </Resource>
    </div>
  </>
}

function RequestSummary({ request }: { request: RequestRow }) {
  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-2 p-5">
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <RecordStatus status={request.status} />
      <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">#{request.id}</span>
    </div>
    <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{request.description}</p>
    <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
      Preferred {request.preferred_date}
      {request.preferred_time ? ` ${request.preferred_time.slice(0, 5)}` : ''} · {request.address}
    </p>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

function QuoteSection({ requestId, quotations, onChanged }: {
  requestId: number
  quotations: QuotationRow[]
  onChanged: () => void
}) {
  const draft = quotations.find(q => q.status === 'DRAFT')
  const sent = quotations.find(q => q.status === 'SENT' || q.status === 'APPROVED' || q.status === 'DECLINED')

  if (sent) return <SentQuotation quotation={sent} />
  return <QuotationForm requestId={requestId} existingDraft={draft} onSaved={onChanged} />
}

function SentQuotation({ quotation }: { quotation: QuotationRow }) {
  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-3 p-5">
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
      <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Quotation #{quotation.id}</h2>
      <RecordStatus status={quotation.status} />
    </div>
    <ItemTable items={quotation.items} />
    <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-line pt-3">
      <span className="sf-mono text-sm">TOTAL</span>
      <strong className="sf-tabular text-lg">LKR {Number(quotation.total_amount).toFixed(2)}</strong>
    </div>
    <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
      Valid until {new Date(quotation.valid_until).toLocaleString('en-GB')}
    </p>
    {quotation.note && <p className="text-sm">{quotation.note}</p>}
    <Notice>
      {quotation.status === 'SENT'
        ? 'Sent — waiting for the client to approve or decline.'
        : quotation.status === 'APPROVED'
        ? 'Approved by the client. A job has been created.'
        : 'Declined by the client.'}
    </Notice>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

function ItemTable({ items }: { items: QuotationRow['items'] }) {
  return <div className="overflow-x-auto">
    <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
      <thead><tr className="sf-mono border-b border-border text-xs">
        <th className="py-2">Description</th>
        <th className="px-2 py-2 text-right">Qty</th>
        <th className="px-2 py-2 text-right">Unit price</th>
        <th className="py-2 text-right">Line total</th>
      </tr></thead>
      <tbody>{items.map(i => <tr key={i.id} className="border-b border-line">
        <td className="py-2">{i.description}</td>
        <td className="sf-tabular px-2 text-right">{i.quantity}</td>
        <td className="sf-tabular px-2 text-right">{Number(i.unit_price).toFixed(2)}</td>
        <td className="sf-tabular text-right">{Number(i.line_total).toFixed(2)}</td>
      </tr>)}</tbody>
    </table>
  </div>
}

type FormItem = { description: string; quantity: string; unit_price: string }

function QuotationForm({ requestId, existingDraft, onSaved }: {
  requestId: number
  existingDraft?: QuotationRow
  onSaved: () => void
}) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<FormItem[]>(() =>
    existingDraft
      ? existingDraft.items.map(i => ({ description: i.description, quantity: i.quantity, unit_price: i.unit_price }))
      : [{ description: '', quantity: '1', unit_price: '0' }]
  )
  const [validUntil, setValidUntil] = useState(() =>
    existingDraft
      ? toLocalInput(existingDraft.valid_until)
      : toLocalInput(new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 86400000).toISOString())
  )
  const [downpayment, setDownpayment] = useState(existingDraft?.downpayment_amount ?? '')
  const [note, setNote] = useState(existingDraft?.note ?? '')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<'draft' | 'send' | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')

  const total = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + Number(i.quantity || 0) * Number(i.unit_price || 0), 0)

  function updateItem(i: number, patch: Partial<FormItem>) {
    setItems(prev => prev.map((it, idx) => idx === i ? { ...it, ...patch } : it))
  }
  function addItem() { setItems(prev => [...prev, { description: '', quantity: '1', unit_price: '0' }]) }
  function removeItem(i: number) { setItems(prev => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i)) }

  function validate(): string | null {
    if (items.length === 0) return 'Add at least one line item.'
    for (const it of items) {
      if (!it.description.trim()) return 'Every line needs a description.'
      if (Number(it.quantity) <= 0) return 'Quantity must be greater than 0.'
      if (Number(it.unit_price) < 0) return 'Unit price cannot be negative.'
    }
    if (!validUntil) return 'Set a validity date.'
    if (downpayment && Number(downpayment) < 0) return 'Downpayment cannot be negative.'
    return null
  }

  async function save(send: boolean) {
    const v = validate()
    if (v) { setError(v); return }
    setPending(send ? 'send' : 'draft'); setError(''); setSuccess('')

    const lineItems: QuotationItem[] = items.map(i => ({
      description: i.description.trim(),
      quantity: Number(i.quantity),
      unit_price: Number(i.unit_price),
    }))
    const payload = {
      items: lineItems,
      valid_until: new Date(validUntil).toISOString(),
      downpayment_amount: downpayment ? Number(downpayment) : undefined,
      note: note.trim() || undefined,
    }

    try {
      const q = existingDraft
        ? await providerService.updateQuotation(existingDraft.id, payload)
        : await providerService.createQuotation(requestId, payload)

      if (send) {
        await providerService.sendQuotation(q.id)
        setSuccess('Quotation sent to client.')
      } else {
        setSuccess('Draft saved.')
      }
      onSaved()
    } catch (c) {
      setError((c as Error).message)
    } finally {
      setPending(null)
    }
  }

  return <SurfaceCard className="space-y-4 p-5">
    <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">
      {existingDraft ? `Edit draft quotation #${existingDraft.id}` : 'Create quotation'}
    </h2>

    <div className="space-y-2">
      <h3 className="font-semibold">Line items</h3>
      {items.map((it, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-12 items-start gap-2">
          <div className="col-span-6">
            <TextField
              label={i === 0 ? 'Description' : ''}
              value={it.description}
              onChange={e => updateItem(i, { description: e.target.value })}
              required
            />
          </div>
          <div className="col-span-2">
            <TextField
              label={i === 0 ? 'Qty' : ''}
              type="number" min={0} step="0.01"
              value={it.quantity}
              onChange={e => updateItem(i, { quantity: e.target.value })}
              required
            />
          </div>
          <div className="col-span-3">
            <TextField
              label={i === 0 ? 'Unit price' : ''}
              type="number" min={0} step="0.01"
              value={it.unit_price}
              onChange={e => updateItem(i, { unit_price: e.target.value })}
              required
            />
          </div>
          <div className={`col-span-1 flex ${i === 0 ? 'pt-7' : 'pt-1'}`}>
            {items.length > 1 && (
              <ActionButton variant="void" size="small" onClick={() => removeItem(i)} aria-label="Remove line">
                ×
              </ActionButton>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
      <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={addItem}>+ Add line</ActionButton>
    </div>

    <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-line pt-3">
      <span className="sf-mono text-sm">TOTAL</span>
      <strong className="sf-tabular text-lg">LKR {total.toFixed(2)}</strong>
    </div>

    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
      <TextField
        label="Valid until"
        type="datetime-local"
        value={validUntil}
        onChange={e => setValidUntil(e.target.value)}
        required
      />
      <TextField
        label="Downpayment (optional)"
        type="number" min={0} step="0.01"
        value={downpayment}
        onChange={e => setDownpayment(e.target.value)}
      />
    </div>

    <TextAreaField label="Note (optional)" value={note} onChange={e => setNote(e.target.value)} />

    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    {success && <Notice>{success}</Notice>}

    <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton onClick={() => save(false)} disabled={!!pending}>
        {pending === 'draft' ? 'Saving…' : 'Save draft'}
      </ActionButton>
      <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => save(true)} disabled={!!pending}>
        {pending === 'send' ? 'Sending…' : 'Send to client'}
      </ActionButton>
    </div>
  </SurfaceCard>
}

function toLocalInput(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso)
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`
}


=== src\features\manager\Requests.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SelectField, SurfaceCard, TextAreaField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, dateTime, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { RequestAttachmentRow, RequestMessage } from '../../types/provider'

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { value: '', label: 'All statuses' },
  { value: 'NEW', label: 'New' },
  { value: 'UNDER_REVIEW', label: 'Under review' },
  { value: 'NEED_INFORMATION', label: 'Needs information' },
  { value: 'QUOTATION_SENT', label: 'Quotation sent' },
  { value: 'REJECTED', label: 'Rejected' },
  { value: 'CANCELLED', label: 'Cancelled' },
]

export default function RequestInbox() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('')
  const resource = useResource(() => providerService.listIncomingRequests(status || undefined), [status])
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Requests" description="Customer requests addressed to your business." />
    <div className="mb-5 max-w-xs">
      <SelectField label="Filter by status" value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
        {STATUS_OPTIONS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
      </SelectField>
    </div>
    <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
      {resource.data && resource.data.items.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No requests" description={status ? 'No requests with this status.' : 'New customer requests will appear here.'} />
        : <div className="space-y-3">
            {resource.data?.items.map(r => (
              <SurfaceCard key={r.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="mb-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">#{r.id}</span>
                    <RecordStatus status={r.status} />
                  </div>
                  <p className="line-clamp-1">{r.description}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-secondary">
                    Preferred {r.preferred_date}{r.preferred_time ? ` ${r.preferred_time.slice(0, 5)}` : ''} · {r.address}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setOpenId(r.id)}>Open</ActionButton>
              </SurfaceCard>
            ))}
          </div>}
    </Resource>
    <Modal open={openId !== null} title="Request details" onClose={() => setOpenId(null)}>
      {openId !== null && <RequestDetail id={openId} onClose={() => { setOpenId(null); resource.reload() }} />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function RequestDetail({ id, onClose }: { id: number; onClose: () => void }) {
  const req = useResource(() => providerService.getRequest(id), [id])
  const msgs = useResource(() => providerService.listMessages(id), [id])
  const atts = useResource(() => providerService.listRequestAttachments(id).catch(() => [] as RequestAttachmentRow[]), [id])
  const [action, setAction] = useState<'review' | 'info' | 'reject' | null>(null)
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [messageText, setMessageText] = useState('')
  const [sendingMsg, setSendingMsg] = useState(false)

  async function runAction() {
    if (pending || !action) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      if (action === 'review') await providerService.reviewRequest(id)
      else if (action === 'info') await providerService.requestInformation(id, text.trim())
      else if (action === 'reject') await providerService.rejectRequest(id, text.trim())
      setAction(null); setText(''); req.reload()
      if (action === 'reject') onClose()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  async function sendMsg(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (sendingMsg || !messageText.trim()) return
    setSendingMsg(true); setError('')
    try { await providerService.sendMessage(id, messageText.trim()); setMessageText(''); msgs.reload() }
    catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setSendingMsg(false) }
  }

  return <Resource {...req} retry={req.reload}>
    {req.data && <>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <RecordStatus status={req.data.status} />
          <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">#{req.data.id}</span>
        </div>
        <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{req.data.description}</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div><span className="text-text-secondary">Preferred date:</span> {req.data.preferred_date}</div>
          {req.data.preferred_time && <div><span className="text-text-secondary">Preferred time:</span> {req.data.preferred_time.slice(0, 5)}</div>}
          <div className="sm:col-span-2"><span className="text-text-secondary">Address:</span> {req.data.address}</div>
        </div>
        {req.data.rejection_reason && <Notice error>Rejected: {req.data.rejection_reason}</Notice>}
        {req.data.cancellation_reason && <Notice>Cancelled: {req.data.cancellation_reason}</Notice>}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-t border-line pt-4">
        {req.data.status === 'NEW' && <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setAction('review')}>Start review</ActionButton>}
        {['NEW', 'UNDER_REVIEW', 'NEED_INFORMATION'].includes(req.data.status) && <>
          <ActionButton onClick={() => { setAction('info'); setText('') }}>Ask for information</ActionButton>
          <ActionButton variant="void" onClick={() => { setAction('reject'); setText('') }}>Reject</ActionButton>
          <Link to={`/manager/quotations/${id}`} className="sf-action inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-docket sf-stamp min-h-11 px-5 text-sm font-semibold">Create quotation</Link>
        </>}
        {req.data.status === 'QUOTATION_SENT' && <Notice>Quotation sent — waiting for client approval.</Notice>}
      </div>

      {action && <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 space-y-3 p-4">
        <h3 className="font-semibold">{action === 'review' ? 'Start review' : action === 'info' ? 'Ask for more information' : 'Reject this request'}</h3>
        {action !== 'review' && <TextAreaField
          label={action === 'info' ? 'Message to client' : 'Reason for rejection'}
          value={text}
          onChange={e => setText(e.target.value)}
          required
        />}
        {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <ActionButton onClick={() => { setAction(null); setError('') }}>Cancel</ActionButton>
          <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={runAction} disabled={pending || (action !== 'review' && !text.trim())}>
            {pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Confirm'}
          </ActionButton>
        </div>
      </SurfaceCard>}

      <div className="mt-6 border-t border-line pt-4">
        <h3 className="mb-3 font-semibold">Attachments</h3>
        <Resource {...atts} retry={atts.reload}>
          {atts.data && atts.data.length === 0
            ? <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">No attachments from the client.</p>
            : <div className="space-y-2">{atts.data?.map(a => (
                <div key={a.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate font-medium">{a.file_name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">{a.content_type} · {dateTime(a.created_at)}</p>
                  </div>
                  <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => providerService.downloadRequestAttachment(id, a.id, a.file_name)}>
                    Download
                  </ActionButton>
                </div>
              ))}</div>}
        </Resource>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 border-t border-line pt-4">
        <h3 className="mb-3 font-semibold">Conversation</h3>
        <Resource {...msgs} retry={msgs.reload}>
          {msgs.data && msgs.data.length === 0
            ? <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">No messages yet.</p>
            : <div className="space-y-2">{msgs.data?.map((m: RequestMessage) => (
                <div key={m.id} className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
                  <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between text-xs text-text-secondary">
                    <span>User #{m.sender_user_id}</span>
                    <span>{dateTime(m.created_at)}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{m.message_text}</p>
                </div>
              ))}</div>}
        </Resource>
        <form onSubmit={sendMsg} className="mt-3 space-y-2">
          <TextAreaField label="Reply" value={messageText} onChange={e => setMessageText(e.target.value)} />
          {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={sendingMsg || !messageText.trim()}>
              {sendingMsg ? 'Sending…' : 'Send message'}
            </ActionButton>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </>}
  </Resource>
}


=== src\features\manager\Schedule.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SelectField, SurfaceCard,
  Tabs, TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, RecordStatus, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { EmployeeRow, JobRow } from '../../types/provider'

const tabs = [
  { key: 'needs', label: 'Needs scheduling' },
  { key: 'pending', label: 'Awaiting client' },
  { key: 'confirmed', label: 'Confirmed' },
] as const
type Tab = typeof tabs[number]['key']

const scheduleStatusMap: Record<Tab, string> = {
  needs: 'NOT_PROPOSED',
  pending: 'PENDING',
  confirmed: 'CONFIRMED',
}

export default function ManagerSchedule() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('needs')
  const jobs = useResource(
    () => providerService.listJobs({ schedule_status: scheduleStatusMap[tab] }),
    [tab],
  )
  const employees = useResource((): Promise<EmployeeRow[]> =>
    providerService.listEmployees().catch(() => [] as EmployeeRow[]),
  )
  const [openJobId, setOpenJobId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Schedule" description="Propose appointment times and assign employees." />
    <Tabs label="Schedule views" items={tabs} value={tab} onChange={setTab}>
      <Resource {...jobs} retry={jobs.reload}>
        {jobs.data && (jobs.data.items.length === 0
          ? <EmptyState
              title="Nothing here"
              description={
                tab === 'needs' ? 'All jobs are scheduled.' :
                tab === 'pending' ? 'No schedules waiting for the client.' :
                'No confirmed appointments yet.'
              }
            />
          : <div className="space-y-3">{jobs.data.items.map((j: JobRow) => (
              <SurfaceCard key={j.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Job #{j.id}</span>
                    <RecordStatus status={j.status} />
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                    Request #{j.request_id}
                    {j.schedule_date && ` · ${j.schedule_date} ${j.schedule_time?.slice(0, 5) ?? ''}`}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
                    {j.assigned_user_id ? `Assigned to employee #${j.assigned_user_id}` : 'Not assigned'}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setOpenJobId(j.id)}>Manage</ActionButton>
              </SurfaceCard>
            ))}</div>
        )}
      </Resource>
    </Tabs>
    <Modal open={openJobId !== null} title={`Job #${openJobId ?? ''}`} onClose={() => setOpenJobId(null)}>
      {openJobId !== null && <JobScheduler
        jobId={openJobId}
        employees={employees.data ?? []}
        onChanged={() => { jobs.reload(); employees.reload() }}
        onClose={() => setOpenJobId(null)}
      />}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function JobScheduler({ jobId, employees, onChanged, onClose }: {
  jobId: number
  employees: EmployeeRow[]
  onChanged: () => void
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const job = useResource(() => providerService.getJob(jobId), [jobId])
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<'assign' | 'schedule' | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [employeeId, setEmployeeId] = useState('')
  const [date, setDate] = useState('')
  const [time, setTime] = useState('09:00')

  async function assign() {
    if (!employeeId) return
    setPending('assign'); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.assignEmployee(jobId, Number(employeeId))
      setEmployeeId(''); job.reload(); onChanged()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }

  async function propose(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!date || !time) return
    setPending('schedule'); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.proposeSchedule(jobId, date, `${time}:00`)
      job.reload(); onChanged()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(null) }
  }

  return <Resource {...job} retry={job.reload}>
    {job.data && (() => {
      const isReassign = !!job.data.assigned_user_id
      const canAssign = employees.length > 0 &&
        ['JOB_CREATED', 'SCHEDULE_PROPOSED'].includes(job.data.status)
      return <>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <RecordStatus status={job.data.status} />
            <span className="sf-mono text-xs text-text-secondary">Schedule: {job.data.schedule_status}</span>
          </div>
          {job.data.schedule_date && (
            <p className="text-sm">
              Proposed: <strong>{job.data.schedule_date} at {job.data.schedule_time?.slice(0, 5)}</strong>
            </p>
          )}
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
            {job.data.assigned_user_id ? `Assigned to employee #${job.data.assigned_user_id}` : 'No employee assigned'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {error && <div className="mt-3"><Notice error>{error}</Notice></div>}

        {canAssign && (
          <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 space-y-3 p-4">
            <h3 className="font-semibold">{isReassign ? 'Reassign employee' : 'Assign employee'}</h3>
            {isReassign && (
              <Notice>Changing the employee may reset the proposed schedule. The backend will validate.</Notice>
            )}
            <SelectField label="Employee" value={employeeId} onChange={e => setEmployeeId(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">— {isReassign ? 'Pick a new employee' : 'Select'} —</option>
              {employees.filter(e => e.status === 'ACTIVE').map(e => (
                <option key={e.id} value={e.id}>{e.name}</option>
              ))}
            </SelectField>
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={assign} disabled={!employeeId || pending === 'assign'}>
                {pending === 'assign' ? 'Assigning…' : (isReassign ? 'Reassign' : 'Assign')}
              </ActionButton>
            </div>
          </SurfaceCard>
        )}

        {job.data.schedule_status === 'NOT_PROPOSED' && job.data.status === 'JOB_CREATED' && (
          <SurfaceCard className="mt-4 space-y-3 p-4">
            <h3 className="font-semibold">Propose schedule</h3>
            <form onSubmit={propose} className="space-y-3">
              <TextField label="Date" type="date" value={date} onChange={e => setDate(e.target.value)} required />
              <TextField label="Time" type="time" value={time} onChange={e => setTime(e.target.value)} required />
              <div className="flex justify-end">
                <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={!date || !time || pending === 'schedule'}>
                  {pending === 'schedule' ? 'Proposing…' : 'Propose schedule'}
                </ActionButton>
              </div>
            </form>
          </SurfaceCard>
        )}

        {job.data.schedule_status === 'PENDING' && (
          <div className="mt-4"><Notice>Schedule proposed — waiting for the client to approve.</Notice></div>
        )}
        {job.data.schedule_status === 'CONFIRMED' && (
          <div className="mt-4"><Notice>Schedule confirmed. The employee can start the job on the scheduled date.</Notice></div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end">
          <ActionButton onClick={onClose}>Close</ActionButton>
        </div>
      </>
    })()}
  </Resource>
}


=== src\features\manager\Team.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  ActionButton, EmptyState, Modal, SelectField, SurfaceCard,
  TextField,
} from '../../components'
import { Notice, PageHeader, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { providerService } from '../../lib/provider-service'
import type { EmployeeRow } from '../../types/provider'

export default function ManagerTeam() {
  const employees = useResource(() => providerService.listEmployees().catch(() => [] as EmployeeRow[]))
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<EmployeeRow | null>(null)
  const [revealedPassword, setRevealedPassword] = useState<{ email: string; password: string } | null>(null)

  return <>
    <PageHeader title="Workers" description="Company employees who can be assigned to jobs.">
      <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>+ Add employee</ActionButton>
    </PageHeader>

    <Resource {...employees} retry={employees.reload}>
      {employees.data && employees.data.length === 0
        ? <EmptyState title="No employees yet" description="Add your first employee to start assigning jobs." />
        : <div className="space-y-2">{employees.data?.map(e => (
            <SurfaceCard key={e.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="sf-display font-semibold">{e.name}</h3>
                  <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${
                    e.status === 'ACTIVE'
                      ? 'bg-[var(--sf-success-bg)] text-[var(--sf-success)]'
                      : 'bg-paper text-text-secondary'
                  }`}>{e.status}</span>
                  {e.must_change_password && (
                    <span className="rounded bg-[var(--sf-warning-bg)] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--sf-warning)]">
                      First login pending
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">{e.email} · {e.phone}</p>
              </div>
              <ActionButton variant="stub" size="small" onClick={() => setEditing(e)}>Manage</ActionButton>
            </SurfaceCard>
          ))}</div>}
    </Resource>

    <Modal open={adding} title="Add employee" onClose={() => setAdding(false)}>
      {adding && <AddEmployee
        onDone={(created) => {
          setAdding(false)
          setRevealedPassword({ email: created.email, password: created.initial_password })
          employees.reload()
        }}
        onCancel={() => setAdding(false)}
      />}
    </Modal>

    <Modal open={!!editing} title="Manage employee" onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
      {editing && <EditEmployee
        employee={editing}
        onDone={() => { setEditing(null); employees.reload() }}
        onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
      />}
    </Modal>

    <Modal open={!!revealedPassword} title="Employee account created" onClose={() => setRevealedPassword(null)}>
      {revealedPassword && <>
        <Notice>
          Share these credentials with the employee. The password <strong>will not be shown again</strong> —
          the employee must change it on first login.
        </Notice>
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-2 rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-4 text-sm">
          <div><span className="text-text-secondary">Email:</span> <strong>{revealedPassword.email}</strong></div>
          <div><span className="text-text-secondary">Initial password:</span> <strong className="sf-mono">{revealedPassword.password}</strong></div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
          <ActionButton variant="stamp" onClick={() => setRevealedPassword(null)}>Done</ActionButton>
        </div>
      </>}
    </Modal>
  </>
}

function AddEmployee({ onDone, onCancel }: {
  onDone: (created: { email: string; initial_password: string }) => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      const created = await providerService.createEmployee({
        name: name.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        phone: phone.trim(),
      })
      onDone({ email: created.email, initial_password: created.initial_password })
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <TextField label="Full name" value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} required minLength={2} maxLength={150} />
    <TextField label="Email" type="email" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} required />
    <TextField label="Phone" type="tel" value={phone} onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)} required minLength={7} maxLength={30} />
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !name.trim() || !email.trim() || !phone.trim()}>
        {pending ? 'Creating…' : 'Create employee'}
      </ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}

function EditEmployee({ employee, onDone, onCancel }: {
  employee: EmployeeRow
  onDone: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
}) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(employee.email)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE'>(employee.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (pending) return
    setPending(true); setError('')
    try {
      await providerService.updateEmployee(employee.id, {
        email: email.trim() !== employee.email ? email.trim() : undefined,
        status: status !== employee.status ? status : undefined,
      })
      onDone()
    } catch (c) { setError((c as Error).message) } finally { setPending(false) }
  }

  return <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
    <div className="rounded-docket border border-line bg-paper p-3 text-sm">
      <p className="font-semibold">{employee.name}</p>
      <p className="text-text-secondary">{employee.phone}</p>
    </div>
    <TextField label="Email" type="email" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} required />
    <SelectField label="Status" value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value as 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE')}>
      <option value="ACTIVE">Active — can be assigned jobs</option>
      <option value="INACTIVE">Inactive — cannot log in</option>
    </SelectField>
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
      <ActionButton type="button" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending}>{pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save changes'}</ActionButton>
    </div>
  </form>
}


=== src\features\marketplace\ClientFlowAI.tsx ===

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ActionButton, FlowAIPanel, TextAreaField } from '../../components'
import { Notice } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { useSession } from '../../app/session'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'
import type { PolicyAnswer } from '../../types/client'

export function ClientFlowAI({ companyId }: { companyId: string }) {
  const { user } = useSession()
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('')
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState('')
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState<PolicyAnswer>()
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  async function ask(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending || !question.trim()) return
    setPending(true)
    setError('')
    setAnswer(undefined)
    setSubmitted(question.trim())
    try { setAnswer(await clientService.ask(companyId, question.trim())) }
    catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message) }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  const cited = answer?.status === 'ANSWERED' && answer.citations.length > 0
  return <div className="space-y-5">
    <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Ask Flow AI</h2>
    <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">Answers come from the selected company’s public policy PDF and include policy page citations.</p>
    {user?.role === 'client' ? <form onSubmit={ask} className="space-y-4">
      <TextAreaField label="Your policy question" required maxLength={1000} value={question} onChange={event => setQuestion(event.target.value)} />
      <ActionButton type="submit" variant="stamp" disabled={pending || !question.trim()}>{pending ? 'Checking policy…' : 'Ask question'}</ActionButton>
    </form> : <Link className="sf-link" to="/login">Sign in as a client to ask →</Link>}
    {error && <Notice error>{error}</Notice>}
    {pending && <FlowAIPanel question={submitted} loading />}
    {answer && <>
      <FlowAIPanel question={submitted} answer={answer.status === 'ANSWERED' && !cited ? 'The policy response did not include evidence. Please try again or open the public policy.' : answer.answer} source={cited ? answer.citations[0].title : undefined} page={cited ? answer.citations[0].page : undefined} />
      {cited && answer.citations.map(citation => <PolicyCitation key={`${citation.policy_id}-${citation.page}`} citation={citation} />)}
    </>}
  </div>
}

function PolicyCitation({ citation }: { citation: PolicyAnswer['citations'][number] }) {
  let url: URL
  try { url = new URL(citation.url, window.location.origin) }
  catch { return <Notice error>Policy citation link is unavailable.</Notice> }
  if (!['https:', 'http:'].includes(url.protocol) || !Number.isInteger(citation.page) || citation.page < 1) return <Notice error>Policy citation link is unavailable.</Notice>
  url.hash = `page=${citation.page}`
  return <a className="sf-link block text-sm" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href={url.href}>{citation.title} · Version {citation.policy_version} · Page {citation.page} ↗</a>
}



=== src\features\marketplace\DirectoryPage.tsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ArrowDown, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight, BriefcaseBusiness, Building2, Check, CheckCheck, ClipboardList, Clock3, FileText, MapPin, Search, ShieldCheck, SlidersHorizontal, Sparkles, Wrench, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { ActionButton, EmptyState, SelectField, TextField } from '../../components'

export function DirectoryPage() {

  const location = useLocation()
  useEffect(() => { if (location.hash) requestAnimationFrame(() => document.getElementById(location.hash.slice(1))?.scrollIntoView()) }, [location.hash])
  const companies = resource.data ?? []
  const services = [...new Set(companies.flatMap(company => company.offerings.filter(offer => offer.active).map(offer => offer.name)))]
  const minimum = (offers: typeof companies[number]['offerings']) => Math.min(...offers.filter(offer => offer.active).map(offer => offer.starting_price))
  const filtered = companies.filter(company => `${company.name} ${company.location} ${company.category} ${company.offerings.map(offer => offer.name).join(' ')}`.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase().trim()) && (!service || company.offerings.some(offer => offer.active && offer.name === service))).sort((a, b) => sort === 'price' ? minimum(a.offerings) - minimum(b.offerings) : a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
  function clear() { setSearch(''); setService(''); setSort('name') }
  return <>
    <section className="sf-discovery-hero">
      <div className="sf-discovery-copy"><p className="sf-eyebrow"><span />A LITTLE LESS HASSLE. A LOT MORE CLARITY.</p><h1>Good service.<br />From people who<br /><span className="sf-marker">get it done.</span></h1><p className="sf-hero-description">Find the right company for your home. Agree a price, confirm a time and follow every step—without the back-and-forth.</p><div className="sf-hero-buttons"><a href="#directory" className="sf-primary-link">Find your service<ArrowUpRight size={18} /></a><a href="#how-it-works" className="sf-secondary-link">See how it works<ArrowDown size={16} /></a></div><div className="sf-hero-reassurance"><span><Check size={15} />Approve the price first</span><span><Check size={15} />Stay in control</span></div></div>
      <div className="sf-service-scene" aria-label="Illustration of a service request workflow"><div className="sf-scene-caption"><span className="sf-mono">LESS CHASING. MORE LIVING.</span><ArrowUpRight size={20} /></div><div className="sf-scene-ticket"><div className="sf-ticket-top"><span className="sf-ticket-icon"><Wrench size={25} /></span><span><strong>Your home, sorted.</strong><small>A clear plan for every service</small></span><span className="sf-ticket-corner"><Check size={15} /></span></div><div className="sf-ticket-line"><span>01</span><div><strong>The right company</strong><small>Explore services in your area</small></div><Building2 size={20} /></div><div className="sf-ticket-line"><span>02</span><div><strong>A price you approve</strong><small>No work before your go-ahead</small></div><FileText size={20} /></div><div className="sf-ticket-line"><span>03</span><div><strong>An appointment that fits</strong><small>Confirm your proposed time</small></div><Clock3 size={20} /></div><div className="sf-ticket-stub"><div><span className="sf-mono">FROM REQUEST TO RECEIPT</span><strong>Every detail. All together.</strong></div><div className="sf-barcode" aria-hidden="true" /></div></div><div className="sf-scene-stamp"><CheckCheck size={18} /><span>YOUR NEXT STEP?<strong>ALWAYS CLEAR.</strong></span></div></div>
    </section>
    <div className="sf-benefit-strip"><span><ShieldCheck size={19} />Your approval at every step</span><span><ClipboardList size={19} />One place for every detail</span><span><BriefcaseBusiness size={19} />Company teams you can browse</span></div>
    <section id="directory" className="sf-directory-section"><div className="sf-section-heading"><div><p className="sf-eyebrow">FIND YOUR NEXT HELPING HAND</p><h2>Find the right team</h2><p>Explore companies. Compare services. Start with a request.</p></div><span className="sf-directory-note">Starting prices shown.<br />Your quotation confirms the total.</span></div>
      <div className="sf-directory-controls"><div className="sf-search-field"><Search size={19} aria-hidden="true" /><TextField label="Search companies or locations" type="search" value={search} onChange={event => setSearch(event.target.value)} placeholder="Company, service or location…" /></div><SelectField label="Service" value={service} onChange={event => setService(event.target.value)}><option value="">All services</option>{services.map(value => <option key={value}>{value}</option>)}</SelectField><SelectField label="Sort by" value={sort} onChange={event => setSort(event.target.value)}><option value="name">Company name</option><option value="price">Starting price: low to high</option></SelectField></div>
      <div className="sf-service-filters" aria-label="Quick service filters"><SlidersHorizontal size={16} aria-hidden="true" /><button type="button" aria-pressed={!service} onClick={() => setService('')}>All services</button>{services.map(value => <button key={value} type="button" aria-pressed={service === value} onClick={() => setService(value)}>{value}</button>)}</div>
      <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}><div className="sf-directory-count"><p><strong>{filtered.length}</strong> {filtered.length === 1 ? 'company' : 'companies'}{demoMode ? import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { EmptyState, SurfaceCard } from '../../components'
import { money, PageHeader, Resource, useResource } from '../../components/client-shared'
import { clientService } from '../../lib/client-service'
import { demoMode } from '../../lib/api'
import { ClientFlowAI } from './ClientFlowAI'
import { PublicFeedback } from './PublicFeedback'
export { NewRequestPage } from './RequestForm'

export function CompanyPage() {
  const { id = '' } = useParams()
  const resource = useResource(() => clientService.company(id), [id])
  const policy = useResource(() => clientService.policy(id), [id])
  const company = resource.data
  return <Resource {...resource} retry={resource.reload}>
    {company && <>
      <Link to="/companies" className="sf-link mb-6 inline-block">← All companies</Link>
      <PageHeader eyebrow={company.category} title={company.name} description={company.description} />
      <div className="grid items-start gap-7 lg:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr]">
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <SurfaceCard className="p-6">
            <h2 className="sf-display mb-4 text-xl font-semibold">Services</h2>
            {company.offerings.filter(offering => offering.active).map(offering => <div key={offering.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-line py-5">
              <div><h3 className="font-semibold">{offering.name}</h3><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-secondary">Starting from {money(offering.starting_price)}</p></div>
              <Link className="sf-link" to={`/companies/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/request?service=${encodeURIComponent(offering.id)}`}>Request service →</Link>
            </div>)}
            {!company.offerings.some(offering => offering.active) && <EmptyState title="No active services yet" description="This provider has not published any services. Ask the provider manager to add an active offering before sending a request." />}
          </SurfaceCard>
          <SurfaceCard className="space-y-4 p-6">
            <h2 className="sf-display text-xl font-semibold">Company details</h2>
            <p>{company.location}</p>
            <a className="sf-link block" href={`tel:${company.phone.replace(/[^+\d]/g, '')}`}>{company.phone}</a>
            <a className="sf-link block break-all" href={`mailto:${company.email}`}>{company.email}</a>
            <h3 className="font-semibold">Focus areas</h3>
            <ul className="list-inside list-disc text-sm text-text-secondary">
              {(company.focus_areas || [company.category]).map(area => <li key={area}>{area}</li>)}
            </ul>
            {company.completed_jobs !== undefined && <p className="font-semibold">{company.completed_jobs} completed {demoMode ? 'sample ' : ''}job(s)</p>}
          </SurfaceCard>
          <PublicFeedback companyId={id} />
        </div>
        <aside className="space-y-5">
          <SurfaceCard className="p-6">
            <h2 className="sf-display mb-3 text-xl font-semibold">Public policy</h2>
            <Resource {...policy} retry={policy.reload}>
              {policy.data ? <a href={policy.data.url} className="sf-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">{policy.data.title} ↗</a> : <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">This company has not published a public policy.</p>}
            </Resource>
          </SurfaceCard>
          <SurfaceCard className="p-6"><ClientFlowAI key={id} companyId={id} /></SurfaceCard>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </>}
